Catalog number : M00088
Uniform title: tattvacintāmaṇi chapters 16 thru end
Author : pūrṇānanda
Commentator : bhuvanmohan ṣaṃkhyatirtha
Description:
Critically edited from original manuscripts with original commentary by bhuvanmohan saṃkhyatirtha and with notes by Chintamani Bhattacharya
Notes:
Transcribed by the staff of Muktabodha under the supervision of Mark S. G. Dyczkowski.

Revision 1: December 11, 2007

Publication year : 1936
Publication city : Calcutta
Publication country : India

ṣoḍaśaḥ prakāśaḥ

[antaryāganirūpaṇapratijñā]

athāntaryajanaṃ vakṣye caturvargaphalapradam || 1 ||

[nirguṇasyāpi paramātmanaḥ sṛṣṭipravṛttiḥ]

ekamāsīt paraṃ brahma sūkṣmaṃ nityamatīndriyam |
jalīyabhāge cātīte sisṛkṣā samapadyata || 2 ||


1 | evaṃ pañcadaśabhiḥ prakāśairnyāsaprakriyāntamabhidhāya avasarasaṅgatyā pūjākrame 
vaktavye -

ātmasthāṃ devatāṃ tyaktvā bahirdevaṃ vicinvate |
karasthaṃ kaustubhaṃ tyaktvā bhramate kācatṛṣṇayā ||

pratyakṣīkṛtya hṛdaye bahisthāṃ pūjayecchivām |
yasya yasya ca devasya yathā bhūṣaṇavāhanam ||

tadeva pūjane tasya cintayet parameśvari |

ityādi-śāktānandataraṅgiṇīṣaṣṭhollāsavacanena,

antaryāgavidhiṃ kṛtvā bahiryāgaṃ samācaret |
bahiryāge nādhikārī antaryāgavivarjitaḥ ||

bahiryāgaphalaṃ nāsti vinā'ntaryajanaṃ kadā |
tasmāt prayatnato vīraścāntaryāgaṃ samārabhet ||

iti kaulāvalīnirṇayatṛtīyollāsavacanena ca bahiryāgasyāntaryāgapūrvakatvaśravaṇāt 
prakāśe'sminnantaryāgakramamabhidhatte - athetyādinā | atheti 
pūjāṅganyāsanirūpaṇānantaramityarthaḥ |

2 | evamantaryāgakramanirūpaṇaṃ pratijñāya -

kāraṇe sarvabhūtānāṃ tattvānyapi ca cintayet |
bījabhāvena līnāni vyutkramāt paramātmani ||

p. 543) [samāsataḥ sṛṣṭiprakriyā]

saṃhārasyāntaraṃ sṛṣṭaṃ budbudākāramuttamam |
tadantarbhuvanāni syustadaṇḍaṃ dvividhaṃ bhavet || 3 ||


evaṃ vicintya deveśi pārthivāṃśaṃ jale gatam |
jalañca tejaso rūpaṃ tejorūpaṃ vicintayet ||

ityādi-gandharvatantraikādaśapaṭaloktadiśā tattvāntarāṇāṃ krameṇa kāraṇabhūtāyāṃ prakṛtau 
prakṛteśca paramātmani laye tanmātrasvarūpanirbhāsalakṣaṇāmavasthāmabhidhatte - 
ekamāsīdityādinā | tathāca prāguktalayakramamabhidhāya

mahāntaṃ prakṛtau līnaṃ cintayet parameśvari |
mumukṣuścintayellīnāṃ prakṛtiṃ paramātmani ||

ityantena prakṛtyantānāṃ paramātmani layamuktvā

nāho na rātrirna sandhyā na sūryo naiva candramāḥ |
idaṃ tamomayaṃ sarvamāsīd bhuvanavarjitam |
aprajñātamalakṣyañca prasuptamiva sarvataḥ |
cintayitvā maheśāni na kiñcidapi bhāvayet ||
ekamāsīt paraṃ brahma nityaṃ sūkṣmamatīndriyam |
nityānandamayaṃ dhāma tejorūpaṃ sanātanam | 

ityādinā tātkālikī paramātmanirbhāsamātrātmikā'vasthā tatraivābhihitā | bhagavatā 
manunā'pi -

āsīdidaṃ tamobhūtamaprajñātamalakṣaṇam |
apratarkyamavijñeyaṃ prasuptamiva sarvataḥ ||

ityādinā mahāpralayāvasthā'nayaiva diśopavarṇitā | sisṛkṣāyāṃ hetumāha- jalīyabhāga iti | 
jalamantareṇa sṛṣṭeḥ sṛṣṭānāṃ jīvānāṃ sthiteścāsasambhavādityarthaḥ | sisṛkṣeyam āpo 
jāyantāṃ kṣitirjāyatāmityādyabhidhānamātreṇa, tadaikṣata bahu syāṃ prajāyeye ti 
chāndogyaśruteḥ |

3-4 | atha sṛṣṭiparipāṭīṃ darśayati - saṃhārasyetyādinā | etena prathamameva jalasṛṣṭirityuktaṃ 
bhavati | gandharvatantrīyaikādaśapaṭale'pi -

p. 544) brahmāṇḍābhyantare toyaṃ tatraiva bhekamavyayam |

[niruktabhekapṛṣṭhe kālādyarcā]

yajet pṛṣṭhe tasya mantrī kālāgnirudrarūpakam || 4 ||

[ādhāraśaktyādīnāṃ svarūpaṃ pūjanopadeśaśca]

ādhāraśaktiṃ tatraiva mūlaprakṛtirūpiṇīm | 
śaraccandranibhāṃ devīṃ paṅkajadvayadhāriṇīm || 5 ||

kūrmākāraśilārūḍhāṃ kūrmañca mūrdhni dhāriṇīm |
nīlābhaṃ pūjayet kūrmamanantaṃ kundasannibham || 6 ||

ūrdhvaṃ brahma samāsīnaṃ pūjayet sādhakottamaḥ || 7 ||


tayoryogādabhūd vṛṣṭiḥ paramāmṛtarūpiṇī |
paripūrṇamidaṃ devi samastaṃ plāvayettu yā ||

tatrāmbhasi svamātmānaṃ cintayet sādhakottamaḥ |
budbadaṃ cintayettatra prakṛtiṃ cintayet parām ||

ityādinā'yamevārtho'bhihitaḥ | tadantariti | vyaktamāha gandharvatantra ekādaśapaṭale -

prakṛterhi mahāntaṃ vai ahañca mahatastathā |
ahaṅkārānmanaścaiva manasaḥ khaṃ samutthitam ||

ākāśād vāyumākṛṣya vāyostejaḥ samutthitam |
tejaso jalamāsādya jalācca pṛthivīṃ smaret ||

iti | dvividhamiti bhāgadvayena vibhaktamityarthaḥ, vibhāgaśca praṇavena karttavyaḥ | taduktaṃ 
tatraiva -

praṇavena dvidhā kṛtvā jalaughaiḥ paripūrayet | iti |

p. 545) tasya mūrdhni sthitaṃ devaṃ śvetaṃ vārāharūpakam | 
tamālaśyāmalāṃ dante dhārayantaṃ vasundharām || 8 ||

nīlendīvarahastāñca hārakeyūrabhūṣitām |
abhyarcayed vasumatīṃ sphuratsāgaramekhalām || 9 ||

raktānnamamṛtaṃ divyaṃ śuddhalākṣārasopamam || 10 ||

hastābhyāṃ pūjayedapsu dvīpaṃ vidrumasannibham |
navaratnasamākīrṇamaṣṭasiddhisamanvitam || 11 ||

[pīṭhacatuṣṭayārcā]

śaktinābhikayormadhye uḍḍīyānaṃ prapūjayet |
jālandharaṃ tathā vaktre gude ca kāmarūpakam || 12 ||

pūrṇagiriṃ hṛdimadhye śrīhaṭṭaṃ tālukopari |
pūrṇagirau svarṇagiripīṭhaṃ saṃpūjayennaraḥ || 13 ||

[kalpodyānārcā]

kalpodyānaṃ yajettasya parito gandhapuṣpakaiḥ |

[kalpavṛkṣasya svarūpamarcanopadeśaśca]

madhye kalpataruṃ mantrī sarvāśā paripūrakam || 14 ||

sarvabhūtāśrayaṃ vṛkṣaṃ nityaṃ varṇamayaṃ param |
pañcaiva mādanaṃ bījaṃ bindunādamahāśipham || 15 ||


12 | śaktinābhikayormadhya iti | anāhatamaṇipūrayormadhya ityarthaḥ |
14 | sarvāśāparipūrakamiti | śākhāpraśākhābhiḥ sarvadikparivyāpakamityarthaḥ |

p. 546) pṛthivyakṣaraśākhābhiḥ sarvāśāsu vijṛmbhitam |
salilākṣarapatraiḥ svaiḥ saṃchāditajagattrayam || 16 ||

vahnivarṇāṅkurairdīptaṃ prabālāṅkurasannibhaiḥ |
marudvarṇalasatpuṣpairdyotayantaṃ vapuḥśriyam || 17 ||

ākāśārṇaphalairnamraṃ sarvabhūtāśrayaṃ param |
parāmṛtākhyamadhubhiḥ siñcantaṃ parameśvarīm || 18 ||

vedāgamādibhiḥ kḷptaṃ samunnatamanoharam |
śivaśaktimayaṃ sākṣādāśritañca jagattrayam || 19 ||

[parijātādicintā]

tanmadhye bhāvayenmantrī pārijātaṃ manoharam |
kadambanāgapunnāgacampakādīn vibhāvayet || 20 ||

kalpamūle ratnabhūmiṃ bālukāṃ kāñcanaprabhām |

[cintāmaṇigṛhādeḥ svarūpaṃ taccintanopadeśaśca]

cintāmaṇigṛhaṃ madhye jyotīrūpamanuttamam || 21 ||

udyadādityasaṅkāśaṃ vyāptabrahmāṇḍamaṇḍalam |
śatayojanavistīrṇaṃ nānāratnopaśobhitam || 22 ||

caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ hemaprākāra śobhitam |
ratnasaṃkḷptasaṃśobhi kavāṭāṣṭakaśobhitam || 23 ||

navaratnena saṃkḷptaṃ tuṅgatoraṇaśobhitam |
hemadaṇḍaśikhālambidhvajāvalipariṣkṛtam || 24 ||

p. 547) madhyakoṇasthitastambha - navaratnasamanvitam |
mahāmāṇikyavaidūryaratnacāmarabhūṣitam || 25 ||

muktādāmavitānāḍhyaṃ svarṇastambhairvirājitam |
mandavāyusamāyuktaṃ gandhapuṣpairalaṅkṛtam || 26 ||

bahuratnaśaṅkhaghaṇṭā - nānādravyavibhūṣitam |
madhumattabhramattuṅgabhramaradhvaniśobhitam || 27 ||

gṛhaprākārasaṃlagnaphalakā(śi?li)vimaṇḍitam |
indrādibhirdevagaṇairvinamritadigantaram || 28 ||

suvarṇaratnaracitaṃ chatrantu maṇḍalopari |
paramojjvalarūpantu tanmadhye ratnamaṇḍalam || 29 ||

nandanodyānamadhyasthaṃ

[ratnavedikādeḥ svarūpaṃ taccintanopadeśaśca]

vedikāṃ ratnanirmitām |
tatrasthāṃ cintayedraktāṃ caturasropaśobhitām || 30 ||

nānāratnasamāyuktaṃ siṃhāsanaṃ tadantare |
tasya madhye mahācakraṃ pīyūṣapūrapūritam || 31 ||

tanmadhye sphuritaṃ dhyāyettiśṛṅgaṃ jyotirātmakam |

[rakṣaṇādiparipāṭī]

astreṇa rakṣaṇaṃ kuryādrakṣecca dhenumudrayā || 32 ||

[yonimudrā]

yonimudrāṃ tato vakṣye bāhyamabhyantaraṃ tathā |
cintayed gudamākuñcya brahmasūtraṃ tathā'param || 33 ||

p. 548) jvaladdīpaśikhākārāṃ(?) saṃkṣobhyāmṛtamaṇḍalam |
sudhādhārāmṛte bhūya āyāti mūlapaṅkaje || 34 ||

evañca saptadhā kuryāddevīsannidhihetave |

[dhyānakramaḥ]

mūlena dhyānayogena smṛtvā brahmamayīṃ parām || 35 ||

pūrakeṇa samāpūrya saṃgopya kumbhakena ca |
sundarīṃ tāṃ samārādhya yoginīgaṇaveṣṭitām || 36 ||

sravadamṛtasaṃyuktāṃ devyaṅgakamalāntare |
brahmaviṣṇubhavairvandyāṃ mahātripurasundarīm || 37 ||

[saśaktikadvārapāladhyānopadeśaḥ]

tato dhyāyed dvārapālān pratidvāre saśaktikān || 38 ||

[sahasrāre devīsaṃsthāpanam]

sahasradalamadhye tu sthāpayet sundarīṃ parām |
bhairavānandaliṅgantu tanmadhye yonisaṃsthitam || 39 ||

[tayorekatvacintā]

ekībhāvaṃ tayoḥ kṛtvā śrāvayedamṛtaṃ budhaḥ |
jihvānanasamāyogāt pibettadamṛtaṃ sadā || 40 ||

[devīpūjā, mānasayāga āvāhananiṣedhaśca]

hṛdaye tu samānīya sundarīṃ pūjayettataḥ |
atraivā''vāhanaṃ nāsti hṛdaye mānasārcane || 41 ||


34 | mūleti | mūlādhārasthapaṅkaja ityarthaḥ |

40 | ekībhāvamiti | tayoḥ sundarībhairavānandaliṅgayoḥ | amṛtaṃ tayoḥ 
sāmarasyasambhūtaparamāmṛtamityarthaḥ |

p. 649) ubhābhyāmamṛtānandaiḥ pūjayedrahasaṃkramāt |

[pādyādyupacārapātrasaṃkhyānirūpaṇam]

pādyapātratrayaṃ kuryādarghyapātratrayaṃ tathā || 42 ||

ṣaḍācamanapātrāṇi madhupātratrayaṃ tataḥ |
ekaṃ subhogapātrantu kārayet ṣoḍaśe dale || 43 ||

[mānasapūjākramaḥ]

sahasradalabhṛṅgāralambikānāla vicyutām |
parāmṛtañca pānīyaṃ bhāvapuṣpaṃ suvāsitam || 44 ||

yathāvidhi kare kṛtvā svarṇapātre prapūrayet |
mānasairupacāraistu dīyate dravyamuttamam || 45 ||

kalpayāmīti manasā upacārān prakalpayet || 46 ||

[tatra pādyādyarpaṇaniyamaḥ]

pādyaṃ pādāmbuje maulāvarghyamācamanaṃ mukhe |
madhuparkaṃ tridhā vaktraṃ madhu pānīyamutsṛjet || 47 ||

punarācamanaṃ dattvā gandhapuṣpādikaṃ budhaḥ || 48 ||

[upacāradānamantre viśeṣaḥ]

hṛdvahnivallabhātoyamantraṃ pādyādiṣu kramāt |
namo vauṣaḍ gandhapuṣpe hṛdantānyān nivedayet || 49 ||


44 | bhāveti | bhāvapuṣpāṇyagre vakṣyante |

49 | athopacāradānamantre viśeṣamāha - hṛditi | hṛt namaḥ  vahnivallabhā svāhā | 
toyamantraṃ svadhā | evañca pādyaṃ namo'ntena arghyaṃ svāhāntena, ācamanīyaṃ 
svadhāntena, gandhaṃ namo'ntena, puṣpaṃ vauṣaḍantena, upacārāntarāṇi tu namo'ntena 
mantreṇa dadyāditi phalitārthaḥ |

p. 550) [atrā'gamāntarasammataḥ kramabhedaḥ]

kuloḍḍīśamatenāpi prakārāntaramucyate || 50 ||

dhūpe dīpe svadhā svāhā naivedyācamane svadhā |
namaḥ sarvopacāreṣu prathamānteṣu yojayet || 51 ||

[atraiva kalpasūtrakṛtāṃ matam]

upacāreṣu sarveṣu kalpasūtramate punaḥ |
kalpayāmi nama iti yojanīyamanurmataḥ || 52 ||

[svayambhūkusumādayo viśiṣṭāḥ pūjopacārāḥ]

svayambhūkusumaiḥ kuṇḍagolotthairapi sādhakaḥ |
bhāvapuṣpaiḥ prayatnena pūjayet parameśvarīm || 53 ||

[tatra pañcadaśa bhāvapuṣpāṇi]

amāyamanahaṅkāramarāgamamadantathā |
amohakamadambhañca adveṣaṃ kṣobhavarjitam || 54 ||

amātsaryamalobhañca daśapuṣpaṃ vidurbudhāḥ |
ahiṃsā paramaṃ puṣpaṃ puṣpamindriyanigrahaḥ || 55 ||

dayāpuṣpaṃ kṣamāpuṣpaṃ jñānapuṣpañca pañcamam |
iti pañcadaśoktāni bhāvapuṣpāṇi pūjane || 56 ||

[anye upacārāḥ]

sudhāmbudhiṃ māṃsaśailaṃ matsyārāśiṃ nivedayet |

[ṣaḍaṅgādipūjā]

ṣaḍaṅgānyatha nityāśca guruṇā ratimarcayet || 57 ||

p. 551) [snapanāṅgamudvartanam]

svavāme maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā tatra nītvā sureśvarīm |
udvartanaṃ yathānyāyaṃ vidhāya sarpiṣā budhaḥ || 58 ||

[devyāḥ snapanam]

sugandhidivyatailena devakanyāsahasrakaiḥ |
sahasraiḥ svarṇakumbhaistu sugandhijalapūritaḥ || 59 ||

siñcayed bhaktibhāvena bhavānīṃ bhaktavatsalām |
nānātīrthodakaiḥ śuddhairgajarājasahasrakaiḥ || 60 ||

nāgakanyāsahasraistu kinnarairapsarogaṇaiḥ |
pañcāmṛtaiḥ pañcagavyairdugdhairdadhnā gavāmapi || 61 ||

nārikelodakairikṣujalaiśca śiśirodakaiḥ |
saptamṛttikayā yuktatoyena śītalena ca || 62 ||

śītaloṣṇodakaiḥ śuddhaistathā ratnānvitodakaiḥ |
jalaiḥ sarvauṣadhīnāñca suvāsitajalairapi || 63 ||

sahasraśīrṣamantreṇa pāvamānyā samāhitaḥ |
bhṛṅgārāṣṭakakumbhaiśca devyāḥ snānaṃ samācaret || 64 ||


63 | jalaiḥ sarvauṣadhīnāmiti | sarvauṣadhigaṇamāha dīpikāyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāye-

surā māṃsī vacā kuṣṭhī śaileyaṃ rajanīdvayam |
śaṭhī campaka-mustañca sarvauṣadhigaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |

64 | sahasretyādi | sahasraśīrṣā puruṣa ityādipuruṣasūktamantreṇetyarthaḥ | pāvamānyeti | 
pāvamānīḥ svastayanī sududhā hi ghṛtacyuta ityādi - pāvamānī-sūkte - netyarthaḥ |

p. 552) [devīśarīrālaṅkaraṇakramaḥ]

śarīraṃ tejasāmṛjya dukūle paridhāpayet |
kaṅkatyā keśasaṃskāraṃ vidhivad bandhanaṃ tathā || 65 ||

lalāṭe tilakaṃ dadyāt sindūraṃ keśamadhyataḥ |
raktavastraṃ tathā mālyaṃ raktālaṅkaraṇaṃ tathā || 66 ||

raktānulepanaṃ kāryaṃ gandhacandana-sihlakaiḥ |
sarvālaṅkaraṇaṃ dadyād yatra yena virājate || 67 ||


65 | vidhivad bandhanamiti niruktaparipāṭyā saṃskṛtānāṃ keśānāmityarthaḥ |

67 | sihlakairiti svārthekaḥ | sihlako gandhadravyaviśeṣaḥ | turaṣkaḥ piṇḍakaḥ sihlo 
yāvano'pītyamaraḥ | sarvālaṅkaraṇamiti | vyaktamāha kaulāvalīnirṇaye tṛtīyollāse -

nāgendrahāraracitaṃ śaṅkhaṃ dadyānmanoharam |
haste keyūrakañcaiva kaṅkaṇaṃ kaṭakaṃ tathā |
pādāṅgurīyakaṃ dadyānnānāratnopaśobhitam |
pādayornūpuraṃ dadyāt kaṭyāñca kṣudraghaṇṭikām |
śiroratnaṃ pradadyācca ratnanirmitam |
tāḍaṅkakuṇḍalaṃ karṇabhūṣaṇaṃ supariṣkṛtam |
nayane kajjalaṃ dadyānnāsāgre gajamauktikam |
grīvāpatraṃ kaṇṭhabhūṣāṃ muktāmaṇivibhūṣitam |
ānandahāratṛtayamaṅgurīyakaratnakam |

ityādinā | śāktānandataraṅgiṇī - gandharvatantrādiṣvapi alaṅkārabhedāḥ pradarśitāḥ | yatra 
yena virājata iti yeṣu yeṣvaṅgeṣu yad yadalaṅkaraṇaṃ śobhāviśeṣamāvahati tattadalaṅkaraṇaṃ 
tatra tatra deyamityarthaḥ |

p. 553) [śrīpādukāntopacāradānānantaraṃ namaskāropadeśaḥ]

ratnaśrīpāduke dattvā pravāladaṇḍacihnite |
yantramadhye samānīya namaskuryāt samāhitaḥ || 68 ||

[namaskāramantraḥ]

samāyātāmahaṃ vande śrīmattripurasundarīm |
sarvasaubhāgyajananīṃ sarvadevasvarūpiṇīm || 69 ||

[punaḥ pūjanopadeśastatkramaśca]

punaḥ pādyādikaṃ dattvā pūjayet parameśvarīm |
nāsārandhre tu tattvena gandhaṃ devyai nivedayet || 70 ||

bhāvapuṣpaistu saṃpūjya dhūpaṃ dadyāt samīraṇam |
paraṃ tejaḥ pradīpañca naivedyāni nivedayet || 71 ||

pṛthvītattvāttathā vāyostattvena cāmaraṃ śubham |
ravimaṇḍalamādarśaṃ chatraṃ pūrṇaniśākaram || 72 ||

padmaṃ samekhalāṃ mālāmānandahāramujjvalam |
puṣpaṃ nānāvidhaṃ divyaṃ saptadvīpasamudbhavam || 73 ||


70-72 | atha yantrārūḍhāyā devyāḥ punaḥ pūjanamupadiśati - punarityādinā | 
pādyādikamityādipadenārghyācamanīye bodhye | gandhādyupacārānāha - nāsetyādinā | 
tattvena caturviṃśatitattvena | caturviṃśatitattvena gandhaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ iti 
kaulāvalītṛtīyollāsavacanasaṃvādāt | bhāvapuṣpairiti | aṣṭādaśa bhāvapuṣpāṇi 
amāyamanahaṅkāramityādinā prāgasminneva prakāśe pradarśitāni | naivedyānīti | 
pṛthvītattvānnaivedyāni nivedayediti sambandhaḥ |

73 | puṣpaṃ nānāvidhamiti śaktipūjāyāṃ vihitamiti bodhyam | viśiṣyā'ha kaulāvalīnirṇaye 
tṛtīyollāse -

karavīraṃ javā droṇaṃ campakaṃ nāgakeśaram |

p. 554) ā mūlād brahmarandhrāntaṃ suṣumṇāmṛtavāyunā |
anāhatadhvanimayīṃ ghaṇṭāmetāṃ nivedayet || 74 ||

pradīpairvandanaṃ kṛtvā dattvā ca pratibodhayet || 75 ||

[darśayitavyāsu mudrāsu sundarīmudrā]

ādhārādutthitaṃ vāyuṃ ṣoḍaśānte suṣumṇayā |
nayane nāsikāgrasthe nimeṣonmeṣavarjite || 76 ||

bhruvormadhye samānīya dantairdantānasaṃspṛśan |
noṣṭhairnoṣṭhaṃ na vā tāluṃ jihvayā jñānakhaḍgakaḥ || 77 ||

śaktau lakṣaṃ bahiḥ kuryānnimeṣonmeṣavarjitam |
eṣā sā sundarī mudrā bhairavīmodakāriṇī || 78 ||

[vismayamudrā]

dakṣiṇā militā muṣṭirnāsikārpitatarjanī |
mudrā vismayasaṃjñā syād vismayāveśakāriṇī || 79 ||


aparāñca kadambañca pārijātādikantathā |
dadyāddevyai prayatnena mālyaṃ gandhasamanvitam || iti |

74 | atha ghaṇṭādānaparipāṭīmāha - ā mūlādityādinā | mūlādhārād 
brahmarandhraparyantavyāpinī suṣumṇākhyā nāḍī varttate | taduktaṃ gāndharve pañcamapaṭale -

merumadhye sthitā yā tu mūlādābrahmarandhragā |
sarvatejomayī sā tu suṣumṇā vahnirūpiṇī || iti |

anayaiva suṣumṇayā mūlādhārād brahmarandhraparyantaṃ vāyuḥ sañcarati | tasyaiva 
vāyorāghātena anāhatākhye śabdabrahmamaye hṛtpuṇḍarīke kaścana śabdo'virāmaḥ pravarttate 
| sa eva śabdo'trānāhatadhvanipadenābhidhīyate | tameva dhvaniṃ ghaṇṭāvādyattvena nivedayet 
iti phalitārthaḥ |

79 | militā muṣṭirityatra niviḍā muṣṭiriti tantrasāre pāṭhaḥ |

p. 555) [nādamudrā]

muṣṭiruddhīkṛtāṅguṣṭhā dakṣiṇā nādamudrikā |

[bindumudrā]

tarjanyaṅguṣṭhasaṃyogād bindvākhyā saiva kīrtitā || 80 ||

[jñānamudrā]

jñānamudrā yadaiva syānnā''bhimukhyena saṃsthitā |

[yogamudrā]

hṛtpradeśe tu sambaddhā yogamudreti kathyate || 81 ||

[bahirantaryonimudre]

yonimudrāṃ tataḥ kuryāt saiva cāntarbhavedapi |
tanmāṃsaśoṇitasvādapramattā muktihetave || 82 ||

eṣā sā paramā muktiḥ śṛṅgārarasabhāvitā |
tasyāḥ svecchā svayaṃ bhūtaṃ paramānandabhairavam || 83 ||

kande sthitā vāyuśaktiḥ suṣumṇādvāragāminī |
nīvāraśūkavattanvī viṣatantutanīyasī || 84 ||

vidyutkoṭipratīkāśā sūryakoṭisamaprabhā |
aśeṣajagadutpattisthitisaṃhārakāriṇī || 85 ||


80 | muṣṭīti | muṣṭyā ruddhīkṛto'ṅguṣṭho yasyāstathābhūtā dakṣiṇā nādamudretyarthaḥ | 
atra dakṣiṇakarasyāṅguṣṭhamitareṇāṅgulicatuṣṭayena ruddhaṃ kuryāditi phalitārthaḥ |

81 | prāguktā tarjanyaṅguṣṭhasaṃyogātmikā bindumudraiva ābhimukhyena na saṃsthitā cet tadā 
jñānamudreti kathyate ityarthaḥ | tathāca kaulāvalīnirṇaye saptamollāse -

jñānākhyamudrayā caiva pūjayet parameśvarīm | 
aṅguṣṭhatarjanībhyāntu jñānamudreyamīritā || iti |

hṛditi | saiva bindumudrā hṛdi saṃyojitā yogamudretyucyata ityarthaḥ |

82 | yonīti | bahirantarbhedena yonimudrā dvividhā bhavati | tatra bahiryonimudrā prasiddhā | 
antaryonimudrāmāha saivetyādinā |

p. 556) hṛdgranthitālukāgranthikapāṭāsphoṭanākulam |
bindulambikayormadhye trikoṇe śāmbhave nayet || 86 ||

praviśya ramate gāḍhaṃ puruṣeṇa sahāniśam |
sthiracittaḥ sadā dhyāyed yathā syānmānasaṃ sthiram || 87 ||

[svayaṃprajñātāsaṃprajñātabhedena samādherdvaividhyam]

samādhirjāyate tatra saṃjñādvayavirājitaḥ |
svayaṃprajñātanāmaiko hyasaṃprajñātanāmadhṛk || 88 ||

[tatra svayaṃprajñātaḥ]

svayaṃprajñātasaṃjñastu śivādhikyena jāyate |

[asaṃprajñātaḥ]

asaṃprajñātanāmā tu śivatattvena vai bhavet || 86 ||

[svayaṃprajñātabhedau]

svayaṃprajñātabhedastu tīvrastīvrataro bhavet |

[asaṃprajñātabhedau]

asaṃprajñātabhedastu mando mandatarastathā || 90 ||

[tatra prathamasya bhedayoḥ svarūpam]

hāsyarodanaromāñcakampasvedādilakṣaṇaḥ |
tīvrastīvrataraścaiva samādhirupalakṣitaḥ || 91 ||

[dvitīyasya bhedayoḥ svarūpam]

nimeṣavarṇi(rji)te netre vapustallakṣaṇaṃ sthitam |
mando mandataraścaiva samādhirupalakṣitaḥ || 92 ||


88 | samādhiriti | saṃprajñātāsaṃprajñātabhedena samādherdvaividhyaṃ dvividhasya ca tasya 
tīvratīvrataratvādibhedena te te bhedāḥ pātañjalasūtravyāsabhāṣye viśiṣya pradarśitāḥ |

p. 557) śāmbhavena tu yogena sukhī bhūyānnirantaram |
tayoḥ saṃyogamāhūya paramāmṛtagokulam || 93 ||

pānīyaṃ manasā dattvā punarjanma na vidyate |
tadānandamayaṃ jñānaṃ prītā bhavati sundarī || 94 ||

[viśrāmārtha kusumaśayane devyāḥ sthāpanaṃ prītisaṃvādanañca]

puṣpaśayyāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā tatra nītvā sureśvarīm |
manonartakatālaiśca śṛṅgārādirasodbhavaiḥ || 95 ||

nṛtyairgītaiśca vādyaiśca toṣayet parameśvarīm |
tasmāt kāmeśvarī mātā pi(prī)tā kāmeśvaraśca saḥ || 96 ||

[mānasayāgaphalam]

manasaikāṃ mahāpūjāmantarārcanamuttamam |
kṛtvā siddhimavāpnoti na sa bandhena badhyate || 97 ||

[jñānahomanirūpaṇopakramaḥ]

jñānahomaṃ pravakṣyāmi yena brahmamayo bhavet |

[tatra caturasrakuṇḍam]

jñānena caturasrākhyaṃ kuṇḍaṃ sañcintya mantravit || 98 ||


98 | evamantarārcanamabhidhāya tadaṅgaṃ jñānahomaṃ vaktumupakramate - 
jñānahomamityādinā | jñānahomo mānasahoma eva | jñānamātreṇaiva 
vakṣyamāṇahomaprakriyāsampādanādasya jñānahomatvam | asya phalamāha- yenetyādinā | 
jñāneneti | jñānena kevalaṃ manovyāpāreṇa cintanamātreṇetyarthaḥ | caturasrākhyamiti | 
kuṇḍamidaṃ saptamaprakāśoktacaturasrakuṇḍavat kalpanīyam | etadabhiprāyeṇaiva jñānārṇave 
dvāviṃśapaṭale -

jñānena caturasrākhyaṃ kuṇḍaṃ sañcintya pūrvavat |

ityuktam |

p. 558) [ātmanāṃ cāturvidhyam]

ātmāno vedasaṃkhyā hi jñātavyāḥ sādhakottamaiḥ |

[niruktātmajñānaphalam]

eṣāṃ vijñānamātreṇa punarjanma na vidyate || 99 ||

[ātmanaḥ svarūpam]

sādhāraṇena pakṣeṇa varttate sarvajantuṣu |
sa ātmā prāṇarūpī tu hṛdaye paramātmakaḥ || 100 ||

[ātmanaścarācaraśarīravyāpakatvam]

śarīraṃ vyāpya sakalaṃ carasyāpyacarasya ca |
haṃsasvarūpī vijñeyaḥ śvāsocchrāsavilāsavān || 101 ||

[bhramavaśādasyaiva jīvasaṃjñā]

jīvastasyaiva nāma syād bhrāntyā śvāsopalakṣitaḥ |
śvāsocchvāsau dehabhāvau bhrāntireva na saṃśayaḥ || 102 ||


99 | ātmāna iti | vedasaṃkhyā ātmāntarātmaparamātmajñānātmabhedena catuḥsaṃkhyākā 
ityarthaḥ | itaḥ prabhṛti prakāśāntaṃ yāvaj jñānārṇavīyadvāviṃśapaṭalena prāyaḥ sarvameva 
saṃvadati | tatrā'tmāna ityādipādadvayāt pūrvam -

ātmabhiścaturasraṃ tad vijñeyaṃ vīravandite |

iti pādadvayaṃ dṛśyate | atrā'tmabhirityādipādadvayaṃ lekhakapramādāt patitamiti pratibhāti | 
anyathātmacatuṣṭayanirūpaṇamasaṅgatimāpadyeta | evañca 
niruktajñānārṇavavacanaparyālocanayā niruktātmacatuṣkeṇa caturasrakuṇḍaṃ vidheyamityāpatati, 
vakṣyati ca svayameva -

ātmāntarātmaparamajñānātmānaḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
etadrūpaṃ hi citkuṇḍaṃ caturasraṃ vibhāvayet || iti |

100-102. ātmanaścāturvidhyaṃ nirūpya tasya sarvamūrtasaṃyogitvādilakṣaṇaṃ svarūpaṃ 
nirūpayati- sādhāraṇenetyādinā bhrāntireva na saṃśaya ityantena | anena jīvātma -

p. 559) kulālacakrabhedena ghaṭākāśaṃ yathā ghaṭe |
ghaṭe bhavati bhinnatvaṃ nā'kāśe bhinnatā bhavet || 103 ||

tathā dehagato jīvo dehanāśe jagatsvayam |
vyāpya tiṣṭhati sarvatra tasmādātmapadaṃ mahat || 104 ||


paramātmanorabhedaḥ pratipāditaḥ | sa sarvaprāṇarūpī carācarākhilaśarīravyāpakaḥ 
haṃsasvarūpī prakṛtipuruṣobhayātmakaśca | tatra śvāsocchvāsādyakhilavilāsavān pratidehaṃ 
vyavasthita ātmaiva bhrānterjīvātmatvena vyapadiśyate | paramārthatastu paramātmetarasya kasyacid 
jīvātmanaḥ sattā nāsti | śvāsapraśvāsādīnāṃ śarīradharmapratītistu bhramavaśādeva | ata 
eva gandharvatantre pañcamapaṭale -

nityaḥ sarvagato hyātmā kūṭastho doṣavarjitaḥ |
ekaḥ saṃbhidyate bhrāntyā māyayā na svarūpataḥ |
tasmād dvaitaṃ na me cāsti na prapañco na saṃsṛtiḥ ||

ityādinā nityatvaikatvavyāpakatvādikamātmanaḥ pratipāditam |

103-104 | nanu tattaddehāvacchedenā'tmano bhedasyānubhavasiddhatvāt kathaṃ tasyaikatvādikaṃ 
sidhyatītyāśaṅkāṃ dṛṣṭāntapradarśanamukhena pariharati- kulāletyādinā | yathā 
ākāśasyaikatve'pi kulālacakrabhedena samutpanneṣu ghaṭeṣu tattadghaṭāvacchinnamākāśaṃ 
bhinnatayā pratibhāti vastutastu ghaṭa eva bhinnatvaṃ bhedo bhavati ākāśe tu bhinnatā na 
bhavet; ākāśamekameva, upādhibhedādeva tasya bhedavyavahāraḥ | ata eva viśvanāthena ekaḥ 
sannapyupādhita ityuktam | evaṃ dṛṣṭāntamupanyasya dārṣṭāntikaṃ pradarśayati - tathetyādinā | 
prāgupadarśitaghaṭādyavacchinnākāśavad dehagato dehāvacchinnaḥ paramātmā jīva ityucyate 
parantu dehanāśe'nupahito'yaṃ svayaṃ jagad vyāpya sarvatra tiṣṭhati, sarvatra jagatsu ayaṃ 
vyāpya sarvaṃ vastujātamiti śeṣaḥ, iti vā sambandhaḥ | yathā tattadghaṭanāśe 
tattadghaṭāvacchinnākāśo mahākāśa eva tathā tattaddehanāśe tattaddehāvacchinna ātmā 
paramātmaiva, na tu jīvatvenā'tmanaḥ kācit svatantrā sattā'stīti phalitārthaḥ | 
etadabhiprāyeṇaiva gandharvatantre pañcamapaṭale -

yathākāśo ghaṭākāśo mahākāśa itīritaḥ |
tathā bhrāntirdvidhā proktā hyātmā jīveśvarātmanā || iti |

p. 560) ātmajñānaṃ śivenoktaṃ śṛṇuṣva ca || 105 ||

[antarātmasvarūpam]

antaḥśabdena sakalaṃ rahasyaṃ sūkṣmarūpakam |
paramātmasvarūpeṇa sarvaṃ vyāpya vijṛmbhate || 106 ||

antaraṅgatayā tena caraṃ vā'pyathavā'caram |
vyāpyate sakalaṃ yasmādantarātmeti gīyate || 107 ||

[paramātmanaḥ svarūpam]

sa eva haṃsa ātmā hi yogigamyo yadā bhavet |
tāvaccañcurbhaveccaiva nigamāgamapakṣavān || 108 ||

śivaśaktipadadvandvo bindutrayavilocanaḥ |
yaśca paramahaṃsastu yasmāt sarvatra caiva hi || 109 ||

tadā bhūtāni jāyante krameṇaiva ca tacchṛṇu |
ākāśaḥ pavano hyagnirjalaṃ pṛthvī krameṇa tu || 110 ||

eteṣāṃ pañcabhūtānāṃ prathamaṃ bījamucyate |
avidyākhyasaromadhye mohapaṅkajapūrite || 111 ||

prapañcakamale bhūtapadmākṣaparijṛmbhite |
vihāro'syaiva mohasya dṛṣṭvā māyāmayo bhavet || 112 ||

ayameva mahākāle niṣprapañco yadā bhavet |
saṃhārarūpī haṃsastu tadātmānaṃ pradarśayet || 113 ||


108 | paramātmasvarūpamāha - sa evetyādinā | tāvadityādinā rūpaparigrahaḥ sūcitaḥ |

p. 561) pakṣitvamasya nāstyeva so'hamātmā pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
paramātmeti jānīhi evametadudāhṛtam || 114 ||

[jñānātmanaḥ svarūpam]

yenedaṃ jñāyate sarvaṃ samatvena ca sarvadā || 115 ||

candraḥ sūryo yathā nityaṃ nīrakṣīreṣu dṛśyate |
tadvat kṣīrasya samatā nīrakṣīravibhedataḥ || 116 ||

kardame candane bhinnaṃ na bhavedantyaje dvije |
sākṣī tu sarvabhūtānāṃ jñānātmā parikīrtitaḥ || 117 ||

[ātmacatuṣṭayanirūpaṇopasaṃhāraḥ]

ātmāntarātmaparamajñānātmānaḥ prakīrtitāḥ |

[pratijñātacaturasrakuṇḍasyaitadātmacatuṣṭayātmakatvam]

etadrūpaṃ hi citkuṇḍaṃ caturasraṃ vibhāvayet || 118 ||

[kuṇḍasya svarūpam]

ānandamekhalāramyaṃ bindutrivalayānvitam |
ardhamātrāyonirūpaṃ brahmānandamayaṃ sadā || 119 ||

[tatra kuṇḍe homaḥ]

paradevamaye sarvajñānadīpavijṛmbhite |

juhuyāt sannibhe vahnau prapañcāhutimutkaṭam || 120 ||

śabdākhyamātṛkārūpamakṣavarṇavirājitam |
akṣarāṇi hutānyatra niḥśabdaṃ brahma jāyate || 121 ||


115 | yenedaṃ jñāyata iti sa jñānātmā parikīrtita iti vakṣyamāṇena sambandhaḥ |

120 | sannibha iti | satī nibhā dīptiryasya tasmin, sandīpita ityarthaḥ |

p. 562) [tatra haviḥkalpanam]

kṛtyākṛtye pāpapuṇye saṅkalpañca vikalpakam |
dharmādharmaiḥ sādhakendro havistena prakalpayet || 122 ||

[tena haviṣā homasyopadeśaḥ]

juhuyāc citsvarūpāgnau manubhistena yājakaḥ |
mūlavidyāṃ brahmarūpāṃ dhyātvā saṃvinmayīṃ parām || 123 ||

[tatra prathamo mantraḥ]

dharmādharmahavirdīpta ātmāgnau manasā śrucā |
suṣumṇā vartmanā nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyaham |
agnijāyānvito mantro mūlānte prakaṭīkṛtaḥ || 124 ||

[dvitīyo mantraḥ]

nābhau caitanyarūpāgnau haviṣā manasā śrucā |
jñānapradīpite nityamakṣavṛtīrjuhomyaham |
vahnijāyānvito mantro mūlānte samudāhṛtaḥ || 125 ||

[tṛtīyo mantraḥ]

prakāśākāśahastābhyāmavalambyonmanīśrucā |
dharmādharmakalāsnehapūrṇamagnau juhomyaham |
agnijāyānvito mantro mūlānte ca mahātmanā || 126 ||


123 | juhuyāditi | manubhiḥ dharmādharmetyādivakṣyamāṇamantraiḥ | tena prāk prakalpitena 
haviṣetyarthaḥ | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam |

124 | jñānahomasya prathamaṃ mantramāha - dharmeti | evañcā'dau mūlaṃ tato 
dharmādharmetyādijuhomyahamityantaṃ tataḥ svāhetyuktvā prathamāhutim ātmasvarūpāgnau 
juhuyādityarthaḥ | akṣavṛttīrindriyavṛttīḥ |

125 | dvitīyamāha - nābhāviti | atrāpi pūrvavanmūlaṃ tato nābhāvityādi juhomyahamityantaṃ 
tataḥ svāheti dvitīyāhutiṃ nābhisthe caitanyarūpāgnau juhuyādityarthaḥ |

126 | prakāśeti | atrāpi homakramaḥ pūrvavat |

p. 563) [caturtho mantraḥ]

antanirantaranirindhanamedhamāne
māyāndhakāraparipanthini saṃvidagnau |
kasmiṃścidadbhutamarīcivikāśabhūmau
viśvaṃ juhomi vasudhādiśivāvasānam || 127 ||

juhuyānmūlamantrānte brahmaprāptyai ca sādhakaḥ || 128 ||

[hoturbrahmarūpatvam]

niṣprapañco yadā kāle jāyate brahmavinnaraḥ |
tadā saṃvinmayaḥ sākṣāt kevalaṃ brahmarūpakaḥ || 129 ||

[upasaṃhāraḥ]

antarhomamanuḥ proktaḥ sarvapāpanikṛntanaḥ || 130 ||

iti śrīpūrṇānandaparamahaṃsaviracite śrītattvacintāmaṇau ṣoḍaśaḥ prakāśaḥ || 16 ||


127 | antariti | antardehābhyantare nirindhanaṃ dāhyamindhanamantareṇa edhamāne prajvalati tathā 
māyāndhakāraparipanthini māyārūpatamaḥpratibandhake adbhutamarīcivikāśabhūmau 
alaukikaraśmiprakaraprakāśākare kasmiṃścidanirvācyasvarūpe saṃviccaitanyaṃ tadrūpāgnau 
vasudhādiśivāvasānaṃ pṛthvītattvādiśivatattvāntātmakaṃ viśvaṃ juhomītyarthaḥ | 
atredamavadheyam - śaivāgame śivādīni kṣityantāni ṣaṭtriṃśattattvāni pratipāditāni | tāni ca 
pañcamaprakāśīya - 113-114 - śa- ślokaṭīkāyāṃ ṣaḍadhvanirūpaṇaprasaṅgena viśiṣya 
pradarśitāni | tatra sṛṣṭikrameṇa śivādikṣityantaṣaṭtriṃśattattvanirṇayaḥ kṛtaḥ | atra tu 
viśvavilayārthaṃ vidheye jñānahome layakrameṇa kṣityādiśivāntaṣaṭtriṃśattattvopadeśaḥ | 
ityabhiprāyeṇaiva vasudhādiśivāvasānamityuktam | homakramastu pūrvavat |



saptadaśaḥ prakāśaḥ

[śrīcakrasvarūpam, mudrāsvarūpam, yantranirmāṇopakaraṇādinirṇayaḥ, rātrau 
yantranirmāṇaniṣedhaḥ, tatra vyavasthā, śrīcakroddhāraḥ, tatra viśeṣaḥ, navacakrabhedāḥ, 
cakrarahasyam, pīṭhārcā]

[yantranirūpaṇapratijñā]

yantroddhāraṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvasiddhipradāyakam || 1 ||


1 | evaṃ pūrvaprakāśe'ntaryāgamabhidhāya prakāśe'smin śrīmattripurasundarīdevyāḥ 
pūjāyantramabhidhātuṃ pratijānīte - yantroddhāramityādinā | atredamavadheyam - 
śrīmattripurāyāstrividhaḥ saṅketo bhavati yantrasaṅketo mantrasaṅketaḥ pūjāsaṅketaśceti | 
taduktaṃ yoginīhṛdaye prathamapaṭale -

cakrasaṅketako mantra - pūjāsaṅketakastathā |
trividhastripurādevyāḥ saṅketaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
yāvadetanna jānāti saṅketatrayamuttamam |
na tāvat tripurācakre paramājñādharo bhavet ||

iti | cakrayantraśabdāvabhinnārthakau | tatra ca mantrasaṅketaḥ pūrvamabhihitaḥ | 
pūjāsaṅketaścāṣṭādaśaprakāśe vakṣyate | yantrasaṅketasya 
nirūpaṇārthametatprakāśārambhaḥ | saṅketapadārthaśca vyākhyāto 
yoginīhṛdayaṭīkāyāmamṛtānandanāthena - saṅketavat saṅketakaḥ, yathā kṛtasamayau 
kāminīkāmukau yatra kvāpi vasata evamasmin śivāviti | evañca bhagavatī kāmakalātmikā 
śrīmattripurādevī bhagavatā kāmeśvareṇātra cakre vasatīti saṅketapadādavagamyate |

p. 565) [yantrapadavyutpattiḥ]

yamayatyakhilaṃ pāpaṃ trāyate mahato bhayāt |
sādhakaṃ pūjanādghyānāttasmād yantraḥ prakīrttyate || 2 ||

ataḥ śarīraṃ yantrañca mantraṃ jñānamakhaṇḍitam || 3 ||

[śarīrasya traividhyam]

śarīraṃ trividhaṃ prāhurbhautikañca manomayam |
paraṃ jñānamayaṃ nityaṃ yadanāśi nirantaram || 4 ||


2 | yantrapadavyutpattimāha - yamayatītyādinā | etena pātakaniyantritvād 
bhayatrāṇakartṛtvāccāsya yantrasaṃjñā | tantrasāre tu -

kāmakrodhādidoṣottha - sarvaduḥkhaniyantraṇāt |
yantramityāhuretasmin devaḥ prīṇāti pūjitaḥ |
tathā - duḥkhaniyantraṇād yantramityāhustantravedinaḥ ||

iti yantrapadavyutpattiḥ pradarśitā |

3 | yantrasya devīśarīrarūpatvamabhidhatte - ata ityādinā | ataḥ 
prāguktākhilapāpaniyantṛtvādiguṇakatvād yantraṃ devyāḥ śarīramityarthaḥ | nanu yantraṃ 
devyāḥ śarīraṃ cet kiṃ tatra jñānamityata āha - mantraṃ jñānamiti | 
janyajñānavyāvartanāya akhaṇḍitamiti, nityamityarthaḥ | ata eva gandharvatantre caturthapaṭale 
śrīcakranirmāṇaparipāṭīmabhidhāya -

evametanmahācakraṃ mahāśrītripurāmayam |
yantraṃ śarīramityāhurmantraṃ jñānamakhaṇḍitam ||

ityanenāyamevārtho vyaktamuktaḥ |

4 | devīśarīrasya traividhyaṃ darśayati - śarīramityādinā | bhautikaṃ manomayaṃ 
jñānamayañceti trividhaṃ śarīramityarthaḥ | tatra jñānamayaśarīrasya viśeṣamāha - paramiti | 
niruktaśarīreṣu jñānamayaṃ śarīraṃ paraṃ śreṣṭhamityarthaḥ | śreṣṭhatve hetumāha - 
nityamityādinā | nityam utpattirahitam anāśi vināśarahitañca | nityamityanenaivotpattivināśa -

p. 566) [tatsvarūpam]

mudrā bhautikamityāhuryantraṃ viddhi manomayam |
mantraṃ jñānamayaṃ prāhurevaṃ tridhā vapurbhavet || 5 ||

atrāpi mānasadhvaṃse bhautikaṃ nāsti niścitam |
vināśi syāddvayaṃ tasmādekaṃ satyamiti sthitam || 6 ||

ādyantamadhyaśūnyatvānna kiñcinnānupaśyati |
kenāpi jāyate naitat sūto jñānamayaṃ yataḥ || 7 ||

na dṛṣṭaṃ na śrutaṃ kaścittato bhāvā bhavanti hi |
dṛśyante tatra kāyāniveha nānā pṛthagvidhāḥ || 8 ||

adho'dhaste sthūlarūpāḥ sūkṣmāścaivottarottaram |
nānāvarṇākṛtibhidā nānājātisamāśrayāḥ |
pratiṣṭhitārthāḥ sarveṣāṃ līyante tatra sarvaśaḥ || 9 ||

[pūrvapratijñātamudrāsvarūpam]

atha mudrāśarīrañca vakṣyāmi paramādbhutam || 10 ||

yonirūpā mahāmudrā śarīramiti kīrttyate |
saṃkṣobhaṇyā strikhaṇḍāntā navāṅgāni prakīrtitāḥ || 11 ||


rāhityārthāvagamasambhave'pi avināśīti punarupādānādatra nityapadenotpattirahitatvamātraṃ 
vivakṣitamiti bodhyam | phalitamāha - nirantaramiti | kālatrayāvacchedena varttamānamityarthaḥ |

5 | krameṇaiteṣāṃ svarūpaṃ nirvakti - mudretyādinā | tathā ca mudrā bhautikaṃ śarīram, 
yantro manomayaśarīram, mantraṃ jñānamayaśarīramiti trividhaṃ śarīramityarthaḥ |

10 | pūrvoddiṣṭeṣu mudrāśarīraṃ vaktuṃ pratijānīte - athetyādinā |

11 | mudrāśarīraṃ nirūpayan mudrāṇāmaṅgāṅgibhāvaṃ darśayati - yonirūpetyādinā |

p. 567) [mudrāpadavyutpattiḥ]

ātmānaṃ modayatyeṣā śāntānandamayaṃ dravam |
janayatyeva sā yasmānmudrā bhūtamayaṃ vapuḥ || 12 ||

[yantranirmāṇādhāropakaraṇādinirṇayaḥ]

gomayena ca saṃlipte bhūpradeśe manorame |
sindūrarajasā vā'pi rajobhiḥ kuṅkumasya vā || 13 ||


tatra yonimudrāyāstrailokyotpattimātṛkārūpatayā prādhānyādaṅgitvam ata eva mahāmudretyuktam 
| śarīramiti aṅgītyarthaḥ | athāṅgabhūtā mudrā uddiśati - saṃkṣobhaṇyā ityādinā | tāśca 
sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇī - sarvavidrāviṇī -sarvākarṣiṇī - sarvavaśyakarī � sarvonmādinī - mahāṅkuśā 
� khecarī � bīja -trikhaṇḍākhyā nava | evañcāṅgāṅgibhāvenāvasthitaṃ mudrādaśakameva 
mudrāśarīramiti vakturāśayaḥ | mudrāṇāmetāsāṃ svarūpāṇi tu aṣṭādaśapaṭale vakṣyante |

12 | ātmānamiti | yasmāt ātmānaṃ modayati śāntānandamayaṃ tamātmānaṃ dravaṃ 
janayati tasmād bhūtamayaṃ vapurmudretyucyata iti sambandhaḥ | tantrasāre tu -

modanāt sarvadevānāṃ drāvaṇāt pāpasantateḥ |
tasmānmudreti vikhyātā munibhistantravedibhiḥ ||

ityanyathā vyutpattikrama uktaḥ |

13 | atha śrīcakranirmāṇādhāropakaraṇādikamabhidhatte - gomayenetyādinā yantrarājaṃ 
samuddharedityantena | anyaccāha gandharvatantre caturthapaṭale -

tataḥ prāṅmukha āsīnaścakroddhāraṃ samārabhet |
hemarūpāditāmrāṇāṃ paṭṭe vā'timanohare ||
caturasre same śuddhe madhyocchūnavibhūṣite |
yugmāṅgamilitaṃ devi paṭṭasyeha samīritam ||
athavā parameśāni bhūmau cakraṃ samālikhet |
hastamātramite śuddhe gomayenopalepite ||

p. 568) yantrarājaṃ likhenmantrī svarṇaratnaśalākayā |
puṣpeṇa vilikhed vā'pi bilvasya kaṇṭakena vā || 14 ||

[tatra kalpāntaram]

athavā hemarajatatāmradarpaṇabhūrjake |
śrīkhaṇḍasambhave pīṭhe raktacandanasambhave || 15 ||

gaṇḍakībhavapāṣāṇe paṭe vā rucipīṭhake |
rocanākuṅkumābhyāntu candanena kulodbhavaiḥ || 16 ||

indukastūrikābhiśca yantrarājaṃ samuddharet |
caturasraṃ samārabhya navacakrāṇyanukramāt || 17 ||


sindūrarajasā tatra kuṅkumenāthavā priye |
ālikheccakrarājañca lekhanyā haimayā tathā ||
athavā kuśamūlena darbhapāṇiḥ samāhitaḥ |
netraramyaṃ yathā cakraṃ tathā kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

iti | jñānārṇave daśamapaṭale'pi -

śrīcakrantu varārohe śrīvidyāvarṇasambhavam |
tatra prāṅmukha āsīnaścakrarājaṃ samālikhet |
bhūpradeśe same varye sindūrarajasā'thavā |
kuṅkumasya rajobhistu bhūmau cakraṃ samālikhet |
ṛjurekhaṃ netraramyaṃ sandhibhedasamam ṛju |
athavā hemaraupyābhyāṃ tāmreṇa bahudhātubhiḥ |
paṭṭaṃ viracya śrīkhaṇḍaraktacandanasambhave |
paṭṭe saṃsthāpya vilikhellekhanyā haimayā priye |
rocanākuṅkumābhyāntu kasturīcandanendubhiḥ |

iti | evamanyāni ca pramāṇāni kaulāvalīnirṇayatṛtīyapaṭalādāvanusandheyāni | 
navacakrāṇyanukramāditi | navacakrāṇi trailokyamohanādīni sarvānandamayāntāni | tāni cāgre 
viśiṣya vakṣyante |

p. 569) [cakrasya krameṇonnatamadhyatve phalabhedāḥ]

unnatādunnataṃ madhyaṃ cakraṃ syānnirdhano dhanī |
tritrikramādunnataṃ tat pūjayitvā śriyaṃ labhet || 18 ||

ekadvitrikramāccaiva śriyai kīrttyai ca kalpate |
navāni samarūpāṇi sarvato'bhīṣṭasiddhaye || 19 ||

[śrīcakranirmāṇasthāpanapūjanapraśastiḥ]

kṛtvā śrīcakranirmāṇaṃ sthāpayet pūjayedapi |
lakṣmīkāntiyaśaḥputradhanārogyādisiddhaye || 20 ||

sīsakāṃsyādiṣu kṛtaṃ pūrvokta viparītakṛt || 21 ||

[yantramantradevatānāmabhedaḥ]

yantraṃ mantramayaṃ proktaṃ mantrātmā devateti ca |
purā bhagavatā proktaṃ tantre sammohanākhyake |
dehātmanoryathā'bhedo'yantradevatayostathā || 22 ||


18-19 | unnatādunnatamiti | trailokyamohanacakrataḥ sarvānandamayacakraṃ yāvat krameṇa 
yathonnatiḥ syāttathā cakraṃ racayediti phalitārthaḥ | tat phalamāha- nirdhana iti | evaṃ kṛte 
nirdhano'pi sādhako dhanavān bhavedityarthaḥ | tritrīti | prathamāt trailokyamohanacakrād dvitīyaṃ 
sarvāśāparipūrakaṃ cakraṃ triguṇonnatam, sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇākhyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tatastriguṇonnatam, 
sarvasaubhāgyadāyakākhyaṃ caturthaṃ tatastriguṇonnatamityevaṃkrameṇa yadi aunnatyaṃ vidadhīta 
tarhi taccakraṃ pūjayitvā śriyaṃ labhedityarthaḥ | ekadvitrīti | prāguktakramonnatiparipāṭyāṃ 
prathamasyaikaguṇatvaṃ dvitīyasya pūrvato dviguṇatvaṃ tatastṛtīyasya pūrvatastriguṇatvaṃ evaṃ 
krameṇa caturthādicakrāṇāṃ krameṇaunnatye śrīkīrtisādhakatvaṃ yantrasyetyarthaḥ | navānīti | 
navānāṃ tulyaunnatye'bhīṣṭasampādakatvaṃ cakrasyetyarthaḥ |

21 | pūrveti | pūrvoktaviparītakṛt lakṣmīkāntyādivināśakṛdityarthaḥ |

p. 570) [yantraṃ vinā pūjane doṣaḥ]

atha pīṭhe mahāyantraṃ likhitvā pūjayed budhaḥ |
vinā yantreṇa cet pūjā devatā na prasīdati || 23 ||

[rātrau yantranirmāṇaniṣedhaḥ]

na rātrāvaṅkayed yantraṃ sādhakastu kadācana |
pramādādaṅkite yantre śāpo bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 24 ||

[tatra yantrapuṣpe pūjanopadeśaḥ]

bhāvacūḍāmaṇimate tatra puṣpe prapūjayet |

[yantrapuṣpāṇi]

yatrāparājitāpuṣpaṃ javāpuṣpañca bhairava || 25 ||


23 | vinā yantreṇeti | spaṣṭam | atraitadavadheyam - praśastamapīdaṃ yantranirmāṇaṃ 
devatāyāḥ purato na kartavyam, dehātmanoryathā'bhedo yantradevatayostatheti pūrvavacanena 
yantradevatayorabhedokterubhayoreva tulyatayā pūjyatvena devatāpūjayā yantrapūjāyāḥ 
siddherasambhavāt | etadabhiprāyeṇaiva mātṛkābhedatantre dvādaśapaṭale -

śālagrāme ca pūjāyāṃ na likhed yantramuttamam |
pratimāyāñca pūjāyāṃ na likhed yantramuttamam ||

ityuktam | pramādataḥ kṛte tu pūjanādikaṃ dviguṇamanuṣṭheyam, taduktaṃ tatraiva -

mahāyantrantu deveśi yadi kuryācca sādhakaḥ |
tatra mūrtiṃ na kuryācca kadācidapi mohataḥ ||
yadi mūrtiṃ prakuryāttu tatra yantraṃ na kārayet |
yadi kuryāttu mohena yajed vāradvayaṃ priye ||
dviguṇaṃ pūjanaṃ tatra dviguṇaṃ balidānakam |
dviguṇaṃ prajapenmantraṃ dviguṇaṃ homayet sudhīḥ |
anyathā viphalā pūjā viphalaṃ balidānakam |
sarvaṃ hi viphalaṃ yasmāttasmād yantraṃ na kārayet || iti |

p. 571) karavīre raktaśukle droṇaṃ vā yatra tiṣṭhati |
tatra devī vasennityaṃ tadyantre pūjanaṃ mama || 26 ||

[śrīcakranirmāṇaparipāṭī tatra trikoṇamaṣṭakoṇañca]

trikoṇaṃ vilikhedādau tanmadhyācca trikoṇakam || 27 ||

pūrvaśaktyagrabhāge tu mānayaṣṭivadālikhet || 28 ||

sandhibhedakrameṇaiva tayoḥ śaktyostataḥ param |
rekhe ākṛṣya koṇābhyāṃ tadagre pūrvage guruḥ || 29 ||

etattrayātmakenaiva navakoṇaṃ prajāyate |


27-30 | atha śrīcakranirmāṇakramamupadiśati - trikoṇamityādinā caturdvāraṃ tathaiva cetyantena | 
tatra trikoṇāntarvartino binducakrasya sarvānandamayākhyasya nirmāṇe viśeṣābhāvāt tadupekṣya 
trikoṇādibhūpurāntānāmaṣṭānāṃ nirmāṇaparipāṭīmabhidadhānaḥ prathamaṃ trikoṇasya 
sarvasiddhipradacakrasya tadbahiraṣṭakoṇasya sarvarogaharacakrasya ca nirmāṇaṃ yugapadāha - 
trikoṇamityādinā navakoṇaṃ prajāyate ityantena | ādau pratīcyagramekaṃ trikoṇaṃ likhet | 
tanmadhyāt niruktatrikoṇamadhyapradeśāt trikoṇakaṃ pratīcyagramanyattrikoṇamālikhet | 
etattrikoṇasyāgraṃ pūrvatrikoṇasya madhyadeśe mūlantu tadbahiriti jñeyam | anantarakaraṇīyamāha 
- pūrvaśaktyagrabhāga iti | śaktiśabdena trikoṇamucyate | tataśca pūrvaśaktyagrabhāge 
madhyatrikoṇāgre mānayaṣṭivat dakṣiṇottarāyatamānadaṇḍavat dakṣiṇottarāyatāmekāṃ 
saralarekhāmālikhet | sandhīti | tataḥ paraṃ 
niruktatrikoṇadvayamadhyatrikoṇāgrasaralarekhānirmāṇānantaraṃ guruḥ tayoḥ śaktyoḥ koṇābhyāṃ 
rekhe rekhādvayamākṛṣya tadagre tayo rekhayoragre agradvayaṃ pūrvage pūrvadiggāminī kuryāditi 
śeṣaḥ | prāguktasaralarekhāyā dakṣottarābhyām agrābhyāṃ tatsambaddhaṃ 
pūrvatrikoṇadvayasandhibhedi pūrvāgraṃ rekhādvayaṃ vidadhītetyarthaḥ | etena prāgagraṃ 
trikoṇāntaraṃ prāptam | evañca paścimāgraṃ trikoṇadvayaṃ pūrvāgrañcaikaṃ 
trikoṇameteṣāñca prāguktarītyā vinyāse madhye trikoṇamekaṃ sarvasiddhi -

p. 572) [antardaśāram]

pūrvaśaktīśavahnibhyāṃ yonirūpāṃ likhettataḥ || 30 ||

dakṣiṇottarakoṇāttu śrīkaṇṭhasya likhed budhaḥ |
rekhāṃ ṣaṭkoṇarūpāntu pūrvaśaktīśavahnitaḥ || 31 ||

ūrddhvarekhe samākṛṣya śrīkaṇṭhāgravicumbinī |
śrīkaṇṭhasya dakṣavāmakoṇāt pūrvavaduddharet || 32 ||

daśakoṇaṃ bhavettatra

[vahirdaṃśāram]

			tathā'nyaddaśakaṃ likhet |


pradākhyaṃ tadbahiraṣṭakoṇañca sarvarogaharākhyaṃ bhavataḥ | ityabhiprāyeṇaiva mūle 
navakoṇaṃ prajāyata ityuktam | gandharvatantre pañcamapaṭale'pi -

pratīcyagraṃ likhedekaṃ trikoṇaṃ taduparyatha |
pūrvatrikoṇamadhyāgraṃ trikoṇāntaramālikhet ||
atha sandhidvayaṃ bhittvā prāgagraṃ vilikhet param |
trikoṇamadhyagaṃ cakramaṣṭakoṇaṃ tathā bhavet ||

	iti vacane aṣṭakoṇapadasya trikoṇamadhyagamiti viśeṣaṇena madhye trikoṇaṃ 
tadbahiraṣṭakoṇamiti sphuṭamavagamyate |

30-33 | athāṣṭakoṇād bahirlekhanīyamantardaśāraṃ sarvarakṣākarākhyaṃ cakramāha - 
pūrvaśaktītyādinā daśakoṇaṃ bhavettatretyantena | pūrvaśaktyā madhyatrikoṇasya 
īśavahnibhyāṃ īśānāgnikoṇābhyāṃ yonirūpāṃ yonyākārāṃ paścimābhimukhīṃ 
trikoṇarekhāṃ likhet | trikoṇāntaramāha - dakṣiṇeti | śrīkaṇṭhasya dakṣiṇottarakoṇābhyāmapi 
pūrvāgrāṃ tathaiva yonyākārāṃ trikoṇarekhāṃ likhet | ime ca rekhe prāguktāsu tisṛṣu 
dakṣiṇottarāyatāsu saralarekhāsu pūrvapaścimarekhe varddhayitvā tataḥ kartavye iti 
vyaktīkariṣyāmaḥ | evaṃkṛte ṣaṭkoṇarūpā rekhā bhavati | tataḥ karaṇīyamāha - pūrveti | 
pūrvaśaktīśavahnitaḥ pūrvaśakteḥ pūrvatrikoṇasya īśānāgnikoṇābhyām ūrddhva rekhe 
sandhibhedapūrvakamūrddhva rekhādvaya -

p. 573) [caturdaśāram]

daśāravāmadakṣe tu madhyakoṇacatuṣṭayam || 33 ||

vardhayitvā ca ṣaṭkoṇaṃ sandhibhedena saṃlikhet |
kakṣamadhyagatā rekhā vardhayet koṇarūpataḥ || 34 ||

śeṣaṃ pravardheyenmantrī daśakoṇavidhānataḥ |
yathā caturdaśārantu jāyate cakramuttamam || 35 ||


mākarṣet | śrīkaṇṭhāgravicumbinīti rekhāviśeṣaṇena sandhibhedapūrvakaṃ rekhākarṣaṇamuktam | 
vicumbinyāviti vaktavye vicumbinītyārṣaḥ prayogaḥ | śrīkaṇṭhasyeti pūrvavat 
koṇarekhākarṣaṇakrameṇa śrīkaṇṭhasya dakṣavāmakoṇāt 
pūrvopadarśitadakṣottarāyatapaścimasaralarekhāṃ yāvat prāguktakrameṇa koṇarekhādvayaṃ 
sandhibhedapūrvakamuddharedityarthaḥ | evaṃ kṛte daśakoṇaṃ sarvarakṣākaracakraṃ niṣpadyate | 
vyaktamāha gandharvatantre pañcamapaṭale -

tato daśāracakrasya nirmāṇaṃ śṛṇu pārvati |
dakṣiṇottaragā rekhāstisro yāstatra tāsvapi |
madhyarekhāṃ parityajya rekhe dve parivardhayet |
tena ṣaṭkoṇakaṃ kṛtvā sandhibhede catuṣṭayam |
kṛtvā rekhādvayaṃ kuryāddaśakoṇa yathā bhavat || iti |

	atha bahirdaśāraṃ sarvārthasādhakaṃ cakramāha - tatheti | tathā 
nirūktadaśārakrameṇaivedaṃ bahirdaśāraṃ kuryāt nātra kaścidapi viśeṣo'sti | vyaktamāha 
gandharvatantre pañcamapaṭale -

tathānyadaśakoṇasya nirmāṇaṃ parikīrttyate |
dakṣiṇottaragāsvevaṃ rekhāḥ pañca bhavanti yāḥ ||
tāsāṃ rekhādvayaṃ (trayaṃ) madhye hitvā pūrvāparantataḥ |
bāhyarekhādvayaṃ devi pūrvavat parivardhayet ||
tena ṣaṭkoṇakaṃ kṛtvā sandhibhedacatuṣṭayam |
kṛtvā rekhādvayaṃ kuryāddaśakoṇaṃ yathā bhavet || iti |

33-35 | atha caturdaśāraṃ sarvasaubhāgyadāyakacakramāha - daśāretyādinā 
cakramuttamamityantena | prāguktadaśāracakrasya vāmato dakṣataśca madhyakoṇacatuṣṭayaṃ 
vardhayitvā,

p. 574) [aṣṭārādicakratrayam]

vṛttamaṣṭadalaṃ vṛttaṃ ṣoḍaśāraṃ trivṛttakam |
bhūbimbatritayaṃ kāryaṃ caturdvāraṃ tathaiva ca || 36 ||

tantracūḍāmaṇau devyā matamīdṛśamīritam || 37 ||


prāguktasandhibhedakrameṇa ṣaṭkoṇaṃ saṃlikhet | etaduktaṃ bhavati - daśāracakrasya 
paścimatastrikoṇatrayasya pūrvataśca trikoṇatrayasya tyāge daśārasya dakṣataḥ koṇadvayaṃ 
vāmataśca koṇadvayamiti koṇacatuṣṭayamavaśiṣyate | tat koṇacatuṣṭayaṃ koṇaghaṭakasaralarekhā 
ityarthaḥ, prāguktakrameṇa vardhayitvā tataḥ prāguktasandhibhedakrameṇa ṣaṭkoṇaṃ saṃlikhet | 
kakṣamadhyagatā rekhāḥ prāguktaṣaṭkoṇavāmadakṣakoṇaghaṭakarekhācatuṣṭayamityarthaḥ, 
koṇarūpeṇa vardhayet | evaṃ kṛte koṇāṣṭakaṃ jāyate | śeṣamiti | avaśiṣṭaṃ koṇaṣaṭkaṃ 
prāguktadaśakoṇarītyā vardhayet | pūrvapaścimānta -saralarekhādvayaṃ pūrvavad varddhayitvā 
daśārasya paścimataḥ pūrvataścaikaikāṃ dakṣiṇottarāyatāṃ saralarekhāmākṛṣya 
daśārakrameṇa ṣaṭ koṇān vardhayedityevā'ha daśakoṇavidhānata ityanena | yatheti, tathā 
pravardhayed yathā uttamaṃ caturdaśāraṃ cakraṃ jāyata iti sambandhaḥ | vyaktamāha 
gandharvatantre pañcamapaṭale -

caturdaśāracakrasya nirmāṇaṃ kathyate'dhunā |
dakṣiṇottaragāsvevaṃ rekhāḥ sapta bhavanti yāḥ ||
tāsāṃ rekhādvayaṃ hitvā bāhye pūrvāparantataḥ |
dīrghaṃ rekhādvayaṃ mantrī pūrvavat parivardhayet ||
tena ṣaṭkoṇakaṃ kṛtvā sandhibhedacatuṣṭayam | 
daśārīkṛtya tenaiva sandhibhedacatuṣṭayāt ||
rekhādvayaṃ bahiḥ kuryādrekhāḥ syurnava pañca ca ||
kalpayedatha tasyāgrairanyakoṇacatuṣṭayam ||
evaṃ caturdaśāraṃ syād vilocanamanoharam || iti

36-37 | athāṣṭadalaṃ sarvasaṅkṣobhaṇākhyaṃ ṣoḍaśadalaṃ sarvāśāparipūrakākhyaṃ 
caturasraṃ trailokyamohanākhyaṃ cakrañcā'ha - vṛttamityādinā | spaṣṭam | 
atrāṣṭadalaṣoḍaśadalayormūle vṛttaṃ sīmārthameva | ṣoḍaśadalād bahirvṛttatrayamapi na 
pūjārtham -

vṛttatrayaṃ maheśāni na pūjāyāṃ nigadyate |

p. 575) [atra viśeṣaḥ]

kuloḍḍīśamate dikṣu mūlaṃ pūrvādito bhavet |

[binducakrasya sarvacakramadhyavarttitvam]

sarvamadhyatrikoṇe tu binducakraṃ likhed budhaḥ || 38 ||


iti gandharvatantrapañcamapaṭalavacanāt | cakramidaṃ saṃkṣipyā'ha tantrasāradhṛtayāmale -

bindutrikoṇavasukoṇadaśārayugma -
manvasranāgadalasaṅgataṣoḍaśāram |
vṛttatrayañca dharaṇīsadanatrayañca
śrīcakrarājamuditaṃ paradevatāyāḥ ||

iti | etadyantraghaṭakapadme keśarakalpananiṣedhamāha tantrasāradhṛtabhūtabhairave -

yo'smin yantre maheśāni keśarāṇi prakalpayet ||
yoginīsahitāstasya hiṃsāṃ kurvanti bhairavāḥ || iti |

38 | evaṃ tatracūḍāmaṇisaṃvādena śrīcakranirmāṇaparipāṭīmabhidhāya kuloḍḍīśasaṃvādena 
nava cakrāṇi vibhajate - kuletyādinā | nava cakrāṇi teṣāṃ nāmāni ca yoginīhṛdaye 
prathamapaṭale krameṇa pradarśitāni yathā -

cakrasya navadhātvañca kathayāmi tava priye |
ādimaṃ bhūtrayeṇa syād dvitīyaṃ ṣoḍaśārakam ||
anyadaṣṭadalaṃ proktaṃ manukoṇamanantaram |
pañcamaṃ daśakoṇaṃ syāt ṣaṣṭhañcāpi daśārakam ||
saptamaṃ vasukoṇaṃ syānmadhyatryasramathāṣṭamam |
navamaṃ tryasramadhyaṃ syāt teṣāṃ nāmānyataḥ śṛṇu ||
trailokyamohanaṃ cakraṃ sarvāśāparipūrakam |
sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇaṃ devi sarvasaubhāgyadāyakam |
sarvārthasādhakaṃ cakraṃ sarvarakṣākaraṃ param ||
sarvarogaharaṃ devi sarvasiddhimayaṃ tathā |
sarvānandamayañcāpi navamaṃ śṛṇu sundari ||

p. 576) [trailokyamohanacakram]

trailokyamohanaṃ nāma caturasraṃ sukhapradam | 
muktābhaṃ cintayenmantrī mātṛkārṇairvimaṇḍitam || 39 ||

[sarvāśāparipūrakacakram]

śaradindupratīkāśaṃ ṣoḍaśāraṃ manoramam |
sarvāśāpūrakaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśasvarasaṃyutam || 40 ||


iti | evañcātra navamaṃ tryasramadhyaṃ syādityanena yat sarvānandamayaṃ cakramuktaṃ tadeva 
sarvamadhyetyādinā mūle samuddiṣṭam-

atra pūjyā mahādevī mahātripurasundarī |

iti yoginīhṛdayaprathamapaṭalavacanenāsya prādhānyāt prathamamabhidhānam, viśeṣastvagre 
vakṣyate | binducakraṃ śivātmakamadhyabindumityarthaḥ |

sarvānandamayaṃ madhyabindusthānaṃ śivātmakam | iti tantrarājapañcamapaṭalavacanāt |

39 | atha krameṇa trailokyamohanādinavacakrāṇi vivṛṇoti - trailokyetyādinā | caturasraṃ bhūpuram, 
tacca parito rekhātrayātmakam; bhūbimbatritayaṃ kāryamiti prāguktatvāt,

bhūbimbatrayametat paśyantyāditrimātṛviśrāntiḥ |

iti kāmakalāvilāsokteśca | madhyatrikoṇādāvirbhūtā paśyantī � madhyamā -vaikharīśaktayo 
niruktabhūpuratrayaparyantaṃ cakāsatītyarthaḥ | muktābhaṃ śvetavarṇamityarthaḥ |

trailokyamohane cakre buddhañca paripūjayet |
brahmaviṣṇuśivākhyāstāstisro rekhāśca tannibhāḥ ||

iti gandharvatantracaturthapaṭalavacanena tu rekhāṇāṃ krameṇa 
tadadhiṣṭhātṛbrahmaviṣṇuśivavarṇatvamabhihitam | tena kramādetāsāṃ raktakṛṣṇaśvetavarṇatvaṃ 
niruktagandharvavacanādavagamyate | mātṛkārṇeriti akārādikṣakārāntamātṛkāvarṇairityarthaḥ | 
taduktaṃ jñānārṇave daśamapaṭale -

caturasraṃ mātṛkārṇermaṇḍitaṃ siddhihetave |
muktāmāṇikyaghaṭitaṃ samasthalavirājitam ||
trailokyamohanaṃ nāma kalpadrumaphalapradam | iti |

40 | atha ṣoḍaśadalātmakaṃ sarvāśāparipūrakacakramāha - śaradindvityādinā | 
śaradindupratīkāśaṃ śaratkālīnacandrasadṛśavarṇaviśiṣṭam | taduktaṃ jñānārṇave 
daśamapaṭale -

p. 577) [sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇacakram]

javākusumasaṅkāśamaṣṭapatraṃ suśobhanam |
sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇaṃ nāma kādivargāṣṭakairyutam || 41 ||

[niruktatritayātmakaṃ sṛṣṭicakram]

etaccakramayaṃ ramyaṃ sṛṣṭikārakamuttamam || 42 ||

[sarvasaubhāgyadāyakacakram]

caturdaśārakoṇantu navīnārkasamaprabham |
sarvasaubhāgyadaṃ nāma kādiḍhāntairvibhūṣitam || 43 ||


ṣoḍaśāraṃ candrabimbarūpantu sakalālayam |
sarvāśāpūrakaṃ bhadre sravatpīyūṣavarṣaṇam ||

iti | ṣoḍaśāraṃ ṣoḍaśadalam | manomayam | atra manaḥpadaṃ 
bhūtapañcakendriyadaśakamanasāṃ devyāḥ ṣoḍaśavikārāṇāmupalakṣakam | 
niruktaṣoḍaśavikārāḥ kāmākarṣiṇyādiṣoḍaśaśaktyākāreṇa ṣoḍaśadalānyadhyāsata iti 
tātparyam | vyaktamāha kāmakalāvilāse -

bhūtānīndriyadaśakaṃ manaśca devyā vikāraṣoḍaśakam |
kāmākarṣiṇyādisvarūpataḥ ṣoḍaśāramadhyāste ||

iti | ṣoḍaśasvarasaṃyutam akārādiṣoḍaśasvaraviśiṣṭam | ṣoḍaśapatreṣu krameṇa ṣoḍaśa 
svarā vartanta ityarthaḥ |

41 | athāṣṭadalātmakaṃ sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇacakramāha - javetyādinā | javākusumasaṅkāśaṃ 
javākusumavadraktavarṇam | aṣṭapatramaṣṭadalam | kādivargāṣṭakairyutam, pañcadaleṣu 
kādipañcavargāḥ | ṣaṣṭhadale yaralavāḥ | saptamadale śaṣasahāḥ | aṣṭamadale lakṣāviti 
vargāṣṭakāvasthitikramaḥ |

42 | etattritayasya sṛṣṭicakratvamāha - etadityādinā | etaccakramayaṃ niruktatrailokyamohana - 
sarvāśāparipūraka -sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇākhyacakratrayamayamityarthaḥ | jñānārṇave 
daśamapaṭale'pi -

etattrayaṃ maheśāni sṛṣṭicakraṃ sukhapradam |

ityuktam | viśeṣastu kāmakalāvilāsa - yoginīhṛdayādiṣu draṣṭavyaḥ |

43 | evaṃ sṛṣṭyātmakacakratrayamuktvā sthityātmakacakratraye vaktavye kramaprāptaṃ

p. 578) [sarvārthasādhakacakram]

taptakāñcanasindūrasadṛśaṃ daśakoṇakam |
sarvārthasādhakaṃ nāma ṇādibhāntairvibhūṣitam || 44 ||

[sarvarakṣākaracakram]

taruṇāruṇasaṅkāśaṃ dvitīyaṃ daśakoṇakam |
sarvarakṣākaraṃ nāma mādikṣāntākṣarairyutam || 45 || 

[niruktatritayātmakaṃ sthiticakram]

sthiticakraṃ trayañcaitanmahājñānapradāyakam || 46 ||


sarvasaubhāgyadāyakaṃ cakramāha - caturdaśāretyādinā | caturdaśārakoṇamiti spaṣṭam | 
navīnārkasamaprabhaṃ bhāsvararaktavarṇam | taduktaṃ jñānārṇave daśamapaṭale -

caturdaśāraṃ deveśi dāḍimīkusumaprabham |
anantaphaladaṃ bhadre sarvasaubhāgyasaṃpradam ||

iti | kādīti | caturdaśakoṇeṣu kakārādiḍhakārāntāścaturdaśa varṇā ekaikaśo varttanta ityarthaḥ 
|

44 | atha sarvārthasādhakacakraṃ vivṛṇoti - taptetyādinā | taptakāñcanasindūrasadṛśaṃ 
raktavarṇam | taduktaṃ jñānārṇave daśamapaṭale -

daśāraṃ taptahemābhaṃ sindūrasadṛśaṃ priye |
sarvārthasādhakaṃ cakraṃ manaścintitadaṃ sadā ||

iti | daśakoṇakaṃ daśakoṇaviśiṣṭam | ṇādīti | ṇakārādibhakārāntā daśa varṇā 
daśāreṣvekaikaśo virājanta ityarthaḥ |

45 | dvitīyadaśakoṇātmakaṃ sarvarakṣākaracakramāha - taruṇetyādinā | taruṇāruṇasaṅkāśaṃ 
raktavarṇam | mādīti | makārādikṣakārāntā daśa varṇā ekaikaśo daśāreṣu vartanta ityarthaḥ 
|

46 | etattritayasya sthiticakratvamāha - sthitītyādinā | etattrayaṃ sarvasaubhāgyadāyaka � 
sarvārthasādhaka - sarvarakṣākarākhyaṃ mahājñānapradāyakaṃ sthiticakramiti sambandhaḥ | 
jñānārṇave daśamapaṭale'pi niruktacakratrayamabhidhāya -

p. 579) [sarvarogaharacakram]

padmarāgapratīkāśamaṣṭakoṇañca siddhidam |
sarvarogaharaṃ nāma vargāṣṭakairvimaṇḍitam || 47 ||

[sarvasiddhipradacakram]

dāḍimīpuṣpasaṅkāśaṃ trikoṇaṃ sarvabhūtidam |
sarvasiddhipradaṃ cakramakathāditrirekhagam || 48 ||

[sarvānandamayacakram]

ha- kṣavarṇamadhyagataṃ sarveṣāṃ siddhikārakam |
sarvānandamayaṃ nāma binducakraṃ paraṃ padam || 49 ||


etattrayaṃ maheśāni sthiticakraṃ sukhapradam |

ityuktam |

47 | evaṃ sthityātmaka - cakratrayamabhidhāya saṃhārātmakacakratraye vaktavye kramaprāptaṃ 
sarvarogaharacakramāha - padmarāgetyādinā | padmarāgamaṇisavarṇaṃ raktavarṇam | 
vargāṣṭakairvimaṇḍitam | sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇacakroktarītyā kādivargāṣṭakamaṣṭāreṣu vartanta 
ityarthaḥ | taduktaṃ jñānārṇave daśamapaṭale -

aṣṭakoṇaṃ varārohe bālārkakiraṇāruṇam |
padmarāgasamaprakhyaṃ sarvarogaharaṃ sadā || iti |

48 | sarvasiddhipradacakramāha - dāḍimītyādinā | dāḍimīpuṣpasaṅkāśaṃ 
dāḍimīkusumavadraktavarṇam | sarvabhūtidam akhilavibhūtisampādakam | a-ka-thāditrirekhagam | 
akārādayaḥ ṣoḍaśa svarāḥ, kādi - tāntāni thādi - sāntāni vyañjanāni ca 
krameṇaikaikarekhārūpeṇa vartanta ityarthaḥ | jñānārṇave daśamapaṭale -

udyatsūryasahasrābhaṃ bandhūkakusumaprabham |
sarvasiddhipradaṃ cakraṃ sakalālayamīśvari |
trikoṇaṃ sarvasaṃbhūtikāraṇaṃ bhūtidaṃ sadā || iti |

49 | athedānīṃ caramaṃ bhagavatyā nivāsasthānaṃ bindvākhyaṃ sarvānandamayacakramāha - 
ha-kṣeti | paraṃ padaṃ kāmeśvarāṅkanilayāyā deśakālākārādibhiranavacchinnāyāḥ

p. 580) [niruktatritayātmakaṃ saṃhāracakram]

sadāśivamayañcaitattrayaṃ saṃhārakārakam || 50 ||

[niruktacakre puṣpāñjalidānopadeśaḥ]

yoginīmūlamantreṇa tatra puṣpāñjaliṃ kṣipet |

[cakrasyāśūnyatāsampādanopadeśaḥ]

muktā-maṇi-pravālairvā vilomamūlavidyayā || 51 ||

aśūnyaṃ sarvadā kāryaṃ śūnye vighnaṃ sadā bhavet |

[navacakropasaṃhāraḥ]

navacakramayaṃ cakraṃ bālādevyā virājitam || 52 ||

[cakreśvarīṇāṃ nāmāni]

prathamā tripurā devī dvitīyā tripureśvarī |
tṛtīyā sundarīnāmnī turyā tripuravāsinī || 53 ||


śrīmattripurasundaryāḥ paraṃ padaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ nivāsasthānamityarthaḥ | ata eva yoginīhṛdaye 
prathamapaṭale sarvānandacakramabhidhāya -

atra pūjyā mahādevī mahātripurasundarī |

ityuktam | vyaktamāha kāmakalāvilāse -

āsīnā bindumaye cakre sā tripurasundarī devī |
kāmeśvarāṅkanilayā kalayā candrasya kalpitottaṃsā ||
pāśāṅkuśekṣucāpa-prasūnaśarapañcakāñcitasvakarā |
bālāruṇāruṇāṅgī śaśibhānukṛśānulocanatritayā ||

iti | atra bālāruṇāruṇāṅgītyabhidhānād bindorapi raktatvamuktaṃ bhavati | 
hakṣavarṇamadhyagatamiti, ha-kṣau varṇau madhyagatau yasyeti vigrahaḥ |

51-52 | mukteti | yantraṃ pūjayitvā tatra vilomamūlamantreṇa muktādyanyatamaṃ sthāpayet 
kadā'pi śūnyaṃ na vidadhītetyarthaḥ | upasaṃharati-naveti | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam |

53-54 | evaṃtrailokyamohanādīni sarvānandamayāntāni nava cakrāṇyupadiśya teṣu

p. 581) pañcamī tripurāśrīśca ṣaṣṭhe tripuramālinī |
saptame tripurā siddhā aṣṭame tripurāmbikā || 54 ||

navame śrīmahādevī mahātripurabhairavī |

[cakre svasvasthāne'rcanīyā devatāḥ]

samastāḥ prakaṭā guptāstathā guptatarāśrayāḥ || 55 ||

sampradāyāḥ kulāḥ kaulā nigarbhāśca rahasyagāḥ |
parāpararahasyāha paramātirahasyagāḥ || 56 ||

rājarājeśvarī pūrṇā mahātripurasundarī |
etāścakrasthitāḥ sarvāḥ sthānaṃ jñātvā samarcayet || 57 ||

[niruktacakrasaṅketajñānavaibhavam]

navadhā cakrasaṅketaṃ yo jānāti sa sādhakaḥ |
navadhā yo na jānāti kathaṃ devīṃ prapūjayet || 58 ||

[niruktaśrīcakrajñānapraśastiḥ]

etacchrīcakrarājaṃ sakalalipimayaṃ sarvadevasya vandyaṃ
nityānandasvarūpaṃ bhuvanavaśakaraṃ bhogamokṣaikahetum |


krameṇa pūjanīyānāṃ navacakreśvarīṇāṃ nāmāni nirūpayati - prathametyādinā | 
yoginīhṛdaye prathamapaṭale'pi niruktakrameṇa nāmānyabhidhāya -

pūjayecca kramādetā navacakre purodite |

ityuktam |

55-57 | navama iti | mahātripurabhairavītyatra mahātripurasundarīti yoginīhṛdaye | samastā iti | 
āsāmāvaraṇaśaktīnāṃ svasvasthānanāmārcākramādīni aṣṭādaśe vakṣyante |

p. 582) śuddho bhūtvā likhitvā yadi paśurahito dhārayet pūjayed vā
sarvā siddhiḥ karasthā bhavati sukṛtinastasya siddhiḥ parā'pi || 59 ||

sarvānandamayaṃ samastajagatāmekādhipaṃ kāmadaṃ
sarvavyādhinirākarañca śubhadaṃ jñānapradaṃ kīrtidam |
sākṣāt kalpataruṃ surāsuragaṇaiḥ saṃveṣṭitaṃ dṛśyate
yenedaṃ sa tu sarvatīrthagamanasnānasya puṇyaṃ labhet || 60 ||

[śrīcakrarahasyanirūpaṇapratijñā]

rahasyaṃ cakrarājasya kathayāmi yathākramam |

[trikoṇāntarbindurahasyam]

madhye binduḥ śivo jñeyaḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakaḥ || 61 ||

bindumadhye vasennityaṃ śrīmattripurasundarī |
mahākāmakalā tatra bindutritayarūpiṇī || 62 ||

[trikoṇarahasyam]

trikoṇaṃ tripurā caiva bālā tripurasundarī |
trikoṇarūpī rudraḥ syāttrikoṇatvāt parā śivā || 63 ||

triṣu pañcadaśī nityā samayā ca caturdaśī |
ṣaḍaṅgayuvatī devī tatra pūrvāditaḥ sthitā || 64 ||


61-62 | atha śrīcakrarahasyaṃ vaktumupakramate - rahasyamityādinā | madhye sarvasiddhipradākhya 
- trikoṇamadhye | binduḥ śivātmakaḥ śvetabinduḥ | bindumadhye śivātmakasitabindumadhye 
śoṇavindvātmikā mahākāmakalā śrīmattripurasundarī vilasatītyarthaḥ | tāṃ viśinaṣṭi - 
bindvityādinā | bindutritayarūpiṇī śivaśaktirūpaṃ niruktabindudvayam, tayoḥ samarasībhūtastṛtīyo 
miśrabinduḥ tattritayarūpiṇītyarthaḥ | viśeṣastu bhūmikāyāṃ draṣṭavyaḥ |

p. 583) [aṣṭārarahasyam]

aṣṭakoṇeṣvaṣṭasiddhirbhūtayo'ṣṭau bhavanti hi |
jṛmbhamohavaśastambhapāśāṅkuśadhanuḥśarāḥ || 65 ||

nivasanti tatra nityaṃ vaśinyādyaṣṭadevatāḥ || 66 ||

[antardaśāra-rahasyam]

daśendriyāṇi deheṣu saṃsthitāni vasanti ca |
sarvajñādimahādevāḥ saṃsthitā daśa devatāḥ || 67 ||

evaṃ dikkoṇasandhyagraprāṇā daśakalā daśa || 68 ||

[bahirdaśāra-rahasyam]

aparaṃ daśakoṇantu daśadikpāladaivatam |
digvātārkapraceto'śvi-vahnīndropendramitra-kāḥ || 69 ||

vaikārikāttu te jātā daśasaṃkhyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
sarvasiddhyādayastatra sadā tiṣṭhanti pūjitāḥ || 70 ||

[caturdaśārarahasyam]

anantarūpacakrasya tathā koṇacaturdaśa |
caturdaśāṅgaśāstrāṇi bhuvanāni caturdaśa || 71 ||

caturdaśāracakreṣu saṃkṣobhaṇyādidevatāḥ |
eko dharmo guṇān sūte adharmastu tathā mataḥ || 72 ||

[aṣṭadala-rahasyam]

aṣṭarūpāṣṭapatreṣu dharmādīni vasanti ca |
anaṅgakusumādīni bodhyāni satataṃ budhaiḥ || 73 ||


69 | mitrakā iti | mitrastṛtīyaḥ sūryaḥ iti rāghavabhaṭṭaḥ | kaḥ prajāpatiḥ | kaḥ 
prajāpatiruddiṣṭa ityekākṣarakoṣaḥ |

p. 584) [ṣoḍaśadala-rahasyam]

ṣoḍaśacchadacakreṣu tithayaḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ |
sarvasaubhāgyadā nityaṃ kāmākarṣaṇapūrvikāḥ || 74 ||

trivṛtte candrasūryāgnitrirekhākāravartinaḥ || 75 ||

śivarekhā pūrvasandhyā brahmāṇī brahmarekhagā |
aṇimādyā brahmarekhāḥ krameṇa parikīrtitāḥ || 76 ||

catuḥṣaṣṭistathā koṭiḥ koṭikoṭivibhūtayaḥ |
yoginyo devadevyaśca cakramāśritya saṃsthitāḥ || 77 ||

asya prabhedāḥ koṇānāṃ dalānāntu prakīrtitāḥ |

[yantrasya parimāṇabhedāḥ]

trikoṇa(?)ñcaikatolaṃ vā caturaṅgulimānakam || 78 ||

tatraiva lekhayeccakraṃ caturasraṃ suśobhanam |
navahastaṃ dvihastaṃ vā tathaikahastameva vā || 79 ||


74-77 | trivṛtta ityādinā cakramāśritya saṃsthitā ityantenoktā āvaraṇaśaktayo'ṣṭādaśe 
pūjākrame granthakṛdbhirviśiṣya vakṣyante |

78-80 | atha yantrasya mānabhedānāha-trikoṇa(?)mityādinā yāmalasammatamityantena | atra 
trikoṇamiti pāṭhaḥ prāmādika iti pratibhāti | caturasramiti vakṣyamāṇena virodhādekatolaṃ veti 
vāśabdavaiyarthyāt svacchandabhairava-tantrarājādivirodhācca | tathāca tantrasāradhṛta-
svacchandabhairave -

kuryācca sthaṇḍile yantraṃ hastamātraṃ susundaram | 
ratnādiṣu tu nirmāṇe mānamicchāvaśād bhavet |

p. 585) likhed yantraṃ bhūpradeśe ratnādau tu nijecchayā |
mūlamuccārayan svastha iti yāmalasammatam || 80 ||

[binducakre mahākāmakalāyā avasthitiḥ]

trikoṇacakramadhye tu mahākāmakalāṃ likhet |
kathitaṃ paramaṃ cakraṃ triṣu lokeṣu durlabham || 81 ||

prārthayed yadi mokṣantu mokṣadvāramidaṃ mahat |
śrīkrame paramaṃ vastu kīrtitaṃ paramātmanā || 82 ||

[śrīcakroddhāre diṅniyamaḥ]

udaṅmukho yadā mantrī cakrarājaṃ prapūjayet |
udīcī dik tadā caiva pūrvāśaiva nigadyate |
īśānantu tathāgneyaṃ bhavedevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 83 ||


ekatolaṃ dvitolaṃ vā tritolaṃ vedatolakam |
ito'dhikaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā prāyaścittī bhaved dhruvam |
raktena rajasāpūrya śrīcakraṃ bhuvi pūjayet |
naśyanti sarvavighnāni prāpyate vāñchitaṃ phalam |

iti | tantrarāje trayastriṃśapaṭale'pi -

navahastaṃ trihastaṃ vā śrīcakramabhiṣecane |
kuryāt sthaṇḍilapūjāyāṃ hastamātre'tisundaram |
ratnādiṣu tu nirmāṇe mānamicchāvaśād bhavet | 
sadā suvihite pūjā śubhānyasmiṃstathā'nyathā |

iti | evañcaiteṣu vacaneṣu tritolaparimāṇadarśanādatrāpi tritolamityeva sādhīyān pāṭha iti 
mantavyam | mūlamiti mūlamantramuccārayan yantraṃ likhediti sambandhaḥ |

81 | trikoṇacakramadhya iti | madhyatrikoṇacakrasya madhye bindāvityarthaḥ

83 | athedānīṃ śrīcakroddhāre diṅniyamaḥ pratipādyate | tatra ca -

tatra prāṅmukha āsīnaścakrarājaṃ samālikhet |

iti jñānārṇavadaśamapaṭalavacanena,

p. 586) tataḥ prāṅmukha āsīnaścakroddhāraṃ samārabhet |

iti gandharvatantra-caturthapaṭalavacanena ca śrīcakranirmāṇe sādhakasya 
pūrvadiṅmukhatvamupadiṣṭam | tadidānīṃ pūrvāṃ diśameva niyamayati- udaṅmukha ityādinā | 
sādhaka udaṅmukhaḥ san cakranirmāṇāya pravṛttaścettarhi uttaradiśa eva pūrvadiktvam, 
īśānasyāgnikoṇatvam | anayā rītyā sarvatra diṅniyama ityuttaravacanebhyo'vagantavyam | etāni 
diṅniyamapratipādakavacanāni kvacicchrīcakroddhāraviṣaye kvacana vā 
śrīmattripurāpūjanaviṣaye'bhihitāni dṛśyante | yathā jñānārṇave daśamapaṭale -

uttarāśāmukho mantrī yadā cakraṃ samuddharet |
uttarāśā tadā devi pūrvāśaiva nigadyate ||

ityādi | evameva kaulāvalīnirṇaye'pi | vacanamidaṃ cakroddhāre diṅniyamapratipādakam | tathā 
gandharvatantre saptamapaṭale -

uttarābhimukho bhūtvā yadā devīṃ prapūjayet |
uttarāśā tadā devi pūrvāśaiva na saṃśayaḥ |
īśānamagnikoṇaṃ syādvāyukoṇaṃ tatheśakam | 
dakṣiṇāśā tadā jñeyā paścimāśā maheśvari |
rākṣasaṃ vāyukoṇaṃ syādāgneyaṃ rākṣasaṃ bhavet |

ityādivacanaiḥ pūjāyāṃ diṅniyamaḥ pratipāditaḥ | evañca niruktajñānārṇava-
gandharvatantrādivacanaparyālocanayā śrīcakroddhārapūjanayorubhayorapi niruktakrameṇa diṅniyamaḥ 
kartavya iti paryavasyati | parantu -
	tatra prāṅmukha āsīnaścakroddhāraṃ samārabhet iti prāgupadarśita-gandharvavacanena 
cakroddhāre prāṅmukhatvaniyamāt -

digvibhāgeṣu kauberī dik śivāprītidāyinī |
tasmāttanmukha āsīnaḥ pūjayettripurāṃ sadā ||

iti gandharvatantrasaptamapaṭalavacanena pūjane uttarāsyatvaniyamācca cakroddhāre 
pūrvadiṅmukhatvasya pūjane cottarāsyatvasya praśastataratvamiti bodhyam | naca 
digvibhāgeṣvityādivacanena pūjane pūrvāsyatvamātraṃ kalpyatāmiti vācyam,

yadāśābhimukho mantrī tripurāṃ paripūjayet |
devīpaścāttadā prācī pratīcī tripurāpuraḥ |

iti guptārṇavatantravacane yadāśābhimukha ityanena yatkiñciddiṅmātrābhidhānāt,

p. 587) paścimāśā yadā mantrī cakrarājaṃ prapūjayet |
pratīcī dik tadā caiva pūrvāśaiva na saṃśayaḥ |
vāyukoṇaṃ tadāgneyamīśānaṃ rākṣasaṃ bhavet || 84 ||

dakṣiṇāśāmukho mantrī yadā cakraṃ prapūjayet |
pūrvāśaiva tadā sā dig rakṣaḥkoṇantu vahnivat || 85 ||

āgneyantu tadeśānaṃ vāyustu nir-ṛtirbhavet |
bṛhacchrīkramatantrasya matametat prakīrtitam || 86 ||

purandaramukho mantrī pūjayettripurāṃ yadā |
devīpaścāttadā prācī pratīcī tripurāpuraḥ |
guptārṇavamatenāpi digvibhāgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 87 ||

[śrīcakrasthāpanādhāraḥ]

atha siṃhāsane mantrī śrīcakraṃ purato nyaset || 88 ||

[sīsakādiṣu cakrasthāpananiṣedhaḥ]

sīsake kāṃsyapātre vā raṅge vā sādhakottamaḥ |
phalakāyāṃ paṭe bhittau sathāpayenna kadācana || 89 ||

sthāpitaṃ yadi lobhena mohādajñānato'pi vā |
kulaṃ vittamapatyañca nirmūlayati sarvadā || 90 ||


udaṅmukho yadā mantrī cakrarājaṃ prapūjayet |

ityādimūloktavacaneṣu pūjane uttarāsyatvādervyaktamabhidhānācca virodhāpatteḥ | tasmāt 
cakroddhāre prāṅmukhatvasya pūjane cottarāsyatvasya praśastataratvaṃ diśāmanyāsāṃ 
praśastatvamiti niṣkarṣaḥ |

p. 588) [pīṭhapūjā]

pīṭhapūjāṃ tataḥ kuryāduparyuparibhāvataḥ |
maṇḍūkaṃ pūjayedādau rudraṃ kālāgnirūpakam || 91 ||

ādhāraśaktiṃ kūrmañca tathā'nantaṃ varāhakam |
pṛthvīṃ sudhāsamudrañca ratnadvīpamataḥ param || 92 ||

svarṇaśailaṃ ratnabhūmiṃ nandanodyānameva ca |
nānāvṛkṣamahodyānaṃ tathaiva kalpavāṭikām || 93 ||

santānavāṭikāñcaiva haricandanavāṭikām |
mandāravāṭikāṃ pārijātavāṭīṃ samarcayet || 94 ||

kadambavanavāṭīñca ratnaprākārakān yathā |
puṣparāgaṃ padmarāgaṃ gomedaṃ vajramapyatha || 95 ||

vaidūryamindranīlañca muktāṃ marakataṃ tathā |
vidrumañceti navakān ratnaprākārasaṃyutān || 96 ||

māṇikyamaṇḍapañcaiva sahasrastambhamaṇḍapam |
amṛtavāpikāmevamānandavāpikāṃ tataḥ || 97 ||

vimarṣavāpikāṃ bālātapodgāramataḥ param |
candrikodgārakaṃ mahāśṛṅgāraparikhāmapi || 98 ||

mahāpadmāṭavīṃ kalpavṛkṣaṃ saṃpūjayennaraḥ |
tataścintāmaṇigṛhaṃ ratnanirmitavedikām || 99 ||


91 | pīṭhapūjāmāha - pīṭhapūjāmityādinā samarcayediti prakāśāntena |

p. 589)  pūrvāmnāyamayaṃ pūrvaṃ dvāraṃ pūrve samarcayet |
dakṣiṇe paścime saumye tattadāmnāyamapyatha || 100 ||

ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ śvetacchatramūrddhve prapūjayet |
dharmaṃ jñānañca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ cāgnito yajet || 101 ||

a- pūrvān pūjayedetān dikṣu pūrvāditaḥ kramāt |
ātmāntarātmaparamajñānātmānaḥ krameṇa tu || 102 ||

cakramadhyasthapadmañca kandaṃ vasudalaṃ tataḥ |
nālamūrddhvacchadādhaśca tattvarūpāntu karṇikām || 103 ||

maṇḍalaṃ vahnisūryendo rajaḥ sattvaṃ tamo'pi ca |
jñānamāyākalāvidyātattvānyatra prapūjayet || 104 ||

gaṇeśaṃ vaṭukaṃ mantrī savye dakṣe prapūjayet |
nāmādivarṇabījāni caturasrāntare punaḥ || 105 ||

īśāgnyantañca mitrīśanāthamuḍḍīśanāthakam |
jālandharāhvayaṃ pīṭhaṃ pīṭhaṃ pūrṇagiriṃ tataḥ || 106 ||

tryasre saṃpūjayenmantrī krameṇānena tatparam |
pūrvādikramayogeṇa phalake āsanasya tu || 107 ||


100 | pūrveti | pūrvadvāre pūrvāmnāyam, evaṃ dakṣiṇapaścimottareṣu 
dakṣiṇapaścimottarāmnāyān krameṇa pūjayedityarthaḥ | etena pūrvāmnāyamayapūrvadvārāya 
namaḥ dakṣiṇāmnāyamayadakṣiṇadvārāya nama ityādirūpaḥ prayoga ityāpatati | tantrasāre tu 
pūrvāmnāyapūrvadvārāya nama ityādirūpaḥ prayoga uktaḥ |

102 | apūrvāniti | adharmamajñānamavairāgyamanaiśvaryañcetyarthaḥ |

105 | caturasreti | śrīcakracaturasrāntare īśānādyagnikoṇāntaṃ krameṇa 
jālandharādipīṭhacatuṣṭayaṃ pūjayedityarthaḥ |

p. 590) pṛthivyāśca patiñcaiva brahmāṇamamṛteśvaram |
viṣṇutejo'dhipaṃ rudraṃ vāryadhīśaṃ tatheśvaram || 108 ||

madhye prāsādamuccārya viyatpadamudīrayet |
tato'dhipataye procya pañcavaktrāya yojayet || 109 ||

sadāśivāya ca mahāpretapadmāsanāya hṛt |
yajedāsanamantreṇa sarvāsanamathāpi vā || 110 ||

pretabījaṃ samuccārya sadāśivamahāpadam |
pretapadmāsanāyeti hṛnmanurmanurīritaḥ || 111 ||

pūrvoktenaiva mantreṇa tasyopari ṣaḍāsanam || 112 ||

tasyopari yajenmantrī prasūnatulikāmapi |
kāmeśvaraṃ tathā kāmeśvarīmūrtiṃ samarcayet || 113 ||

iti śrīpūrṇānandaparamahaṃsaviracite śrītattvacintāmaṇau saptadaśaḥ prakāśaḥ || 17 ||


109-110 | prāsādeti | hauṃ viyadadhipataye sadāśivāya mahāpretapadmāsanāya nama iti 
prayogaḥ |

111 | preteti | pretabījaṃ hasauriti | tathā ca hasauḥ sadāśivamahāpretapadmāsanāya nama iti 
prayogaḥ |



aṣṭādaśaḥ prakāśaḥ

[sāmānyārghyasthāpanādivisarjanāntasaparyākramaḥ]

[sāmānyārghyasthāpanam (tatra maṇḍalam)]

vidhāya vāmabhāge tu trikoṇavṛttabhūpuram |
athavā caturasrantu jalena parikalpayet || 1 ||

caturasrantu tat kuryāttrikoṇadvayasaṃyutam |

[śaṅkhādhārasthāpanam]

ādhāraṃ tatra saṃsthāpya pūjayed vahnimaṇḍalam || 2 ||

ādhāreṇa vinā pūjā niṣphalā parikīrtitā |

[ādhārabhedāḥ]

ādhāraṃ vividhañcaiva ṣaṭpadaṃ vā catuṣpadam || 3 ||


1 | evaṃ pūrvaprakāśe cakrasaṅketamabhidhāya pūjāsaṅketamabhidadhānaḥ prathamaṃ 
sāmānyārghyasthāpanaparipāṭīmāha-vidhāyetyādinā | trikoṇeti | ādau trikoṇaṃ tadbahirvṛttaṃ 
tadbahirbhapuraṃ caturasramityarthaḥ | atra trikoṇamadhomukhaṃ kāryaṃ śaktiviṣayatvāt | taduktaṃ 
tārārahasyadhṛtarudrayāmale -

yatra yatra mahāvidyā śaktividyā upāsitā |
tatra tatra trikoṇañca adhomukhamudīritam |
devatrikoṇe kartavye ūrddhvaṃ tat parikīrtitam |

iti | kalpāntaramāha - athaveti | jalena caturasraṃ parikalpya tadantastrikoṇadvayaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ |

p. 592) athavā vartulākāraṃ trikoṇaṃ vā samācaret |
suvarṇarūpyaracitaṃ tripadaṃ vā'pi pūjayet || 4 ||

[mantropadeśaḥ]

bījoktau tadanuktau vā māyāśrīpūrvamarcanam |
mantrānuktau nāmamantraṃ sarvatraivopayojayet || 5 ||

[pādukāmantraprayogaḥ]

śrīpādukākhyamantrantu dadyādante yathākramam || 6 ||

[vahnerdaśakalāḥ]

dhūmrā ca nīlavarṇā ca kapilā visphuliṅginī |
jvālā haimavatī kavyavāhinī havyavāhinī |
raudrī saṅkarṣiṇī vahneretāḥ kalāḥ parā yathā || 7 ||


5 | tripadaṃ vā'pi pūjayedityuktaṃ tatpūjāmantraṃ nirdiśati - bījoktāviti | māyābījaṃ hrīṃ, 
śrībījaṃ śrīṃ | tathā ca hrīṃ śrīṃ maṃ vahnimaṇḍalāya daśakalātmane 
arghyapātrādhārāya nama iti prayogaḥ | tantrasārakārastu niruktamāyābījāt pūrvaṃ 
vāgbhavabījasyāpi yojanayā tritārīprayogamupadideśa | tripurārcanarahasye tu mūlamantrānantaraṃ 
śrīmahātripurasundarīsāmānyārghyādhāramaṇḍalāya nama iti maṇḍalaṃ saṃpūjya oṃ 
daśakalātmane'gnimaṇḍalāya śrīmahātripurasundarīsāmānyārghyādhārāya nama 
ityarghyādhārapūjākrama uktaḥ, tatra māyā-śrībījayorullekho na dṛśyate | mantrānuktāviti | 
nāmamantraṃ tattaddevatānāmarūpaṃ mantramityarthaḥ | atra viśiṣyānuktāvapi nāmnaḥ 
praṇavapūrvakatvamavagantavyam | vyaktamāha durgāpūjātattvadhṛtayogiyājñavalkya-
brahmapurāṇayoḥ -

dhyātvā praṇavapūrvantu daivatantu samāhitaḥ |
namaskāreṇa puṣpādi vinyasettu pṛthak pṛthak ||

tathā-

oṃkārādisamāyuktaṃ namaskārāntakīrtitam |
svanāma sarvadevānāṃ mantra ityabhidhīyate || iti |

6 | śrīpāduketi | prathamaṃ devatānāma tataḥ śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmi nama iti 
pādukāmantraḥ |

7 | evamādhāraṃ tatretyādinā vahnimaṇḍalapūjāmupadiśya tatraiva vahnikalārcāmupadiśati - 
dhūmrā cetyādinā | yoginīhṛdayaṭīkādhṛtasvacchandasaṃgrahe'pi -

p. 593) [tatra kalpāntaram]

dhūmrārciruṣmā jvalinī jvālinī visphuliṅginī |
suśrīḥ surūpā kapilā havyakavyavahe tathā |
ābhirvā pūjayed vahniṃ yādikṣāntādibhiḥ kramāt || 8 ||


ṣaṭkoṇāntastrikoṇe tu vahnibimbaṃ kalāyutam |
āvāhyā''dhārato devi pūjayenmantravittamaḥ ||

ityuktam | yadyapyatra vacane ṣaṭkoṇāntastrikoṇa ityanena viśeṣārghyasthāpana eva 
vahnyādikalānāmarcanamupadiṣṭaṃ na tu sāmānyārghyasthāpanāvasare, tripurārcanarahasya-
kaulāvalīnirṇaya-tantrasārādiṣvapi tathaiva dṛśyate, tathā'pi mūle tadvaiparītyena 
sāmānyārghyasthāpana eva vahnyādikalāpūjanopadeśād viśeṣārghye 
tadanabhidhānāccā'gamāntarasammato'yaṃ kramo mūlakṛdbhiḥ paramahaṃsapādaiḥ parigṛhīta iti 
pratibhāti | dhūmreti | dhūmrādisaṅkarṣiṇyantā vahnerdaśa kalā ityarthaḥ |

8 | kalpāntaramāha-dhūmrārciriti | tathācaitatkalpe dhūmrārcirādyāḥ kavyavahāntā daśa kalā 
ityarthaḥ | atra tantrasārādiṣu nāmnāṃ kramabhedaḥ svarūpabhedaśca dṛśyete | ābhirveti | 
vāśabdaḥ kalpāntarabodhakaḥ | ābhirdhūmrādibhiḥ dhūmrārcirādibhirveti sambandhaḥ | ābhiriti 
viśeṣayati-yādikṣāntādibhiriti | yakārādikṣakārāntadaśavarṇānāmekaikamādau niyojya 
namo'ntena pūjayedityarthaḥ | atra yakārādivarṇāḥ sabindukā grāhyāḥ | tathā ca 
kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhollāse dhūmrārcirityādyabhidhāya -

yādisendukṣakārāntā abjādyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
ṅe-yutā vai namo'ntāśca kalā vahneritīritāḥ ||

ityanenānusvārayuktatvaṃ yakārādīnāmuktam, abjādyā ityanena śrībījapūrvakatvamapyuktam, 
etenaitanmate śrīṃ yaṃ dhūmrāyai nama iti prayogakramaḥ paryavasyati | mūlakṛnmate tu bījoktau 
tadanuktau vā māyāśrīpūrvamarcanam iti prāguktavacanānusāreṇa hrīṃ śrīṃ yaṃ dhūmrāyai 
nama ityādirūpaḥ prayoga iti pratibhāti | tantrasārakṛtā tu yādīnāṃ pūrvaṃ tritārīprayoga 
uktaḥ | yoginīhṛdayaṭīkādhṛtasvacchandasaṃgrahe'pi -

p. 594) [kalānāmāsāṃ svarūpam]

taruṇāruṇasaṅkāśā varābhayakarā imāḥ |

[tatra sakala-vahnimaṇḍalārcākramaḥ]

udarārṇa vāgbhavaṃ vā dattvā tanmaṇḍalaṃ yajet || 9 ||


yādikṣāntātmavarṇāni kalā vahnermaheśvari |
etāḥ kalāḥ samabhyarcya svasvavarṇairyathāvidhi || 
svabījādyāścaturthyantā nāmamadhyā namo'ntikāḥ |
proktā vahnikalā mantranāmāni śṛṇu sāmpratam ||
dhūmrārcirnīlaraktā ca kapilā visphuliṅginī |
jvālāmālinyarciṣmatī tadūrdhvaṃ havyavāhinī |
aṣṭamī kavyavāhā ca raudrī saṃvāhinī tathā || 

iti vacane svabījādyā ityanena śrībījādyaṃ vā māyāśrībījādyaṃ vā vāgbhava-
māyāśrībījarūpatritārīpūrvakatvaṃ vā'bhipretamiti saṃkṣepaḥ |

9 | niruktānāṃ vahnikalānāṃ svarūpamāha - taruṇeti | navoditasūryasavarṇāḥ | 
varābhayakarāḥ ekena kareṇa varamapareṇa cābhayaṃ dadhatya ityarthaḥ | pūrvaṃ pūjayed 
vahnimaṇḍalam ityuktam, tadvahnimaṇḍalapūjākramamupadiśati-udarārṇamityādinā | udarārṇaṃ 
maṃ vāgbhavaṃ aiṃ iti bījaṃ vā dattvā tanmaṇḍalaṃ vahnimaṇḍalaṃ yajedityarthaḥ | evañca 
niruktayorbījayoranyataraduccārya vahnimaṇḍalāya daśakalātmane arghyapātrādhārāya namaḥ 
ityanena pūjayediti bodhyam | vyaktamāha kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhapaṭale -

pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā saṃpūjya tena vartmanā |
ādau senduṃ mahākālaṃ vahnimaṇḍalaṃ ṅeyutam |
hṛdantaṃ mantramuccārya ādhāre pūjayettataḥ |

iti | mahākālaṃ makāram | tatrāpi jñānārṇave caturdaśapaṭale -

vahniṃ tatra vicintyātha pūjayed yantrikāmayam |

	ityabhidhānād arghyapātrādhārāya nama ityādhārapātrapadollekhaḥ kāryaḥ | 
tripurārcanarahasyādiprāmāṇikapūjāprayogeṣvapi tathaiva dṛśyate |

p. 595) [sakala-sūryamaṇḍalārcā]

pātraṃ nidhāya tatraiva kalābhiḥ sūryamarcayet |

[dvādaśa saurakalāḥ]

tapinī tāpinī cānyā vibudhā bodhinī tathā || 10 ||

kalinī ṣoḍaśī caiva vāruṇyākarṣiṇī tathā |
māyā viśvāvatī hema(a)*prabhā etāḥ kalā raveḥ || 11 ||

[tatra prakārāntaram]

tapinī tāpinī dhūmrā marīcirjvālinī ruciḥ |
suṣumṇā bhogadā viśvā bodhinī dhāriṇī kṣamā |
yajedābhiḥ kalābhirvā tattanmantreṇa sādhakaḥ || 12 ||


10-11 | atha sakala-sūryamaṇḍalapūjanamupadiśati-pātramityādinā | tatraiveti 
prāguktaṣaṭpadādyanyatamādhāre ityarthaḥ | vyaktamāha kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhollāse -

eṣāmanyatamaṃ pātraṃ sthāpayettripadopari |

iti | atrāpi pūrvavanmaṇḍalamālikhya saṃpūjya ca sūryamaṇḍalamarcayediti jñeyam | 
tathācoktaṃ tatraiva viśeṣārghyaprakaraṇe -

pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā pūjayet pūrvavartmanā |
sūryasya maṇḍalaṃ tatra cintayitvā'tha pūjayet ||

iti | dvādaśa sūryakalā nirūpayati-tapinītyādinā | tāpinyādyāḥ prabhāntā raverdvādaśa 
kalāḥ |

12 | kalpāntaramāha-tapinītyādinā | kalpe'smiṃstapinyādyāḥ kṣamāntā dvādaśa

p. 596) [kalārcākramaḥ]

kādi-ṭhāntān binduyutān bhādi-ḍāntān sabindukān |
anyo'nyakramayogeṇa kalādau yojayet kramāt || 13 ||

[kalānāṃ svarūpam]

varābhayakarāḥ sarvā atasīpuṣpasodarāḥ | 

[sauramaṇḍalārcākramaḥ]

lalāṭaṃ kāmabījaṃ vā saṃyojya maṇḍalaṃ raveḥ || 14 ||


sūryakalāḥ | kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhaprakāśe'yameva kramaḥ parigṛhīto dṛśyate | yajediti 
tapinyādiprābhāntābhiḥ tapinyādikṣamāntābhirvā yajedityarthaḥ | tattanmantreṇeti 
pūrvavanmāyā-śrībījādipūrvakamantreṇetyarthaḥ |

13 | tatrā'dau varṇayojanamāha-kādītyādinā | kādi-ṭhāntān kakārādiṭhakārāntān tathā 
bhādi-ḍāntān bhakārādi-ḍakārāntān vilomaparipaṭhitān binduyuktān niruktānāṃ 
dvādaśakalānāmādau yojayedityarthaḥ | evañca prāguktakrameṇa bījamuccārya kaṃ bhaṃ 
tapinyai namaḥ, khaṃ maṃ tāpinyai nama ityādirūpaḥ prayogakramaḥ paryavasyati | vyaktamāha 
yoginīhṛdayaṭīkādhṛtasvacchandasaṃgrahe -

astraprakṣālitaṃ śaṅkhaṃ sthāpayitvā tathopari |
hṛdayāt sūryabimbañca samāvāhya svamantrataḥ ||
kādi-ṭhāntaṃ bhādi-ḍāntaṃ varṇajātaṃ kramotkramāt | 
dvayaṃ dvayaṃ prayoktavyaṃ kalāśca tadanantaram ||

iti | atrāpi kaulāvalīnirṇaya-yoginīhṛdayādiṣu pūrvavat kalānāṃ nāmabhedaḥ svarūpabhedaśca 
dṛśyete |

14 | kalānāmāsāṃ svarūpamāha-varābhayeti | sarvā eva ekena hastena varamapareṇa 
cābhayaṃ dadhatyaḥ | atasīpuṣpasodarāḥ atasīpuṣpasavarṇāḥ | pūrvaṃ kalābhiḥ 
sūryamarcayedityuktam, tatkramamupadiśati-lālāṭamiti | lālāṭam aṃ, kāmabījaṃ klīṃ | 
bījayoretayoranyataramādau saṃyojya ravermaṇḍalaṃ pūjayedityarthaḥ | pūjākramamāha 
kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhapaṭale -

p. 597) [arghyapātrāpūraṇam]

tīrthodakena śuddhena pūrayenmūlamuccaran |

[somakalārcopadeśaḥ]

praṇavena gandhapuṣpe dattvā somakalārcanam || 15 ||

[ṣoḍaśa somakalāḥ]

amṛtā mānasī tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭiḥ prītī ratistathā |
śrīśca hrīśca svadhā rātrirjyotsnā haimavatī tathā || 16 ||

chāyā'nyā pūrṇimā vidyā amāvāsyā ca ṣoḍaśī | 
ābhirvā pūjayet somaṃ parābhirvā yathākramāt || 17 ||


maṇḍalaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā pūjayet pūrvavartmanā | 
sūryasya maṇḍalaṃ tatra cintayitvā'tha pūjayet |
svarādyaṃ bindusahitamarkamaṇḍalaṃ ṅeyutam | 
namo'ntaṃ mantramuccārya pūjayitvā'rghyapātrakam | 
kalāḥ saṃpūjayettatra bhānordvādaśasaṃjñakāḥ |

iti | evañca lalāṭakāmabījayoranyataramuccārya aṃ arkamaṇḍalāya dvādaśakalātmane 
arghyapātrāya nama iti prayogaḥ |

15 | athārghyapātrasyā'pūraṇādikamāha-tīrthetyādinā | pūrayet arghyapātramiti śeṣaḥ | 
mūlamuccarannityantarbhūtaṇyarthaḥ uccārayannityarthaḥ | tatastatra praṇavena gandhapuṣpādikaṃ 
nikṣipya somakalārcanaṃ somamaṇḍalārcanaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ | taduktaṃ svacchandasaṃgrahe -

elālavaṅgakakkolajātīphalasusaṃyutān |
sugandhikusumairdravyaiḥ saha tasmiñ jale kṣipet | 
navayoniṃ samālikhya pūrvavajjalamadhyataḥ |
niśuddhitaḥ samāvāhya candramaṇḍalamarcayet | iti |

16-17 | atha ṣoḍaśa somakalāstatpūjanañcopadiśati-amṛtetyādinā | amṛtādyā amāvāsyāntāḥ 
ṣoḍaśa kalāḥ | ābhirniruktābhiḥ kalābhiḥ | kalpāntaramavatārayati-parābhirveti, 
vakṣyamāṇābhirvetyarthaḥ |

p. 598) [tatra prakārāntaram]

amṛtā mānadā pūṣā tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭī ratidhṛtiḥ |
śaśinī candrikā kāntirjyotsnā śrīḥ prītiraṅgadā || 18 ||

pūrṇā pūrṇāmṛtā kāmadāyinyaḥ svaragāḥ kalāḥ |

[somakalānāṃ svarūpam]

taruṇāruṇasaṅkāśā varābhayakarāḥ smṛtāḥ || 19 ||

[kalārcākramaḥ]

paścimādikrameṇāpi kalāḥ sarvāḥ prapūjayet |

[somamaṇḍalārcā]

śaktyarṇaṃ dakṣakarṇaṃ vā niyojya maṇḍalaṃ vidhoḥ || 20 ||

[ānandabhairavayoḥ pūjanam]

ānandabhairavaṃ devamānandabhairavīmapi |


18-20 | tā evā'ha-amṛteti | kalpe'smin amṛtādyāḥ pūrṇāmṛtāntāḥ kāmadāyinyaḥ 
vāñchitārthapradāḥ ṣoḍaśa kalāḥ, etāḥ pūjayedityarthaḥ | ayameva kalpaḥ 
samānānupūrvīkavacanena pratipāditaḥ kaulāvalīnirṇaye | svaragāḥ akārādi-ṣoḍaśa-svaragatāḥ 
kalā ityarthaḥ | atra svarajāḥ kalā ityuktaṃ kaulāvalīnirṇaye | 
evañcākārādiṣoḍaśasvarāṇāmekaikamādau niyojya ekaikamarcayediti bodhyam | vyaktamāha 
yoginīhṛdayaṭīkādhṛtasvacchandasaṃgrahe -

ādikādyantikāḥ saumyāḥ kalāḥ ṣoḍaśa kīrtitāḥ |

iti | bījayogaśca pūrvavat | evañca pūrvavad bījamuccārya aṃ amṛtāyai namaḥ | āṃ 
mānadāyai nama ityādirūpaḥ prayogakramaḥ paryavasyati | kalānāmāsāṃ svarūpamāha - 
taruṇeti | spaṣṭam | paścimādikrameṇa vṛttākāreṇa pūjayet | tantrasāre'pyevameva |

	atha candramaṇḍalapūjākramamāha-śaktyarṇamiti | śaktyarṇaṃ sauḥ iti, dakṣakarṇam uṃ 
| bījayogaḥ pūrvavat | evañca niruktabījamuccārya uṃ somamaṇḍalāya ṣoḍaśakalātmane 
arghyapātrāmṛtāya nama iti prayogaḥ |

21-22 | athā'nandabhairavānandabhairavyostatra pūjāmupadiśati - ānandetyādinā |

p. 599) [ānandabhairavamantraḥ]

śivasomau mātṛkāntaṃ kālabhūvaruṇānalam || 21 ||

vāmakarṇasamāyuktaṃ vāyumardhendumaṇḍitam |
ānandabhairavāyeti śikhāmantraṃ tato vadet || 22 ||

ānandabhairavaḥ soma(?) ānandabhairavīṃ śṛṇu |

[ānandabhairavīmantraḥ]

pūrvavad varṇamālikhya candraṃ hitvādimaṃ nayet || 23 ||

karṇe vāmākṣi saṃyojya sudhādevyai manuṃ dṛśoḥ |

[arghye japakramaḥ]

asyopari japenmūlaṃ tāraṃ vā saptadhā budhaḥ || 24 ||


ādāvānandabhairavaṃ tata ānandabhairavīṃ, prapūjayediti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ | 
tatrā'nandabhairavamantramāha-śivetyādinā | śivo hakāraḥ, soma�scandrabījaṃ sakāraḥ, 
mātṛkāntaṃ kṣakāraḥ, kālo makāraḥ, bhūḥ pṛthvībījaṃ lakāraḥ, varuṇabījaṃ vakāraḥ, 
analaṃ vahnibījaṃ rakāraḥ, vāyubījaṃ yakāraḥ, tacca vāyubījaṃ vāmakarṇena ṣaṣṭhasvareṇa 
samāyuktaṃ tathā ardhendunā anusvāreṇa maṇḍitam | evañca ha sa kṣa ma la va ra yūṃ iti 
prāptam | tata ānandabhairavāyeti | tataśca śikhāmantraṃ vaṣaṭ iti | eva~ca ha sa kṣa ma la 
va ra yūṃ ānandabhairavāya vaṣaḍityānandabhairavamantraḥ paryavasyati |

23-25 | ānandabhairavamantramupasaṃharati-ānandeti | atra soma iti prāmādikaḥ pāṭhaḥ, prokta 
ityeva samīcīna iti pratibhāti | athā'nandabhairavīmantramāha-ānandabhairavīmityādinā | 
pūrvavadānandabhairavakrameṇa hakārādivarṇāṣṭakamālikhyetyarthaḥ | candraṃ hitvā 
dvitīyavarṇaṃ sakāraṃ ullaṅghya ādimaṃ hakāraṃ nayet dvitīyākṣarāt sakārāt parataḥ 
prayuñjītetyarthaḥ, anena ha sa ityatra sa ha iti prāptam | tathā karṇe niruktamantrastha 
vāmakarṇasthāne vāmākṣi caturthasvaraṃ saṃyojya | anena yūṃ ityatra yīṃ iti prāptam | 
tataḥ sudhādevyai iti | tataśca dṛśormanuṃ vauṣaḍiti padaṃ yojayediti śeṣaḥ | evañca ha sa 
kṣa ma la va ra yīṃ

p. 600) [upasaṃhāraḥ]

sāmānyārghyamidaṃ proktaṃ

[viśeṣārghyanirūpaṇapratijñā]

				viśeṣārghyamathocyate || 25 ||

[śrīpātrakalpanā]

gṛhītvājñāṃ mahādevyāḥ śrīpātraṃ parikalpayet |

[maṇḍalanirmāṇam]

ātmaśrīcakrayormadhye maṇḍalaṃ racayed yathā || 26 ||

īkāreṇānvitaṃ tryasraṃ ṣaḍasraṃ vṛttabhūpure |
tryasrabāhye'thavā vṛttaṃ tadabhyukṣettadambunā || 27 ||


sudhādevyai vauṣaḍityānandabhairavīmantraḥ paryavasyati | asyeti | asya sāmānyārghyasyopari 
mūlamantraṃ tāraṃ praṇavaṃ vā saptadhā japedityarthaḥ | sāmānyārghyamupasaṃharati-
sāmānyeti |
	evaṃ sāmānyārghyaparipāṭīmabhidhāya viśeṣārghyasthāpanaprakriyāmabhidhatte - 
viśeṣārghyamityādinā |

26-27 | atha pātrasthāpanayogyamaṇḍalanirmāṇasthānamupadiśati-ātmeti | ātmaśrīcakrayormadhye 
ātmanaḥ śrīcakrarūpāyā devatāyāśca madhyavartinyāṃ bhūmau maṇḍalaṃ 
vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa racayedityarthaḥ | taduktaṃ kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhapaṭale -

ātmadevīdvayormadhye bhūmau cakraṃ likhettataḥ |

iti | maṇḍalaracanāparipāṭīmāha - īkāreṇeti | īkāreṇānvitamīkāragarbhaṃ trikoṇam | 
kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhapaṭale'pi -

madhye māyāṃ samālikhya prokṣayettadanantaram |

iti | tatrāpi māyāpadena caturthasvara eva bodhyaḥ | evañca caturthasvaragarbhaṃ trikoṇaṃ 
tadbahiḥ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tadbahirvṛttaṃ tadbahiścaturasrarūpaṃ bhūpuram ityeko maṇḍalaracanākalpaḥ | 
kalpāntaramāha-tryasreti | athavā prāguktatryasrabāhye vṛttaṃ vidadhīta | evañcātra kalpe 
ādāvīkāragarbhaṃ trikoṇaṃ tadbahirvṛttaṃ tadbahiḥṣaṭkoṇaṃ tadbahiścaturasramiti viśeṣaḥ | 
evaṃ maṇḍalaracanāyāṃ kalpadvayamanyatrāpi dṛśyate | yathā kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhapaṭale-

p. 601) [tatra pīṭhacatuṣṭayādyarcā]

caturasre pūrṇaśailamuḍḍīyānābhidhaṃ param |
jālandharaṃ kāmarūpaṃ ṣaṭkoṇeṣu ṣaḍāsanam || 28 ||

[ṣaṭkoṇārcāyāṃ vikalpaḥ]

ṣaḍaṅgaṃ pūjayed vā'pi tattanmantramudīrayan |

[trikoṇādyarcā]

trikoṇañca tribhiḥ kūṭairmadhya ādhāraśaktikām || 29 ||


ātmadevīdvayormadhye bhūmau cakraṃ likhettataḥ |
trikoṇañcaiva ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vṛttaṃ bhūgṛhasaṃyutam ||

iti | vacanamidaṃ pūrvakalpapratipādakam | tathā jñānārṇave caturdaśapaṭale-

trikoṇavṛttaṣaṭkoṇaṃ caturasrantu maṇḍalam |

iti | vacanamidamuttarakalpapratipādakam | evañca tantrabhedena kalpabhedadarśanāt 
svasvagurusaṃpradāyānusāreṇaikataraḥ pakṣaḥ samāśrayaṇīya iti dik | taditi | tanmaṇḍalaṃ 
tadambunā sāmānyārghyajalenābhyukṣedityarthaḥ | vyaktamuktaṃ kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhapaṭale-

madhye māyāṃ samālikhya prokṣayettadanantaram |
astrāntamūlamantreṇa sāmānyārghyajalena ca || 

iti |

28 | atha prāguktamaṇḍale caturasrādikrameṇa pūjanamupadiśati-caturasra ityādinā | caturasre 
pūrṇaśailādipīṭhacatuṣṭayamarcayet | pūjanañca pūrvādikrameṇa, taduktaṃ kaulāvalīnirṇaye 
ṣaṣṭhapaṭale -

caturasre pūrṇaśailamuḍḍīyānaṃ jalandharam | 
pūjayet kāmarūpañca pūrvādikramayogataḥ |

iti | atha niruktacaturasrāntaḥṣaṭkoṇe pūjanamāha -ṣaḍiti | ṣaṭkoṇeṣu ṣaḍāsanaṃ pūjayediti 
pareṇa sambandhaḥ | ṣaḍāsanāni ca amṛtārṇavāsana-
