potāmbujāsanātmāsanacakrāsanasarvamantrāsanasādhyasiddhāsanāni tripurārcanarahasye 
vyaktamuktāni veditavyāni | prayogastu hrīṃ śrīmiti dvitārīṃ aiṃ hrīṃ śrīmiti tritārīṃ voccārya 
amṛtārṇavāsanāya namaḥ ityādirūpaḥ |

29 | kalpāntaramāha - ṣaḍaṅgamiti | vyaktamāha jñānārṇave caturdaśapaṭale -

p. 602) trikoṇavṛttaṣaṭkoṇamādhāraṃ pūrvameva vā |

[maṇḍale'rghyādhārasthāpanakramaḥ]

hṛdā prokṣitamādhāraṃ sthāpayenmaṇḍalopari || 30 ||

[ādhārārcā]

bālātribījaṃ tripurasundaryarghyāsanaṃ vadet |
sādhayāmi namo'ntena manunādhāramarcayet || 31 ||

prapūjya pūrvavad vahneḥ kalāntamantramuccaran | 
syād vahnimaṇḍalāyeti rūpaṃ daśakalātmane || 32 ||

arghyapātrāsanāyeti hṛdādhāre samarcayet | 
ṣaṭkoṇeṣu ṣaḍaṅgāni vyastairmadhye śivāṃ manoḥ || 33 ||


dvirāvṛttyā ṣaḍaṅgāni ṣaṭkoṇeṣu prapūjayet |

iti | evañcāgnyādikrameṇa prathamaṃ vāgbhavakūṭaṃ tataḥ kāmarājakūṭaṃ tataḥ 
śaktikūṭaṃ punarapi niruktavāgbhavādikūṭatrayamuccārya pūjayedityarthaḥ | tantrasārakārairapi 
agnyādikrameṇa ṣaṭkoṇe mūlavidyayā dvirāvṛttyā ṣaḍaṅgāni pūjayedityuktam | 
tripurārcanarahasye'pyevameva | tattanmantramudīrayannityasyāpyayamevābhiprāyo 
jñānārṇavatantrasārādisaṃvādenāvagamyate | antastrikoṇapūjanamāha-trikoṇeti | tribhiḥ 
kūṭairvyastakūṭatrayeṇetyarthaḥ | jñānārṇave caturdaśapaṭale'pi -

vyastakūṭairyajettatra trikoṇe parameśvari |

ityuktam | madhya iti | trikoṇamadhye ādhāraśaktiṃ pūjayedityarthaḥ |

30 | hṛdā nama ityanena |

31 | athārghyapātrādhārārcāmantramāha - bāleti | tathāca aiṃ klīṃ sauḥ 
tripurasundaryarghyāsanaṃ sādhayāmi nama iti mantraḥ paryavasyati |

32-33 | pūrvavaditi | sāmānyārghyoktakrameṇa vahnerdaśakalāḥ saṃpūjya vahnimaṇḍalāya 
daśakalātmane arghyapātrāsanāya nama ityādhāre samarcayedityarthaḥ |

p. 603) [arghyapātranirṇayaḥ]

pātraṃ kāñcanakācarūpyajanitaṃ muktākapālodbhavaṃ
viśvāmitramayañca kāmadamidaṃ haimaṃ priyaṃ sphāṭikaṃ |
tāmraṃ prītidamaṣṭasiddhijanakaṃ śrīnārikelodbhavaṃ
kāpālaṃ sphuṭamantrasiddhijanakaṃ muktipradaṃ mauktikam || 34 ||

śaṅkham alāvupātrañca tathā puṇyatarūdbhavam | 
mṛnmayaṃ tāmrajaṃ vā'pi tathā lohasamudbhavam || 35 ||

purā vai nirmitaṃ pātraṃ viśvāmitreṇa dhīmatā | 
viśvāmitrakapālañca sarvapātrottamottamam || 36 ||

brahmahatyādipāpāni tathā viśvāsaghātakam |
dṛṣṭvā pātraṃ nārikelaṃ sarvapāpaṃ praṇaśyati || 37 ||

kalpakoṭipradānāni tathā hemaśatāni ca | 
yo dadāti munīndrāya rāhugraste niśākare || 38 ||

tatphalāt koṭiguṇitaṃ nārikelārghyapātrataḥ |
abhāve tasya pātrasya pātramanyat prakalpayet || 39 ||

dṛṣṭvārghyapātraṃ paramaṃ brahmādyā devatāḥ sadā |
nṛtyanti sarvayoginyaḥ prītyā siddhiṃ dadātyalam || 40 ||

[kāmanābhedena pātrabhedāḥ]

vaśyākarṣaṇakārye tu hemapātraṃ praśasyate | 
śāntike pauṣṭike caiva rājataṃ kārayed budhaḥ || 41 ||


41 | pātrabhedena phalabhedamāha haratattvadīdhitidhṛtaśivarahasye -

hemapātrāṇi sarvāṇi gṛhītāni bhavantu me |
arghyaṃ dattvā tu raupyeṇa loke pūjyasutā/llabhet ||

p. 604) lauhapātraṃ vijānīyānmāraṇoccāṭane tathā |
stambhakāryeṣu pāṣāṇaṃ vidveṣe lauhamṛnmayam || 42 ||

kulotsādanakāryeṣu kācapātraṃ praśasyate |
kāṣṭhapātraṃ vijānīyānmantrārādhanakarmaṇi || 43 ||

[sarvatra viśvāmitrapātrasya prāśastyam]

sarvakāryeṣu kartavyaṃ viśvāmitra manuttamam | 

[pātrabhedena phalabhedāḥ]

śaṅkhaṃ jñānapradaṃ proktamalāvu sarvasiddhidam || 44 ||

uktaṃ mahārājatantramataṃ paramadurlabham |

[arghyapātraparimāṇam]

ṣoḍaśāṅgulamānaṃ tat pātraṃ sarvasamṛddhidam || 45 ||


tāmrapātreṇa saubhāgyaṃ dharmaṃ manmayasambhave |
sarvālābhe tu māheyaṃ svahastaghaṭitaṃ yadi ||

ā(mala ? sana)ñcārghyapātrañca bhagnamāsādayenna tu |
sarvatra svarṇavattāmramarghyapātre tato'dhikam ||

pātrāṇāmādaraḥ kāryaḥ pātrāṇyevottamāni tu |
bahirhomakriyāyāntu vinā pātraṃ na sidhyati || iti |

adhikāribhedena pātrabhedamāha gandharvatantre dvādaśapaṭale -

śaṅkhaśuktirajatasvarṇairdivyānāṃ pātramiṣyate |
mahāśaṅkhaistu vīrāṇāṃ phalajaṃ vanavāsinām || iti |

arghyādau tāmrapātrasya prāśastyamāha -

pūjārghyapātraṃ naivedyādyādhārapānapātrakam |
sarvaṃ tāmramayaṃ kuryād yatkiñcidiha pūjane ||
tāmre devāḥ pramodante tāmre devāḥ sthitāḥ sadā |
devīprītikaraṃ tāmraṃ tasmāttāmraṃ prayojayet ||

p. 605) dvādaśāṅgulamānaṃ vā vasvaṅgulamathāpi vā |
etannyūnañca yat pātraṃ tat pātramadhamaṃ smṛtam || 46 ||

[varjanīyapātranirūpaṇam]

atyantasthūlapātrantu sūkṣmapātrantu varjayet |
eteṣāmācaredekaṃ pāthasā sādhakottamaḥ || 47 ||

[pātrasthāpanakramaḥ]

astramantreṇa prakṣālya sthāpayed yantrikopari |


45-47 | arghyapātrasya parimāṇamāha - ṣoḍaśetyādinā |

viśiṣyā'ha kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhapaṭale -

ukteṣveteṣu pātreṣu pātramekaṃ prakalpayet |
ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistāraṃ ṣaḍaṅgulasamucchrayam ||
athavā'pi tadardhaṃ syāttadardhaṃ vā'pi kārayet | 
ekāṅgulaṃ tathā sārdhaṃ ṣaṭkañcaiva pramāṇataḥ ||
atisūkṣmamatisthalaṃ sacchidraṃ parivarjayet |
na yojayed bhagnapātraṃ svapnādeśaṃ praśasyate ||

iti | śivārcanacandrikādhṛtaśivarahasye'pi -

ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulaṃ pātramuttamaṃ parikīrtitam |
madhyamantu tribhāgonaṃ kaniṣṭhaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam ||
vasvaṅgulavihīnantu na pātraṃ kārayet kvacit ||
nābhīvivarayuktāni puṇḍarīkākṛtīni ca |
drākṣānīlotpalādīni pātrāṇi parikalpayet ||

iti | tathā haratattvadīdhitidhṛtaśyāmārcanacandrikāyām -

bhagnaṃ vasvaṅgulinyūnaṃ kṣīṇaṃ sacchidrameva ca |
vijātīyañca deveśi varjayet sarvathā priye || iti |

p. 606) [pātrapratiṣṭhāpanamantraḥ]

bālābījatrayasyānte tatastripurasundarī || 48 ||

viśeṣārghyapadāt pātraṃ pratiśabdādanantaram |
sthāpayāmīti manunā pratiṣṭhāpya kalā raveḥ || 49 ||

[tatra sūryakalāmaṇḍalārcā]

prapūjya vidhivad vidvān syāt sūryamaṇḍalāya ca |
dvādaśeti kalātmane cārghyapātrāya hṛnmanuḥ || 50 ||

[maṇḍalavidhiḥ]

trikoṇaṃ vṛttaṣaṭkoṇaṃ pātramadhye likhettataḥ |


48-50 | ādhāre'rghyapātrasthāpanamāha- astreti | astramantreṇa phaḍityanena | prakṣālya 
sāmānyārghyodakeneti śeṣaḥ | yantrikopari tripadikādiprāguktādhāre | tathāca gandharvatantre 
dvādaśapaṭale -

tathaivāstreṇa yutayā sāmānyārghyodakena tu |
prakṣālya parameśāni maṇḍale sthāpayettataḥ ||

ityanena maṇḍale'rghyapātrādhārasthāpanasya kramamupadiśya -

pūrvavat parameśāni pātraṃ prakṣālya sādhakaḥ |

ityādinā ādhārasthāpanakrameṇaivārghyapātrasyāpi sthāpanamuktam | 
arghyapātrapratiṣṭhāmantramāha-bāleti | tathāca aiṃ klīṃ sauḥ tripurasundarīviśeṣārghyapātraṃ 
pratiṣṭhāpayāmīti pratiṣṭhāpanamantraḥ pūrvavat | dvitārīprayogastritārīprayogo vā sarvatra 
pūrvavat | kalā raveriti | prāguktasāmānyārghyakrameṇa dvādaśa tapinyādisūryakalāḥ 
prapūjyetyarthaḥ | sūryamaṇḍaleti | (aṃ) sūryamaṇḍalāya dvādaśakalātmane arghyapātrāya 
nama iti mantreṇārghyapātraṃ pūjayedityarthaḥ |

p. 607) [maṇḍalapūjā]

samastavyastakūṭena pūjayet tu trikoṇake || 51 ||

[pātrāpūraṇakramaḥ]

bālātribījaṃ tripurasundaryarghyapadopari |
pātraṃ saṃpūrayāmīti saṅkalpya manunā'munā || 52 ||

anulomavilomābhyāṃ mātṛkārṇaṃ samuccaran |
amṛtāmṛtabuddhyā tu śrīpātraṃ paripūrayet || 53 ||

[pātrāpūraṇadravyāṇi]

jalācchataguṇaṃ proktaṃ dugdhodakamanuttamam |
dugdhāddadhi śataguṇaṃ dadhnaḥ śataguṇaṃ ghṛtam || 54 ||

ghṛtācchataguṇo hetu(?) devīdakṣiṇasādhane |


51 | trikoṇamiti | niruktārghyapātramadhye ādau trikoṇaṃ tadbahirvṛttaṃ tadbahiḥ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ 
likhedityarthaḥ | atra kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhapaṭale -

maṇḍalaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā pūjayet pūrvavartmanā |

ityabhidhānāt, tantrasāre'pi pūrvavad yantramālikhyetyukterjñānārṇavādāvapi tathā darśanāt 
atrāpi ṣaṭkoṇād bahirbhūpuraṃ likhediti hṛdayam | samasteti trikūṭātmakamantreṇa; vyastakūṭena 
pṛthak pṛthak vāgbhava - kāmarāja - śaktikūṭenetyarthaḥ |

52-53 | atha pātrāpūraṇakramamāha - bāleti | tathāca ai/ klī/ sau/ ḥ tripurasundaryarghyapātraṃ 
saṃpūrayāmīti mantreṇa saṃkalpyetyarthaḥ | anulometi | akārāt kṣakārāntaṃ punaḥ

vibhāvyetyarthaḥ | śrīpātramarghyapātram |

54 | atha pātrāpūraṇadravyāṇi dravyabhedena phalabhedāṃścā'ha - jalāditi | jalād 
dugdhodakasya śataguṇaphalajanakatvaṃ tato dadhnaḥ śataguṇaphalajanakatvamityevaṃ 
krameṇoktānāṃ phalajanakatvamavagantavyamityarthaḥ | dakṣiṇasādhana iti | dakṣiṇārakrameṇa 
sādhana ityarthaḥ | viśiṣyā'ha gandharvatantre dvādaśapaṭale -

p. 608) [arghyadravyāṇi]

dūrvākṣataṃ raktapuṣpaṃ bilvapatrañca candanam || 55 ||

nānāsugandhikadravyaṃ navaratnāṣṭagandhakam |

[sakalasomamaṇḍalārcā]

dattvā yajet somakalāḥ pūrvavat paścimakramāt || 56 ||

syāt somamaṇḍalāyānte ṣoḍaśeti kalātmane |
arghyāmṛtāya nama ityaghya dadyād vāmakrameṇa tu || 57 ||

[gandhāṣṭakam]

candanāgurukastūrīcorakuṅkumarocanāḥ |
jaṭāmāṃsī kapiyutā śaktergandhāṣṭakaṃ viduḥ || 58 ||


mātṛkāṃ pratilomena mūlamantrābhimantritaiḥ |
ādimaiḥ pūrayed vā'pi tathā puṣpāsavairapi ||

kapilāyāstathā kṣīrairnārikelāmṛtairapi |
ikṣurasaiḥ sugandhaiśca madhuratritayena vā ||

pūrayettanmāhāpātraṃ nānādravyaiḥ kadācana |
adhikāriprabhedena dravyabhedā bhavanti hi ||
svayambhūkusumantatra karpūrāgurucandanaiḥ |
rocanākuṅkumairmiśraṃ kastūrīparibhāvitam |
nikṣipet parameśāni śarkarāṃ madhusaṃyutām || iti |

55-56 | atha tatrārghyadravyārpaṇamāha - dūrveti | spaṣṭam | naveti | navaratnaṃ 
gandhāṣṭakañca dattvā yajed vakṣyamāṇakrameṇeti śeṣaḥ | navaratnāni pañcamaprakāśe 
(5/93 ślo.) pradarśitāni |

	gandhāṣṭakantu svayamevāgre vakṣyati | yajediti | pūrvavat sāmānyārghyakrameṇa 
amṛtādiṣoḍaśacandrakalā yajedityarthaḥ | someti | (uṃ) somamaṇḍalāya ṣoḍaśakalātmane 
arghyāmṛtāya nama iti prayogaḥ |

58 | pūrvamaṣṭagandhakamityuktam, tad gandhāṣṭakaṃ nirūpayati -candanetyādinā | coraḥ 
kṛṣṇaśaṭhī, kapiḥ gāṭhiyālā iti tantrasāraḥ | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam |

p. 609) [arghyāmṛte yantralekhanādikramaḥ]

puṣpeṇa yantramālikhya pūrvasomāmṛtāntare |
akathādīṃstrirekhāsu haṃ laṃ kṣaṃ madhyato likhet || 59 ||

[tatrārcāvāhane]

trikoṇaṃ pūjayenmantrī trikūṭena yathākramāt |
hastābhyāṃ pātramācchādya tīrthāvāhanamācaret || 60 ||

[pañcaratnārcākramaḥ]

pūrvādito dikṣu madhye pañcaratnaṃ prapūjayet || 61 ||

[pañcaratnamantraḥ]

kāntāntaṃ somatapanaṃ bhāntaṃ dhāntaṃ mahīyutam |
dakṣakarṇendusaṃyuktaṃ pañcaratnamanukramāt || 62 ||

[pañcaratnāni]

gaganaṃ svargapātālaṃ marttyaṃ nāgamanantaram |
pañcaratnamidaṃ proktaṃ devyānandakaraṃ mahat || 63 ||


59 | puṣpeṇeti | pūrvasomāmṛtāntare somakalātmakārghyasthāmṛtābhyantare | yantraṃ 
trikoṇavṛttaṣaṭkoṇacaturasrātmakayantramityarthaḥ | akatheti | antastrikoṇaghaṭakarekhātraye krameṇa 
akārādiṣoḍaśasvarān, kakārāditakārāntān thakārādisakārāntāṃśca vyañjanavarṇān, tathā 
madhyato niruktatrikoṇābhyantare haṃ laṃ kṣamiti varṇatrayaṃ likhediti phalitārthaḥ | 
atrākārādikakārādithakārādivarṇaniyāmako'kathaśabda iti jñeyam |

60 | trikoṇamiti | niruktatrikoṇaṃ trikūṭena vāgbhava-kāmarāja-śaktikūṭairvyastaiḥ krameṇa 
pūjayedityarthaḥ | tīrthāvāhanamiti oṃ gaṅge cetyādinetyarthaḥ |

61 | pūrvādita iti | pūrvādidikcatuṣṭaye catvāri madhye caikamityevaṃkrameṇa pañcaratnaṃ 
prapūjayedityarthaḥ |

62-63 | pañcaratnānyāha-kāntāntamityādinā | kāntānto gakāraḥ, somaḥ sakāraḥ, tapanaḥ 
pakāraḥ, bhānto makāraḥ, dhānto nakāraḥ, etadvarṇapañcakaṃ mahīyutaṃ mahyā pṛthvībījena 
lakāreṇa yuktaṃ tathā dakṣakarṇena pañcamasvareṇa indunā'nusvāreṇa ca saṃyuktam |

p. 610) [ānandabhairavārcā]

ānandabhairavaṃ devaṃ pūrvavat paripūjayet |

[pañcabhirmantrairarcanasyopadeśaḥ]

kuladravyāṇi niḥkṣipya pañcamantraiḥ samarcayet || 64 ||


etena gluṃ sluṃ pluṃ mluṃ nluṃ iti bījapañcakaṃ prāptam | etat pañcaratnaṃ krameṇa 
gaganādīnāṃ pūrvaṃ yojayedityarthaḥ | upasaṃharati-pañcaratnamityādinā | evañca gluṃ 
gaganaratnāya namaḥ, sluṃ svargaratnāya namaḥ, pluṃ pātālaratnāya namaḥ, mluṃ 
marttyaratnāya namaḥ nluṃ nāgaratnāya namaḥ iti prayogakramaḥ | evameva 
tripurārcanarahasye'pi | kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhapaṭale tu gaganaratnebhyo namaḥ 
ityādibahuvacanāntaḥ prayogo dṛśyate | yathā -

saṃpūjayettataḥ paścāt pañcaratnaṃ susādhakaḥ |
pañcāntakaṃ binduyutaṃ dakṣakarṇasamanvitam ||
gaganānte ca ratnebhyo namo'ntamanumuddharet |
śītalaṃ śakrasaṃyuktamagrabinduvibhūṣitam ||
svargapadaṃ samuccārya ratnebhyo nama ityapi |
pañcamaṃ devarājañca dakṣaśrotrasamanvitam ||
pātālapadaratnebhyo namo'ntaścāparo manuḥ |
mahākālaṃ kṣitiyutaṃ dakṣakarṇasamanvitam ||
marttyaratne tataḥ paścād bhyo namo'tra samuddharet |
vāmapādaṃ kṣitiyutaṃ dakṣaśravaṇabhūṣitam | 
nāgapadaṃ samuccārya ratnebhyo nama ityapi ||

iti | asmābhistu mūle gaganamityādyekavacanāntapadadarśanād granthakṛdguru-
śrīmadbrahmānandakṛtatripurārcanarahasye 
gaganaratnāyetyādyekavacanāntaprayogadarśanāccaikavacanānta eva prayogaṣṭākāyāṃ 
pradarśitaḥ | parantu tatra pātālaratnāt pūrvaṃ marttyaratnasya samāveśo dṛśyata iti viśeṣaḥ |

64 | ānandeti | pūrvavat prākpradarśitasāmānyārghyoktakrameṇa | atrā'nandabhairavyā api 
pūrvavat pūjanaṃ vidheyaṃ tripurārcanarahasya-tantrasārādau tathā darśanāt |

p. 611) [tatra prathamo mantraḥ]

[dvitīyaḥ]

[mantrāṇāṃ pura�scaraṇakathanam]

ekaviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ

[brahmahatyādiprāyaścittakathanam]

brahmahatyādidoṣāṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vadāmyatha || 1 ||

[tatra kramaḥ]

raktapuṣpairyajeccakraṃ samastaraśmi saṃyutam |
kulācārakrameṇaiva karpūrakṣodamaṇḍitam || 2 ||

[tatra kālaniyamaḥ]

māsatrayeṇa niyataṃ mahāpātakakoṭayaḥ |
janmāntarakṛtāḥ sarve kṣaṇānnāśaṃ prayānti ca |
lakṣmīstasya gṛhe vaśyā susthirā sarvakāmadā || 3 ||

[javāpuṣpena pūjāpraśaṃsā]

javāpuṣpaiśca mantrajñaḥ pūrvavat pūjayecchivām |
brahmahatyādipāpāni pūrvajanmakṛtānyapi |
nāśayennātra sandeho dhanavān jāyate naraḥ || 4 ||

[sugandhikusumaiḥ pūjāphalam]

śivāṃ saṃpūjayennityaṃ sugandhikusumairapi |
mahāpātakayuktātmā tatkṣaṇāt pāpahā bhavet || 5 ||

[śamyādibhiḥ pūjāphalam]

śamīdūrvāṅkurāśvattha-pallavairatha sādhakaḥ |
māsena hanti kaluṣaṃ saptajanmani yat kṛtam || 6 ||

p. 793) [ketakīpatraiḥ pūjāphalam]

ketakītaruṇaiḥ patraiḥ pūrvavat pūjayed yadi |
upapātakasaṅghāṃśca nāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ |
saubhāgyamatulaṃ tasya jāyate paramādbhutam || 7 ||

[śatapatraiḥ pūjāphalam]

pūjanācchatapatraiśca sarvapāpaṃ vināśayet |

[śvetapadmaiḥ pūjāphalam]

pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ śvetapadmaiḥ prapūjanāt || 8 ||

kulācārakrameṇaiva bhavet saubhāgyavallabhaḥ |
viṃśajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ śvetapadmaiḥ prapūjayet |
māsamātreṇa sakulaṃ mokṣastasya kare sthitaḥ || 9 ||

[bandhūkakusumaiḥ pūjāphalam]

bandhūkakusumaiḥ śuddhaiḥ sarvavat pūjayed yadi |
trailokyaṃ vaśagaṃ tasya sarvapāpaṃ hared dhruvam || 10 ||

[vilvapatraiḥ padmaiśca pūjāphalam]

vilvapatraiśca jalajaiḥ sahaiva paripūjanāt |
samṛddhimān bhavettadvat sarvapāpaharaḥ svayam || 11 ||

[mallikādipuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

mallikā-mālatī-jātī-kundaiśca śatapatrakaiḥ |
śvetotpalaiśca saṃśuddhaiḥ pūjayenmāsamātrakam || 12 ||

viṃśajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ campakaiḥ pūjanādapi |
kulācārakrameṇaiva pātakaṃ śatajanmajam || 13 ||

p. 794) brahmahatyādijanitaṃ nāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ |
muktistasya kare nūnaṃ vācā jīvasamo bhuvi || 14 ||

[agastyādipuṣpaḥ pūjanaphalam]

agastya-bāṇā-bandhūka-javā-raktotpalairapi |
pātakaṃ nāśayettadvat sākṣāt kāmasamo bhavet || 15 ||

[campakādipuṣpaiḥ pūjanaphalam]

campakaiḥ pāṭalaiścaiva vakulairnāgakeśaraiḥ |
kahlāraiḥ sindhuvāraiśca pūjayed yadi sādhakaḥ |
saubhāgyamatulaṃ tasya māsamātreṇa vai bhavet || 16 ||

[ratnapūjāvidhānakathanam]

ratnapūjāvidhānantu kathayāmi viśeṣataḥ || 17 ||

puṣpāṇi racayenmantrī māṇikyaghaṭitāni ca |
taistu pūjā prakartavyā cakrarājasya pūrvavat |
nānāpuṣpaiḥ sugandhaiśca karpūrakṣodacarcitaiḥ || 18 ||

[ratnapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjāphalam]

ekaviṃśatirātreṇa viṃśatirdharaṇībhujām |
dāsabhūtā bhavatyeva mahārogāṃśca nāśayet || 19 ||

sūryavat kāntimān mantrī jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 20 ||


15-16 | agastyeti | agastyaṃ vakapuṣpaṃ, bāṇā nīlajhiṇṭī, nīlā jhiṇṭī dvayorbāṇā dāsī 
cārtagalaśca sā || ityamaraḥ |
	bandhūkaḥ bandhujīvakaḥ (dupahariyā iti khyātaḥ) bandhūko bandhujīvakaḥ ityamaraḥ | 
pāṭalaṃ pāṭalāyāḥ puṣpam (golābākhyaṃ puṣpamiti kecit) |

p. 795) muktāratnairaṅkitāni svarṇapuṣpāṇi yāni ca |
taistu pūjā prakartavyā nānāratnaistu pūrvavat || 21 ||

ekaviṃśatirātreṇa rājapatnyo vaśānugāḥ |
kalākāntiyuto mantrī jāyate śubhagaḥ kṣitau || 22 ||

[prabālapuṣpaiḥ svarṇapuṣpaiśca pūjāphalam]

prabālaracitaiḥ svarṇaiḥ puṣpaiśca bahubhiryajet |
anyairnānāvidhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ saptāhaṃ kulavartmanā || 23 ||

krūrāstasya vaśāḥ sarve vairivargān vināśayet |

[marakatapuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

tathā marakatakṣiptaivarī mitraṃ dhruvaṃ bhavet || 24 ||

[padmarāgapuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

padmarāgamahāratnanirmitaiḥ svarṇasaṃyutaiḥ |
kusumaiḥ pūjayeccakraṃ trisaptāhaṃ krameṇa tu |
surāstasya vaśāḥ sarve bṛhaspatisamo bhavet || 25 ||

[hīrakayuktasvarṇapuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

suvarṇaracitaiḥ puṣpairvajra-ketakavāsitaiḥ |
ekaviṃśatirātreṇa mohayejjagatīmimām || 26 ||

devā daityā vaśāstasya jāyante nātra saṃśayaḥ || 27 ||

[nīlakāntamaṇiyuktasvarṇapuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

indranīlamayaiḥ svarṇapuṣpaiścakraṃ prapūjayet |

p. 796) ekaviṃśatirātraiśca tathā nīlaiśca nīrajaiḥ |
vairiṇo nāśamāyānti śeṣā vaśyā bhavanti ca || 28 ||

[gomedayuktasvarṇapuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

gomedaghaṭitaiḥ svarṇapuṣpaiḥ kundaiśca kiṃśukaiḥ |
saptāhād vairiṇī vaśyā ghātasteṣu prajāyate |
trisaptāhānmahāpāpasañcayaṃ nāśayed dhruvam || 29 ||

[nūtanasvarṇaratnamayapuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

svarṇaratnamayaiḥ puṣpairnavaiścāpi prapūjayet |
tadā'śvamedhadaśakaṃ phalaṃ bhavati niścitam || 30 ||

kravyādajīvā vaśyā hi bhavantyeva na saṃśayaḥ |

[vaidūryayuktasvarṇapuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

vaidūryaghaṭitaiḥ svarṇapuṣpaiścampakajātibhiḥ |
cakrarājaṃ samabhyarccya trailokyaṃ stambhayet kṣaṇāt || 31 ||

ekaviṃśatibhirvārairmahāpāpaharo bhavet || 32 ||

[nānāratnayuktasvarṇapuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

nānāratnamayaiḥ svarṇapuṣpairyadi śivāṃ yajet |
tadā devāśca munayaḥ pannagā rākṣasādayaḥ || 33 ||

sarve vaśyā bhavantyeva trisaptāhānna saṃśayaḥ |
janmakoṭibhavaṃ pāpaṃ sādhakasya vināśayet || 34 ||

[svalpasvarṇaratnādiyuktapuṣpaiḥ pūjāphalam]

svarṇaratnādipūrṇatvaṃ yadi na syāttathā punaḥ |
taireva puṣpaiḥ pūjā hi karttavyā sādhakottamaiḥ || 35 ||

p. 797) alaṅkārasvarūpeṇa pūjayeccakranāyikām |
kevalaṃ yaistu puṣpaistu trailokyaṃ stambhayet khalu || 36 ||

māsamātreṇa pāpāni saptajanmabhavāni ca |
nāśayenmohayet sarvaṃ samudravalayāṃ dharām || 37 ||

[paryupitapuṣpaiḥ pūjananiṣedhaḥ]

puṣpaiḥ paryuṣitaiścaiva nārccayet svarṇajairapi |

[nirmālyādipuṣpaiḥ pūjananiṣedhaḥ]

nirmālyabhūtaiḥ kusumairucchiṣṭairapi nārccayet || 38 ||

[apāmārgādidalaiḥ pūjanaphalam]

apāmārgadalairbhṛṅgairvilvairmarakatairapi |
patrairebhiḥ prayatnena pūjayet parameśvarīm || 39 ||

[śāktapuṣpakathanam]

ambhojotpala-bandhujīva-vijayā-punnāga-nāgānyathojātī-kunda-kuraṇṭa-campaka-javā-yūthī-ramā-
pāṭalaiḥ |
vilvāśoka-hayāri-kubja-madanairmandāra-dūrvāṅkurairdroṇāgastyaparājitāni kusumānyuktāni 
śāktānyalam || 40 ||

[mallikātagarādibhiḥ pūjā kartavyā]

sarvakālodbhavaiḥ puṣpairmallikā-tagarādibhiḥ |
sitaraktaiḥ prayatnena pūjayet parameśvarīm || 41 ||



40 | ambhojotpaleti | vijayā śephālikā, punnāgaṃ keśara-puṣpam, nāgaṃ nāgakeśarapuṣpam, 
kuraṇṭaṃ raktajhiṇṭīpuṣpam, hayāri karavīrapuṣpam, kubjam apāmārgaphalam, madanaṃ 
vakulapuṣpam, (mayanā iti khyātaṃ vā) mandāram arkapuṣpam (ākanda iti khyātam) |

p. 798) [kalikābhiḥ pūjāniṣedhaḥ]

kalikābhistathā neṣṭaṃ vinā campakapaṅkajaiḥ |

[jalajacampakayoḥ paryuṣitadoṣābhāvaḥ]

na paryuṣitadoṣo'sti jalaje campake'pi ca || 42 ||

[yantrapuṣpakathanam]

yatrāparājitāpuṣpaṃ puṣpaṃ javākhyamuttamam |
karavīre śuklarakte droṇāpuṣpañca eva hi || 43 ||

eṣu devī vasennityaṃ tadyantre tāṃ prapūjayet |
ataḥ prayatnato mantrī puṣpairebhiḥ samarcayet || 44 ||

yoginītantre tripurāprakaraṇe �

[puṣpaviśeṣe kecana vidhiniṣedhāḥ]

śephālikāntu kahlāraṃ śaratkālaṃ vinā tyajet || 45 ||

bakulasya tvaśokasya arjunasya maheśvari |
pūjayed vṛntavarjena savṛntena vivarjayet || 46 ||

viṣṇukrāntā javā nāgakeśaraṃ nāgavallabham |
bandhūkañcaiva kahlāraṃ savṛntena samarccayet || 47 ||

na spṛśed vṛntavarjañca bhūmiṣṭhañca na saṃspṛśet |
śephāli vakulañcaiva bhūgatena samācaret || 48 ||

puṣpañcākṣatasaṃyuktaṃ tathā raktavilepitam |
sarvānte sundarīṃ nyasya raktapuṣpaṃ tathā nyaset || 49 ||

p. 799) [vilvapatrasya prāśastyam]

patreṣu vilvapatrāṇi dattvā devyai prayatnataḥ |
vilvapatrairvinā pūjā śākte śaive na dṛśyate || 50 ||

[aṅgaraṅgeṣu sindūrasya praśastatā]

aṅgaraṅgeṣu sindūraṃ devyāḥ prītikaraṃ param |
ataḥ prayatnato mantrī sindūraṃ vinivedayet |

[mukhavāseṣu tāmbūlasya praśastatā]

mukhavāseṣu tāmbūlaṃ devyānandakaraṃ param || 51 ||

evameva vidhānena pūjayed yadi pārvatīm |
abhīṣṭaṃ saptarātreṇa labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 52 ||

[vāraviśeṣe puṣpaviśeṣaprāśastyam]

ravivāre'ruṇāmbhojaiḥ kumudaiḥ somavāsare |
bhaume raktotpalaiḥ saumyavāre tagarasambhavaiḥ || 53 ||

guruvāre ca kahlāraiḥ śukravāre sitāmbujaiḥ |
utpalaiḥ śanivāre ca pūjayedabdamādrāt || 54 ||

[vāraviśeṣe nivedanīyadravyāṇi]

nivedayet kramātteṣu ravivārādisaptasu |
pāyasaṃ dugdha-kadalī-navanītaṃ sitā-dhṛtam || 55 ||

[varṣamātradevīpūjanaphalam]

ekamabdaṃ samārādhya devīṃ gandhādibhiḥ kramāt |
grahapīḍāṃ vijityāśu sukhāni ca samaśnute || 56 ||


55 | nivedayediti | sitā śarkarā |

p. 800) [parvatāgre devīpūjanaphalam]

parvatāgre yajeddevīṃ palāśakusumairniśi |
siddhadravyaiśca saptāhāt khecarīmelanam bhavet || 57 ||

[araṇyādiṣu pūjāphalam]

araṇye vaṭamūle vā kuñje vā dharaṇībhṛtām |
kadambajātipuṣpābhyāṃ siddhadravyaiḥ śivāṃ yajet |
māsena siddhaḍākinyaḥ pratyakṣā vāñchitapradāḥ || 58 ||

[samudratīre pūjāphalam]

ketakīkusumaiḥ siddhāśceṭakā vāridhestaṭe |
ājñāmabhīṣṭāṃ vanitāṃ kāmamāyāṃ mahādbhutām |
vasūni mālāṃ bhūṣāñca dadyurasyecchayā bhṛśam || 59 ||

[akṣadrumapīṭhopari devīpūjāphalam]

pīṭhamakṣadrumaiḥ kṛtvā tatra devīṃ yajenniśi |
śālmalīkusumaiḥ siddhadravyairmāsantu nirbhayaḥ || 60 ||

[śmaśāne pūjanaphalam]

pitṛbhūmau sādhakendraḥ sidhyantasya piśācikāḥ |

[vetālasiddhuyapāyaḥ]

nirjane vipine rātrau māsamātrantu nirbhayaḥ || 61 ||

yajeddevīṃ cakragatāṃ siddhadravyasamanvitām |
mālatījātipunnāgaketakīmadhubhiḥ kramāt || 62 ||


60 | pīṭhamakṣeti | akṣadramaiḥ vibhītakavṛkṣaiḥ |

p. 801) tena siddhyanti vetālānatha vai svecchayā caret |

[madhyarātre sthānaviśeṣe devīpūjāphalam]

śmaśāne caṇḍikāgehe nirjane vipine'thavā || 63 ||

madhyarātre yajeddevīṃ kṛṣṇavastrādibhūṣitaḥ |
kṛṣṇacakre'tikṛṣṇāṃ tāmabhivādya prapūjayet || 64 ||

sādhyayoniṃ tadagre tu baliṃ chindannivedayet |
siddhadravyasamopetaṃ māsāttadbhāvalocanāt || 65 ||

jāyante bhīṣaṇāḥ kṛṣṇāstābhyaḥ sādhyaṃ nivedayet |
viśvasaṃhārasantuṣṭāṃ punaretya nijecchayā || 66 ||

[tirodhānasiddhayapāyaḥ]

devyā lalāṭanetre syuḥ prārthanena tirohitā || 67 ||

[māhāsiddhyupāyaḥ]

raktabhūṣāmbarā nopamālābhūṣitavigrahaḥ |
udyāne nirjane devīṃ cakre sañcintya pūjayet || 68 ||

kahlāracampakāśokapāṭalāśatapatrakaiḥ |
siddhadravyasamopetairmāyāḥ sidhyanti māsataḥ || 69 ||

[kāmarūpasiddhyupāyaḥ]

māsaṃ prasādalābhena kāmarūpo bhavennaraḥ |
yābhirviśvaṃ yayau viśvacārī viśvavinodavān || 70 ||

p. 802) [trikālajñatvasiddhyupāyaḥ]


ṣaḍādhārāramadhye tu cakraṃ sañcintya pūjayet |
candracandanakastūrīmṛganābhimadodbhavaiḥ |
trikālajño bhaveddevīṃ teṣu samyag vicintayet || 71 ||

[pūgodyāne devīpūjāphalam]

pūgodyāne yajeddevīṃ siddhadravyairdivāniśam |
nivasaṃstatra tatpuṣpairjāyate manmathopamaḥ || 72 ||

[pūrṇāsu kanyakāyāṃ devīpūjāphalam]

pūrṇāsu niyataṃ devīṃ kanyakāyāṃ samarccayet |
kṛtyāḥ pareritā mantrā vimukhāstān grasanti vai || 73 ||

[madanāropaṇakathanam]

madanāropaṇaṃ vakṣye sādhakānāṃ hitāya ca |
saṃvatsarakṛtā pūjā yenaiva pūrṇatā bhavet || 74 ||

śivasya madanaḥ pūrvaṃ tapobhaṅganibandhanāt |
bhasmābhavattadā patnī ratiḥ prītiśca duḥkhitā || 75 ||

tannetravārisambhūtā madanasya tadā bhavet |
tat saurabhañca saubhāgyaṃ mahadāsīttadadbhutam || 76 ||

tena santuṣṭahṛdayād varo dattaḥ śivena tu |
ratyai prītyai ca tasmāttu madanaḥ prakṛtīkṛtaḥ || 77 ||
