[jñānadūtīkathanam]

jñānadūtīmatho vakṣye yayā brahma sanātanam |
prakāśākhye yāgagehe praviśed varamandire || 1 ||

icchājñānakriyādūtīracite svabhagānvite |
anayā parayā dūtyā tatra jñānamaye śive || 2 ||

paryaṅke puruṣārthaiśca vedaiśca parimaṇḍite |
ātmāntarātmaparamātmajñānātmaparibhūṣite || 3 ||

tatpadārthaḥ padārthastvaṃ tattvaṃ padasamuccayaḥ |
padārthatrayametattu jñānād brahmaprakāśakam || 4 ||

etatsūtramayaiḥ paṭṭairgumphite sumanohare |
prapūjayet parāṃ tatra bhaved bhūtiścaturvidhā || 5 ||

hastinī śaṅkhinī cānyā citriṇī padminī tathā |
sarvābharaṇasampannāḥ sundaryaścārulocanāḥ || 6 ||

vaikharī hastinī jñeyā sthūlāvasthā parā kalā |
yayedaṃ dhāryate sarvaṃ brahmāṇḍaparimaṇḍalam || 7 ||

vargāṣṭakena niyataṃ sūte diggajasañcayam |
tenaiva kariṇī proktā madhyamā śaṅkhinī bhavet || 8 ||


7 | vaikharī akārādikṣakāraparyantavarṇamālā, vidyeti śridharaḥ |

p. 815) śaṅkhinī ca mahāśuddhā sarvapāpavivarjitā |
sarvadevapriyā sā hi kāntisaurabhyaśobhitā || 9 ||

tathā vanaspatigatā madhyamāśuśubhagrahā |
citriṇī vallarī devī sarvaśaktisvarūpiṇī || 9 ||

śivaśaktimayī devī pāti caiva jagattrayam |
teṣu sarveṣu paśyantī jīveṣu paramāśrayā || 10 ||

evaṃ brahmaṇi saṃlīnā parārādhyā pareśvarī |
tāṃ dūtīṃ tatra saṃpūjya śamādikusumaiḥ śuciḥ || 11 ||

tadaṅgeṣu kalāḥ pūjyāḥ krameṇa sādhakottamaiḥ |
yathā śāntikalā bodhakalā vyāptikalā parā || 12 ||

jñānātmanaḥ kalā jñeyā caturthī syāttataḥ param |
padminī cāparā jñeyā haṃsa (ḥ?) svacchavikāśitā || 13 ||

haṃsaḥ ? dvandvena padmaṃ hi ghalkā ? saṅkocameva tu |
vikāśaṃ mātri ? saubhāgyaṃ tathā varṇavilāsataḥ || 14 ||

parā prakāśamāyāti padminī tu matā bhavet |
tasya dehe sādhakendraiḥ kalāḥ pūjyāstu ṣoḍaśa || 15 ||

citkalā sakalā śāntikalā kālākalā parā |
ājñāvāstu kalā jñeyā saṃlīnā paravastuni || 16 ||

bhūyaḥ śāntikalādyāstu jñānātmanaḥ prakāśitāḥ |
virāgatā mokṣakalā paramātmakalā tathā || 17 ||

p. 816) vidyākalācatasrastu vijñeyāścāntarātmani |
vidyāsantoṣatā tṛptiḥ paraṃ brahmaprakāśikāḥ || 18 ||

paramātmakalā jñeyā saṃlīnā paravastuni |
etāstu ṣoḍaśa kalāḥ śrīvidyānteṣu saṃsthitāḥ || 19 ||

ṣoḍaśāntā brahmarūpā kalā ṣoḍaśarūpiṇī |
ātmayogasvarūpeṇa trailokyaṃ racayatyasau || 20 ||

antarātmā sattvarūpo rajorūpaśca viśrutaḥ |
ata eva sattvarūpo rajorūpaḥ paraḥ śivaḥ || 21 ||

paramātma(a) vidhirjñeyo jñānātmā tūryasādhakaḥ |
etāḥ kalāśca niyataṃ paradehe prakāśayet || 22 ||

tatpadārthantu jānīhi mukhamasyā na saṃśayaḥ |
tataḥ padapadārthābhyāṃ kucayugmakrameṇa tu || 23 ||

nādo mūlaṃ hi yogasya śāstrasyeti vinirṇayaḥ |
nādastu sarvabhūtānāṃ bhaved yonirna saṃśayaḥ || 24 ||

śaktiṃ saṃpūjayettatra trikoṇe bhagamālikām |
sarvānandamaye madhye sundarīṃ pūjayet parām || 25 ||

svātmaliṅge tato mantrī śivañca paripūjayet |
tārañca bhuvaneśānīṃ mahātripurasundarīm || 26 ||

namaḥ śivāya vidyeyaṃ daśārṇā parikīrttitā |
parasparasvabhāvena brahmānandaraso bhavet || 27 ||

p. 817) taṃ rasaṃ manasā mantrā vahannāsāgataṃ smaret |
arghyāmṛtena saṃyojya tataḥ śrīcakramarccayet || 28 ||

kuṇḍagolodbhavaṃ dravyaṃ proktaṃ paramadurlabham |
brahmānandamaye jñāne pāpapuṇyavivarjite || 29 ||

na brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇastu na kṣattraḥ kṣattriyastathā |
vaiśyo'pi na ca vaiśyaḥ syānna śūdraḥ śūdra eva ca || 30 ||

cāṇḍālo naiva cāṇḍālaḥ pukkaso naiva pukkasaḥ |
sarvaṃ samaṃ vijānīyāt paramārthaviniścayāt || 31 ||

ākāśāt patitaṃ toyaṃ bindumārgeṇa gacchati |
grāmamadhye sthitaṃ toyaṃ viṣṭhāmūtrādipūritam || 32 ||

mṛgago'śvādimārjārakharādirudhirānvitam |
samastamapi gaṅgāyāṃ prayāti militaṃ yathā || 33 ||

gaṅgāmṛtaṃ bhavet sarvaṃ kāluṣyaṃ naiva niṣṭhati |
yathā saṃvinmaye jñāne samatā sarvajantuṣu || 34 ||

sarvadravyeṣu niyataṃ samatā nātra saṃśayaḥ |
yathā sūryaprakāśo'yaṃ sattvenaiva pravarttate || 35 ||

tataḥ svasyāpi cāntyasya tamaḥ saṃharati kṣaṇāt |
śoṣaya(n) dugdhamūtrādicandanaṃ naiva karddamam || 36 ||

ata eva vicārajño mantrī sarvamayo bhavet |
siddhistadaiva vijñeyā śrīguroḥ kṛpayā parā || 37 ||

p. 818) [adbhutatrikūṭasādhanaprakārakathanam]

tathādbhutaṃ pravakṣyāmi trikūṭasyāpi sādhanam |
śuklāmbaradharo dhīro gandhakastūrīmaṇḍitaḥ || 38 ||

muktāphalasphuradbhūṣābhūṣitaḥ śuklamālyadhṛk |
dhavalāgāramadhye tu brahmacaryavrato naraḥ || 39 ||

pūjayecchubhrakusumairnaivedyañca samujjvalam |
pāyasaṃ ghṛtasaṃpūrṇaṃ tathā'mṛtaphalaudanam || 40 ||

dugdhaṃ modakasaṃyuktaṃ nānāpūpānnapūritam |
naivedyaṃ darśayeddevyai vāgīśvaryai susādhakaḥ || 41 ||

[tatra vāgbhavakūṭasādhanam]

manaḥsaṅkalpaśubhrairvā sādhayed vāgbhavākṣaram |
vāgbhavākhyāṃ japed vidyāṃ vāgīśīṃ saṃsmared budhaḥ || 42 ||

[vāgīśīdhyānam]

karpūradhavalāṃ śubhrāṃ śubhrābharaṇabhūṣitām |
muktāphalāmalamaṇijapamālālasatkarām || 43 ||

pustakaṃ varadānañca dadhatīmabhayapradām |
kucabhārabharākrāntāṃ dīpābhāṃ tanturūpiṇīm || 44 ||

gacchantīṃ brahmamārgeṇa liṅgabhedakrameṇa tu |

[devyā viśeṣacintā]

visargasthāmṛtaṃ divyaṃ kṣīradhāropamaṃ dhruvam || 45 ||


      42 | vāgbhavākhyāmiti ai/ iti bījamityarthaḥ |

p. 819) pītvā kulāmṛtañcaiva punareva viśet kulam |
punareva kulaṃ gacched dvādaśānte niveśayet || 46 ||

sahasrāre mahāpadme vāgbhavaṃ pūrṇacandrakam |
brahmagranthiṃ tato bhittvā saptaṣaṣṭhākṣadhārayā || 47 ||

lambikāṃ brahmamārgeṇa tālumūle praveśayet |
jihvāmāpūrya mantrajño mātṛkāvarṇarūpiṇīm || 48 ||

bahirgatāśca vāgrūpā(ṃ) jihvāgre'mṛtarūpiṇīm |
ā mūlād brahmarandhrāntaṃ tasmājjihvāpradeśakam || 49 ||

vāgbhavāṃ bījarūpāntāṃ dīpākārāṃ vicintayet |
hastayoḥ pādayormantrī tathā nāḍītrayeṣu ca || 50 ||

ādhārāttu samutthāpya vāgbhavaṃ dvādaśe nayet |
dvādaśādhāramānīya tadgranthiṃ paribhedayet || 51 ||

tasmājjihvāgramānīya dīpābhāṃ paribhāvayet |

[asya lakṣavārajapaphalam]

lakṣamekamidaṃ japtvā sarvapāpaharaḥ svayam || 52 ||

vākpatirjāyate sadyo mūko'pi sukavirbhavet |
vāde sadasi sadvākyastambhakaḥ prativādinaḥ || 53 ||

satpaṇḍitaghaṭāṭopajetā pratihataprabhaḥ |
sattarkaprativākyārthasarvālaṅkāraśāstravit || 54 ||

padagumphairmahāvākyakarttā mantrī prajāyate |
avyāhatamatiḥ śāstre vijñeyo vākpatirbhavet || 55 ||

p. 820) [bālakajihvāyāṃ mantralekhanam]

bālakasya tu jihvāyāṃ tridinābhyantare likhet |
madhunā śvetadūrvābhiḥ kiṃ vā svarṇaśalākayā || 56 ||

amṛtaṃ vāgbhavaṃ kūṭaṃ vāgīśatvapradāyakam |
sa eva paṇḍitaḥ śrīmān sa cāmūrkho bhavet kila || 57 ||

[bālakasya mūrdhni japaphalam]

etad vāgbhavakūṭantu bālakasyāpi mūrddhani |
hastaṃ dattvā yadi japecchatamaṣṭottaraṃ budhaḥ || 58 ||

so'pi ślokān sarvathaiva karoti vākpatiryathā |
etattu kathitaṃ sāraṃ vāgbhavasyāpi sādhanam || 59 ||

[culukapānam]

atho devīṃ sacakrāntaculuke salile smaran |
mātṛkāsahitāṃ vidyāṃ trirāvṛttyā'mṛtātmikām || 60 ||

nāḍīṃ sarasvatīṃ jihvāṃ dīpākārāṃ smaran pibet |

[culukapānaphalam]

abdāccaturvidhaṃ tasya pāṇḍityaṃ bhuvi jāyate || 61 ||

[prātaḥkāle pānaphalam]

evaṃ nityamuṣaḥkāle yaḥ kuryācchuddhamānasaḥ |
sa yogī satyavijñānī sa kavirbhavati dhruvam || 62 ||

[kāmakūṭasādhanaphalam]

atha vakṣyāmi saubhāgyaṃ kāmakūṭasya sādhanam |

[kāmakūṭasādhanaprakāraḥ]

raktālaṅkārasubhago raktamālyānulepanām || 63 ||

p. 821) raktavastrānvitāṃ samyaṅmadhyakāmakalātmanā |
raktapuṣpaiśca vividhaiḥ kusumādibhirarcayet || 64 ||

[kāmakalācintā]

tataḥ kāmakalārūpāṃ madanāṅkuramadhyame |
udyadādityasaṅkāśāṃ sphuraddīpaśikhāprabhām || 65 ||

gacchantīṃ brahmamārgeṇa brahmarandhrāntameva ca |
patadbhirindudhārābhīraktalākṣārasopamām || 66 ||

bandhūkakusumākārāṃ kāntibhūṣaṇabhūṣitām |
ikṣukodaṇḍapuṣpeṣuvarābhayalasatkarām || 67 ||

kāmarājasvarūpāntu bālārkakiraṇāruṇām |
cintayet kulapīṭheṣu saṃkṣobho jāyate kṣaṇāt || 68 ||

kāmakalā(ṃ) hṛdo madhye bandhūkasadṛśaprabhām |
cittākarṣaṇakārī ca nārīṇāñca viśeṣataḥ || 66 ||

mūrdhni bhāge kāmakalā varṣantī raktadhārayā |
dhāraṇāt saṃprayogeṇa karoti vaśagaṃ jagat || 70 ||

[kāmakūṭaprayogakathanam]

toṣayantī jagat sarvaṃ prayogaṃ kathayāmyatha |
vidarbhyādyena kāmena manmathāntakṛtaṃ kuru || 71 ||

kandarpasaṃpuṭaṃ kṛtvā koṇagarbhagataṃ tataḥ |
makaradhvajasaṃjñantu kārayet sādhakottamaḥ || 72 ||

p. 822) mīnaketugataṃ kṛtvā bhūrjapatre suśobhane |
rocanāgurukastūrīkarpūrakuṅkumādibhiḥ || 73 ||

sādhyaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ prāṇaṃ kārayeddhemamadhyagam |
japet pañcasahasraṃ vā kāmabījantu sādhakaḥ || 74 ||

mūlaṃ bhānusahasrantu yatnena sādhayedapi |
athavā nābhimadhye tu hṛdaye mukhamaṇḍale || 75 ||

padmarāgapratīkāśaṃ kāmarājaṃ manoharam |
manasā cintayed yastu tasyaiśvaryaṃ bhavet kila || 76 ||

ādhārānnābhiparyantaṃ pradīpasya śikhāṃ smaret |
tasmādudaraparyantaṃ padmarāgasamaprabham || 77 ||

eteṣu vāgbhavaṃ bījaṃ hṛdaye kāmarājakam |
tasyaiva karṇikāmadhye sūryābhāṃ cintayedatha || 78 ||

tasmāt bhrūmaṇḍalaṃ yāvat padmarāgasamaprabham |
raktavastraparīdhāno lākṣārasagṛhe sthitaḥ || 79 ||

raktāsanopaviṣṭastu raktamālyairvibhūṣitaḥ |
paramānandahṛdayastripurīkṛtavigrahaḥ |
evambhūtavapuḥ kāntaṃ kāmarājaṃ vicintayet || 80 ||

[śaktikūṭasādhanam]

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi śaktikūṭasya sādhanam || 81 ||

sṛṣṭisaṃhāraparyantaṃ śarīraṃ cintayet parām |
sravatpīyūṣadhārābhirbalantīṃ viṣahāriṇīm || 82 ||

p. 823) hemaprabhābhāsamānāṃ vidyunnikarasuprabhām |
sphuraccandrakalāpūrṇakamalāṃ varadābhayau || 83 ||

jñānamudrāñca dadhatīṃ sākṣādamṛtarūpiṇīm |
dhyātvā viṣaṃ harenmantrī nānākāravyavasthitam || 84 ||

hṛdi śāntādinānnantaṃ(?) jagadāhlādanaṃ param |
puraḥ kaṇṭhāditālvantaṃ śaktikūṭaṃ vicintayet || 85 ||

ādhārād brahmarandhrāntaṃ tasmādādhārakāvadhi |
atha madhyācchaktikūṭaṃ vidyudagnisamaprabham || 86 ||

kevalaṃ padmarāgantu bhruvormadhye vicintayet |
tasmādābrahmarandhrāntaṃ turīyaṃ padmarāgadhṛk || 87 ||

sahasrārāttu mūlāntaṃ padmarāgasamaprabham |

[kulaśakticintā]

tasmādābrahmarandhrāntaṃ kulaśaktiṃ vicintayet || 88 ||

vainateyasamo mantrī viṣabhārasahasranut |
bhūtapretapiśācāṃśca nāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 89 ||

cāturthakajvarān sarvānapasmārāṃśca nāśayet || 90 ||

[trukūṭasampanna-mahātripurasundarīcintāphalam]

atha trikūṭasampannā mahātripurasundarī |
cintitā sādhakasyāśu trailokyavaśakāriṇī || 91 ||

krameṇa nābhihṛdvaktramaṇḍalasthā'ruṇaprabhā |
padmarāgamaṇisvacchacintanāt sādhakasya ca || 92 ||

p. 824) aṇimādiguṇaiśvaryaṃ saubhāgyañca prajāyate |
tannāma saṃsmaranmantrī yoginīnāṃ bhavet priyaḥ || 93 ||

mātṛcakraṃ tasya kāye tena sārddhaṃ sukhī bhavet |
patravān dhanavānmantrī mantradhyānānna saṃśayaḥ || 94 ||

iti śrīpūrṇānandaparamahaṃsaviracite śrī-tattvacintāmaṇau dvāviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ || 22 ||


trayoviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ

[tripurasundarīvidyāyā jīvanaprāptyupāyakathanam]

mahātripurasundaryā yathā syājjīvanānvitā |
bhuvi vidyā tathedānīṃ vakṣyāmi ca sudurlabhām || 1 ||

[vidyāyāḥ svarūpaviśeṣakathanam]

vidyudā?kṣīṃ parāṃ nityāṃ kālikāṃ kālabhāṣiṇīm |
khaḍgamuṇḍavikaṭāsyāṃ vyāghracarmavibhūṣitām || 2 ||

raktamālyāmbaradharāṃ ghorarūpāṃ caturbhujām |
khaḍgaśūlakapālañca varadāṃ tīkṣṇanāsikām || 3 ||

siddhyarthaṃ jīvayeddevīṃ sarvavidyāsujīvinā || 4 ||

[vidyāyāḥ svarūpāntaram]

raktāmbhodhisthapotollasadaruṇasarojādhirūḍhā karābjaiḥ |
pāśaṃ kodaṇḍamikṣūdbhavakamaṭhamathāpyaṅkuśaṃ pañca bāṇān |

vibhrāṇāsṛkapālaṃ trinayanavilasatpīnavakṣoruhāḍhyā |
devī bālārkavarṇā bhavatu śubhakarī prāṇaśaktiḥ parā naḥ || 5 ||


      1 | maheti | mahātripurasundaryā vidyā yathā bhuvi jīvanānvitā (dīptimatī) syāt, tathā 
idānīṃ tāṃ sudurlabhāṃ vidyāṃ vakṣyāmītyanvayaḥ |

p. 826) [dīpanīvidyākathanam]

śrīṅkāraṃ prathamañcaiva drīṃklīṃkāraṃ tataḥ param |
haiṃ haiṃ kalahrīṃ sauḥ kalahrīṃ tataḥ paṭhet || 6 ||

klīṃ klīṃ tadadho bījaṃ drīṃkāraṃ śrīṃ tataḥ param |
sarveṣāṃ dīpanīvidyā sarvacakreṣu yojitā || 7 ||

[pratimāyāṃ pūjāviśeṣakathanam]

pratimāyāṃ tathā kāryā baliṃ dadyāt samāhitaḥ |
anaṅgakusumādiñca cakramadhye prapūjayet || 8 ||

svayambhu kusumaṃ dattvā kuṇḍagolodbhavaṃ tathā |
pūjayeccakramadhye tu vādyairnānāvidhai rasaiḥ || 9 ||

huṃkāraśabdaiḥ karpūrairgītaiśca candanāruṇaiḥ |
narādibalidānaiśca mahiṣairmeṣachāgalaiḥ || 10 ||

gṛhītvā śoṇitaṃ pātre svakīyahṛdayodbhavam |
pūjayettripurāṃ devīṃ juhuyādagnisammukhe || 11 ||

[devyā dakṣiṇapārśve jīvanījapaprakāraḥ]

devyā dakṣiṇapārśve tu jīvanījapamācaret |
balīnāṃ śoṇitaṃ dattvā kālikāṃ pūjayettataḥ || 12 ||

krodhīśottarasaṃyuktaṃ nādabindukalānvitam |
krodhānantastataḥ paścāt saṃyuktena paṭhet sudhīḥ || 13 ||


	6-7 | śrīṅkāramiti | sarvacakrasādhāraṇīṃ dīpanīmāha-śrīṃkāramityādinā | tathā ca 
śrī/ hrī/ klī/ hai/ hai/ kalahrī/ sauḥ kalahrī/ klī/ klī/ hasauḥ hrī/ śrī/ iti mantro bhavati |

p. 827) pinākī ca susūkṣā ca krodhyānantastataḥ paṭhet |
anilo bhautikaścaiva tadadhaḥ praṇavaṃ likhet || 14 ||

eṣā jñeyā mahāvidyā mahācakrasamanvitā |
mahāmedhāpradā vidyā mahāghoravināśinī || 15 ||

mahāvidyā mahākāmā mahāpretāsanasthitā |
śvetarūpā ca candrasthā bālā ca bhagamālinī || 16 ||

anantarūpamākhyātā sā ca pripurasundarī |
parameyaṃ parā śaktiḥ prathamaṃ pārśvasaṃsthitā || 17 ||

dvitīyā kṣitivikhyātā tatparā paścimottarā |
sā ca devī mahādevī mahākālikasundarī || 18 ||

yasyā merorjagad vyāptaṃ surāsuranarādikam |
mahāpadmāsanā devī mahājīvanakāriṇī || 19 ||

pratimāṃ jīvitāṃ kṛtvā anayā śoṇitaṃ kṣipet |
sarvaduṣṭanivārāñca dhyāyeddevīṃ sulocanām || 20 || 

[pretāsane japaphalam]

pretāsane japed yastu sarvakāmaphalaṃ labhet |
kṣitau ca khyātivikhyāto'sādhyaṃ sādhayet parām || 21 ||

[mahādhyānakathanam]

mahādhyānaṃ pravakṣyāmi parāṃ śaktisvarūpiṇīm |
nīlapadmāsanāṃ devīṃ nīlapadmasamaprabhām || 22 ||

caturbhujāṃ trinayanāṃ raktamālyavibhūṣitām |
vāme khaḍgañca śūlañca vajramaṅkuśadakṣiṇe || 23 ||

p. 828) candramaṇḍalamūrddhānaṃ sūryamaṇḍalasaṃsthitām |
raktavastraparīdhānāṃ raktagandhānulepanām || 24 ||

brahmaviṣṇuśivādīnāṃ śirasā pādasevitām |
unmattayauvanopetāṃ pretasthāṃ liṅgapīḍitām || 25 ||

evaṃrūpāṃ mahādevīṃ cintayet sādhakottamaḥ || 26 ||

[parameśvarīpūjānantaraṃ cakraṃ pūjanam]

anayā vidyayā mantrī saṃpūjya parameśvarīm |
tadā cakre padaṃ kṛtvā pratimāṃ caṇḍikāṃ yajet || 27 ||

saṃhāramudrayā devīṃ sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ |

[tato balidānam]

balidānaṃ tataḥ kuryād yathāśaktyā vidhānataḥ |
ānandarūpavān bhūtvā sundarīputravat kṣitau || 28 ||

kālīṃ kāmāturāṃ bhīmāṃ pracaṇḍagaṇaveṣṭitām |
kālakhaḍgadharāṃ devīṃ rudhiraughañca bhakṣitām || 29 ||

durlabhāṃ paramāṃ devīṃ mahātripurasundarīm |

[ākarṣaṇabījakathanam]

kathayāmi paraṃ bījamākarṣaṇakaraṃ param || 30 ||

viṣṇoḥ padaṃ vahnibījaṃ caturthasvarabhūṣitam |
nādabindukalātītaṃ śaktiṃ visargasaṃyutām || 31 ||

amukī(ṃ?) jīvamākarṣa dviṭhāntaṃ sādhakaḥ paṭhet |

[trikoṇacakramadhye devakanyādilikhanam]

śivarephakayormadhye trikoṇacakramadhyage || 32 ||

p. 829) śiśurūpākarṣakarīṃ devakanyāṃ surāṅganām |
nāgagandharvakanyāñca ṛṣikanyāñca khecarīm || 33 ||

vilikhya sādhakaḥ śrīmānaruṇā(ṃ) cintayet satīm |
hṛdi maulau varāṅge ca javāṃ dattvā ca kanyakām || 34 ||

raktapuṣpaṃ samādāya kuṅkumaṃ gandhavāsitam |
cakramadhye śataṃ dattvā kanyānāma prapūjayet |
samāgacchati sā nārī mārabāṇena pīḍitā || 35 ||

[cāmuṇḍādhyānakathanam]

mahātripurasundaryā mūlavidyā yathoditā |
cāmuṇḍā bheditānāmnī dhyānaṃ vakṣyāmi durlabham || 36 ||

sūryakoṭinibhāṃ bālāṃ trinetrāmaruṇāmbarām |
dhanuḥpustakabāṇākhyadharāṃ bandhūkasannibhām || 37 ||

cakraliṅge samāviṣṭāṃ kāñcīyuktanitambinīm |
stanabhāronnatākārāṃ sindūrapūritānanām || 38 ||

sphuranmukuṭamāṇikyakiṅkiṇījālamaṇḍitām |
muktāhārasamāyuktāṃ tribalīmadhyabhūṣitām || 39 ||

sarvaśṛṅgāraveśāḍhyāṃ kāmāveśanakāriṇīm |
nityānandādibhirddevīṃ pūjayet sādhakottamaḥ || 40 ||

[vaktavya-paramadurlabhavidyā-praśaṃsā]

eṣā ca vidyā kathitā kathayāmi sudurlabhām |

p. 830) yā devī jagati khyātā mahāparvatavāsinī |
paramānandahṛdayaḥ sādhayenmohanātmikām || 41 ||

yasyānandājjagad vyāptaṃ yā devī paramā kalā |
mahāmohanadātrī ca devāsuranarādiṣu || 42 ||

rajyate sakalaṃ nityaṃ tadā devyā carācaram |
duṣṭavairī bhaveddāsa īśaḥ sarvajanasya ca || 43 ||

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi vidyāṃ paramadurlabhām |
śivaśaktisamāyuktāṃ turīyasvarabhūṣitām || 44 ||

nādabindusamāyuktāṃ sarvalokamanoharīm |
rephahakārayormadhye trikoṇe cakramadhyage || 45 ||

sambodhanapadañcaiva sādhyaṃ karmapadānvitam |
pāśāṅkuśapuṭā śaktirnādabinduvibhūṣitā || 46 ||

[prāṇamantrakathanam]

yādyāḥ sapta sakārāntā vyomasargendusaṃyutā |
tadnte haṃsamantraḥ syāttato'muṣyapadaṃ vadet || 47 ||

prāṇā iti vadet paścādiha prāṇāḥ padaṃ tataḥ |
amuṣya jīva ihataḥ sthito'muṣyapadaṃ vadet || 48 ||


	47 | yādyāḥ sapteti | tathāca � yaṃ/ raṃ/ laṃ/ vaṃ/ śaṃ/ ṣaṃ/ saṃ/ haṃ/ saḥ, 
amuṣya prāṇā iha prāṇāḥ, amuṣya jīva iha sthitaḥ, amuṣya sarvendriyāṇi, amuṣya 
vāṅmanaścakṣuḥśrotraghrāṇaprāṇā ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu svāhā iti prāṇamantro 
bhavati |

p. 831) sarvendriyāṇyamuṣyānte vāṅmanaścakṣurantataḥ |
śrotraghrāṇapadāt prāṇā ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciram |
tiṣṭhantu vahnijāyāntaḥ prāṇamantro'yamīritaḥ || 49 ||

pratyamuṣyapadasyādau pāśādīni prayojayet |
amuṣyetipadasthāne sādhyanāma samuccaret || 50 ||

prayogeṣu samākhyātaḥ prāṇamantro manīṣibhiḥ |
sādhyaṃ pratiṣṭhitaprāṇamākarṣaṇādi yojayet || 51 ||

etā vidyāgaṇāḥ sarvāḥ pūjayet sādhakottamaḥ |
bṛhacchīkramatantrasya matametadudīritam || 52 ||

[mahāyantradhāraṇakathanam]

athocyate mahāyantraviśeṣadhāraṇaṃ param |
śubhe'hni snānamācarya śubhe lagne śubhe kṣaṇe || 53 ||

siddhiyoge mahāparve pūjāṃ kṛtvā likhedidam |

[mantralikhanadravyoddeśaḥ]

kuṇḍagolodbhavaṃ dravyaṃ svayambhu kusumaṃ tathā || 54 ||

rocanā kuṅkumaṃ kṣaudraṃ karpūraṃ candanaṃ madam |
etaddravyeṇa vilikheccakrarājaṃ manoharam || 55 ||

[likhanaprakāraḥ]

trikoṇāntaragatāṃ māyāṃ vilikhet sādhakottamaḥ |
rephahakārayormadhye devānāmābhimukhyadam || 56 ||

p. 832) sādhyanāma dvitīyāntaṃ tasyorddhve vilikhenmanum |
tatraiva mātṛkāṃ sarvāṃ vilikhet kramavāhyataḥ || 57 ||

[yantraśodhanaprakāraḥ]

pañcagavyāmṛtenaiva mūlena pariṣecayet |
tataḥ pratiṣṭhitaprāṇāṃ vidyāmaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 58 ||

tat spṛṣṭvā prajapenmantrī devīdhyānaparāyaṇaḥ |

[dhāraṇaprakāraḥ]

hemno madhyagataṃ kṛtvā rajatasyāpi vā punaḥ || 59 ||

kare kṛtvā labhedvaśyaṃ hṛdaye strī suvallabhe |
kaṇṭhe dhanaṃ labhed bhāle stambhanaṃ jṛmbhaṇaṃ bhavet || 60 ||

śikhāyāṃ mokṣamāpnoti tasmād yatnena dhārayet |
dhāraṇīyaṃ prayatnena gopanīyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 61 ||

[mantradāne pātrāpātrollekhaḥ]

asatyāya na dātavyaṃ putraśiṣyāya caiva hi |
bhaktiyuktāya śiṣyāya suputrāya prakāśayet || 62 ||

paśubhyo bāndhavebhyo'pi na kadācit prakāśayet |

[apātre mantradānaphalam]

kṛte tu aśubhaṃ tasya mantrā yānti parāṅmukhāḥ || 63 ||

duḥkhañca varddhate nityaṃ tasmād yatnena varjayet |

[gorocanādinā cakralikhanaphalam]

gorocanādibhirmantrī cakrarājaṃ samālikhet || 64 ||

p. 833) atīva sundarīṃ ramyāṃ tanmadhye pratimāṃ varām |
jvalantīṃ sāmasahitāṃ kāmarājavidarbhitām || 65 ||

[cintāviśeṣe phalaviśeṣaḥ]

cintayet sādhakendro'pi janānāñca sahasraśaḥ |
adṛṣṭapūrvā yuvatī śrutamāśu sudurlabhā || 66 ||

rājakanyā'tha cārvaṅgī bhayalajjāvivarjitā |
āyāti sādhakaṃ samyaṅmantramūḍhā satī dhruvam || 67 ||

cakramadhyagato bhūtvā sādhakaścintayedatha |
udyatsūryasahasrābhamātmānamaruṇantathā || 68 ||

sādhyamapyaruṇībhūtaṃ cintayet sādhakottamaḥ |
anena kramayogena svayaṃ kandarparūpavān || 69 ||

sarvasaundaryasubhagaḥ sarvalokavaśaṅkaraḥ |
sarvaiḥ pūjopahāraistu mudrāsahitavigrahaḥ || 70 ||

[cakre nāmalikhane phalaviśeṣaḥ]

cakraṃ saṃpūjayed yastu yasya nāma vidarbhitam |
sa bhaveddāsavadāśu dhanāḍhyo vā'pi bhūpatiḥ || 71 ||

cakramadhyagataṃ kuryānnāma yasyāntu yojayet |
adṛṣṭāmapi mantrajño yonimudrāsamanvitaḥ || 72 ||

haṭhādānayate śīghraṃ yakṣiṇīṃ rājakanyakām |
nāgakanyāmapsarasaṃ khecarīṃ vā surāṅganām || 73 ||

p. 834) vidyādharīṃ divyarūpāmṛṣikanyāṃ ripustriyam |
madanodbhavasantāpasphurajjaghanamaṇḍalām || 74 ||

kāmabāṇaprabhinnāntaḥkaraṇāṃ lolacakṣuṣam |

[cakre mahākalādhyānaphalam]

mahākāmakalādhyānayogādeva na saṃśayaḥ |
kṣobhayet svargabhūrlokapātālatalayoṣitaḥ || 75 ||

[vaśīkāritilakakathanam]

rocanābhāgamekantu bhāgamekantu kuṅkumam |
atha bhāgadvayaṃ mantrī candanaṃ mardayet samam || 76 ||

ekatra tilakaṃ kuryāttrailokyavaśakāraṇam |
aṣṭottaraśatāvṛttyā mantrayitvā vaśaṃ nayet || 77 ||

rājānaṃ nagaraṃ grāmaṃ yena yad yat pradṛśyate |
mantriṇā sādhakendreṇa tat sarvaṃ tasya vaśyagam || 78 ||

[vaśīkaraṇārthaṃ tāmbūlādyabhimantraṇam]

tāmbūlaṃ dhūpamudakaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ dadhi |
dugdhaṃ ghṛtaṃ dantapatraṃ vastraṃ karpūrameva ca || 79 ||

kastūrīṃ ghusṛṇañcailāṃ lavaṅgaṃ jātipatrakam |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japtvā yasmai kasmai prayacchati || 80 ||

sa vaśyo jāyate nūnaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
striyaśca sakalā vaśyā dāsībhūtā bhavanti hi || 81 ||

haṭhākarṣaṇametattu kathitaṃ nānyathā bhavet |

[yoṣidākarṣaṇaprakāraḥ]

rahasyasthānake mantrī likhedrocanayā bhuvi || 82 ||

p. 835) cāruśṛṅgāraveśāḍhyāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām |
pratimāṃ sundarīṃ prītāṃ vilikhya sumanoharām || 83 ||

tadbhālagalahṛnnābhijanmamaṇḍalayojitām |
janmanāmamahāvidyāmaṅkuśāntavidarbhitām || 84 ||

sarvasandhiṣu dehasya madanākṣaramālikhet |
sphuraddāḍimapuṣpābhaṃ cintayeddehasandhiṣu || 85 ||

tadāśābhimukho bhūtvā svayaṃ devīsvarūpadhṛk |
mudrāntu kṣobhaṇīṃ baddhvā mantramaṣṭaśataṃ japet || 86 ||

niyojya dahanāgāre candrasūryakalātmake |
tato vikalacārvaṅgīṃ mārabāṇaprapīḍitām || 87 ||

evamākarṣayennārīṃ yojanānāṃ śatādapi || 88 ||

[avadhyatvopāyakathanam]

mātṛkāṃ vilikheccakre bāhyataḥ sakalāṃ budhaḥ |
bhūrjapatre svarṇapatre raupyapatre'pi tāmraje |
so'vadhyaḥ sarvajantūnāṃ vyāghrādīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 89 ||

[rājavallabhatopāyaḥ]

tathaiva mārasaṃyuktāṃ sasaṃjñāṃ cakramaṇḍitām |
karpūrakuṅkumādyaiśca rājavallabhatāṃ vrajet || 90 ||

[vaśīkaraṇaprakāraḥ]

anena vidhinā śuddho rocanāgurukuṅkumaiḥ |
vilikheccakra rājena sādhyanāma ca mantravit || 91 ||

p. 836) vidarbhitaṃ svanāmnā tu yasmin kasminnupasthitam |
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ vāpi sakalaṃ janamaṇḍalam || 92 ||

vaśīkuryāt sādhakendro nūnameva na saṃśayaḥ |

[trailokyavaśīkaraṇopāyaḥ]

mahātripurasundaryāḥ kāmakoṭikalā smṛtāḥ || 93 ||

evamevamavaṣṭabhya sādhyanāmākṣarāṇi tu |
dhanamapyarthane bāṇairmātṛkāyāḥ praveṣṭaye || 94 ||

hemamadhyagataṃ kuryācchikhāyāṃ vāmake bhuje |
dhārayed yatra kutrāpi trailokyavaśakāraṇam |
rājendravṛndasakalaṃ dāsabhūtāṃ karoti ca || 95 ||

rājāno vājinaḥ sarpā mahāduṣṭā mahotkaṭāḥ |
vyāghrāḥ keśariṇo mattā vaśyāstasya bhavanti hi || 96 ||

[mahākṣobhakaraṇam]

pūrvakrameṇa nagaranāmasaundaryamantravit |
madhye catuṣpathe vāpi caturdikṣu nidhāpayet || 97 ||

mahākṣobho yoṣitāñca janānāṃ mahatāmapi |
tathaiva sarvajantūnāṃ purasthānañca jāyate || 98 ||

[kāmatulyatvaprāptyupāyaḥ]

etanmadhyagatāṃ pṛthvīṃ saśailavanakānanām |
jvalantīṃ sarvarājendramaṇḍitāṃ sāgarāmbarām || 99 ||

māsamekaṃ cintayeyuḥ sākṣātkāmasamo bhavet |
dṛṣṭyā'karṣayate mantrī trailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 100 ||

p. 837) [dṛṣṭimātreṇa viṣādināśakṣamatāprāptiḥ]

dṛṣṭyā viṣaṃ nāśayate tatkṣaṇānnātra saṃśayaḥ |
bhūtapretapiśācāṃśca jvarāṃścāturthikādikān || 101 ||

śūlagulmākṣirogāṃśca dṛṣṭyā nāśayate kṣaṇāt |

[śrīsamākarṣaṇasāmarthyaprāptiḥ]

etat sindūrasubhagaṃ rātrau saṃpūjayed yadi || 102 ||

yojanānāṃ śatādeva samyagākarṣayecchriyam |

[digvidiksthajanavaśīkāraḥ]

yadā dikṣu vidikṣvevaṃ samyag devīṃ prapūjayet || 103 ||

tatra dikṣu sthitān lokān vidikṣusthān janānapi |
vaśamānayate śīghraṃ saputrapaśubāndhavān || 104 ||

[trailokyavaśīkaraṇam]

bhūrjapatre samālikhya rocanā'gurukuṅkumaiḥ |
tanmadhye nagaraṃ deśaṃ maṇḍalaṃ khaṇḍameva vā || 105 ||

nāmnā vidarbhitaṃ samyak pūjayitvā yathāvidhi |
bhūmimadhyagataṃ kṛtvā trailokyaṃ vaśamānayet || 106 ||

[kaṇṭhādau dhāraṇe phalaviśeṣaḥ]

athavā dhārayet kaṇṭhe śikhāyāṃ bāhumūlake |
yatra kutra sthito mantrī kṣobhayennagaraṃ mahat || 107 ||

[arkakṣīrādibhiścakralikhanaphalam]

arkakṣīreṇa saṃyuktaṃ dhustūrakarasaṃ tathā |
rocanā kuṅkumañcaiva lākṣākastūrīraktakam || 108 ||

p. 838) ebhirlikheccakrametad yasya nāmnā ca sādhakaḥ |
tasya vyāghrabhayavyādhiripusargagajādikam || 109 ||

cauragrahajanāviṣṭaḍākinīśākinībhavam |
bhayaṃ na vidyate nūnaṃ paramantrābhicārakam || 110 ||

[nityaṃ cakradhāraṇaphalam]

nityaṃ sandhārayenmantrī kālamṛtyuṃ vināśayet |
trikoṇamadhyagāṃ devīṃ trikoṇomayamadhyagām || 111 ||

adhastānnāmasaṃyuktāṃ rocanākuṅkumānvitām |
nidhāpayet saptamāhe dāsavat kiṅkaro bhavet || 112 ||

[sarvavādistambhanam]

pītadravyaiḥ samālikhya pītadravyaiḥ samarcayet |
pūrvāśābhimukho bhūtvā stambhayet sarvavādinaḥ || 113 ||

sahasravadano'pyeva mūko bhavati tatkṣaṇāt |
nāmnā yasya sa vāgmī tu pāṣāṇa iva jāyate || 114 ||

[śatrumāraṇaprakāraḥ]

cakraṃ vilikhya mantrajño mahānīrarasena tu |
nāma saṃyojya vidhivad dakṣiṇābhimukho budhaḥ || 115 ||

vahnau dagdhvā tu taccakraṃ mārayed vairisaṅkulam |

[vidveṣaṇakaraṇam]

mahiṣāśvapurīṣābhyāṃ rasamākṛṣya mantravit || 116 ||

gomūtreṇa ca saṃlikhya nāma sandarbhya pūrvavat |
kṣiptvā palāśamadhyasthaṃ vidveṣaṇakaraṃ bhavet || 117 ||

p. 839) [uccāṭanakaraṇam]

kṛtvā rocanayā droṇakākapakṣasya madhyagam |
lambamānaṃ tathākāśe śatrūccāṭanakārakam || 118 ||

[sarvavaśīkaraṇam]

mahānīlīrocanābhyāṃ dugdhakṣārarasādibhiḥ |
vilikhya dhārayenmantrī sarvavarṇān vaśaṃ nayet || 119 ||

[prakārāntareṇa sarvavaśīkaraṇam]

anenaiva vidhānena sthāpayed vārimadhyagam |
tenodakena susnātaḥ pītaṃ tat sarvavaśyakṛt |
saubhāgyaṃ jāyate tena pānīyena na saṃśayaḥ || 120 ||

[kṣobhaṇaprakāraḥ]

etanmadhyagatāṃ pṛthvīṃ nagaraṃ vāpi sādhakaḥ |
saptāhāt kṣobhayet sarvaṃ jvālāmālāṃ vicintayet || 121 ||

[strīvaśīkaraṇaprakāraḥ]

daśadaṇḍagate rātrau sarāve dāhavarjite |
sindūrakuṅkumābhyāñca cakrarājaṃ samālikhet || 122 ||

sādhyapratikṛtiṃ kanyāṃ tanmadhye vilikhed budhaḥ |
tadūrdhve mūlamantrañca tadbāhye mātṛkāṃ likhet || 123 ||

sāvaraṇāṃ sveṣṭadevīṃ tatraiva paripūjayet |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ kundaṃ mūlaṃ sādhyasamanvitam || 124 ||

pratyekakṣepaṇāccaiva haṭhāt kuṇḍamidaṃ mahat |
calajjalendusadṛśīṃ trikoṇāmaruṇāruṇām || 125 ||

p. 840) pralayānalasaṅkāśāṃ sādhyāyā yonimadhyataḥ |
dhyāyan sahasraṃ japtvaiva tāmānayati niścitam || 126 ||

[strīvaśīkaraṇe prakārāntaram]

upaviśya ca śayyāyāṃ nirjane śūnyaveśmani |
yasya nāmnā svayaṃ bhuṅkte tāmbūlaṃ niśi vā divā |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā sāyātyeva tadantikam || 127 ||

[prakārāntareṇa strīvaśīkaraṇam]

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā pānīyaṃ prāśayedatha |
yasyāḥ prāṇaiḥ kalpayitvā sāyāti madavihvalā || 128 ||

[dāsīkaraṇaprakāraḥ]

candra-candana-kastūrī-raktasindūramiśritam |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā tilakaṃ kārayed yadi |
yaṃ yaṃ paśyati tacchīghraṃ sa sa dāso bhaved dhruvam || 129 ||

[strīvaśīkāre kalpāntaram]

madhusarpiḥ samunmiśrya sitākalpitaputtalīm |
sādhyaprāṇaiḥ kṛtaprāṇāṃ japtvā aṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 130 ||

bhānuvāraṃ samāsādya yaḥ khādet saptarātrakam |
sā ca yāti vihvalāṅgī sādhakasyāntikaṃ dhruvam || 131 ||

[sarvavādistambhanam]

pūrvāśābhimukho bhūtvā pītadravyaiḥ samarcayet |
pīṭhasthāne samālikhya stambhayet sarvavādinaḥ || 132 ||

p. 841) [prakārāntareṇa sarvalokavaśīkaraṇam]

uttarābhimukho bhūtvā sindūrarajasā likhet |
saṃpūjya vidhivad vidvān sarvalokaṃ vaśaṃ nayet || 133 ||

[sarvayoṣinmanoharaṇam]

paścimābhimukho bhūtvā candanena samālikhet |
saṃpūjya vidhivad vidyāṃ sarvayoṣinmano haret || 134 ||

vallabho jāyate mantrī dāsīmiva vaśaṃ nayet |

[mahāhānikaraṇam]

yamāśābhimukho bhūtvā cakraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ samarccayet || 135 ||

yasya nāmnānvitaṃ tatra mahāhāniḥ prajāyate |

[stambhavidveṣaṇādiprakāraḥ]

agni-rākṣasa-vāyavya-śambhu-koṇeṣu yojitam || 136 ||

pūrvavat sādhakaśreṣṭhaḥ krameṇaiva niyojitam |
stambhavidveṣaṇavyādhiśatrūccāṭanakaṃ bhavet || 137 ||

[sarvavaśīkaraṇam]

rocanābhirlikheccakraṃ kṣīramadhye kṣiped budhaḥ |
sarvavaśyakarañcaiva bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 138 ||

[śatroruccāṭanakaraṇam]

gomūtramadhyagaṃ samyak śatroruccāṭanaṃ bhavet |

[vidveṣaṇakaraṇam]

tailasthaṃ cakrarājantu vidveṣaṇakaraṃ bhavet || 139 ||

[śatrunāśakaraṇam]

jvalajjvalanamadhyasthaṃ śatrunāśakaraṃ bhavet |

p. 842) [khecaratvasiddhiḥ]

yadaikānte caturmārge sindūrarajasā likhet || 140 ||

sarvabāhyata ārabhya yāvanmadhyañca sādhakaḥ |
akārādikṣakārāntāṃ mātṛkāṃ tatra saṃlikhet || 141 ||

pūjayedrātrisamaye kulācārakrameṇa tu |
sādhakaḥ khecaraścaiva jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 142 ||

[ajarāmaratvaprāptiḥ]

girāvekatarau tadvadarccayet kulamārgataḥ |
ajarāmaratāṃ labdhvā sukhī bhavati māntrikaḥ || 143 ||

[khaḍgādi sarvasiddhilābhaḥ]

śmaśāne pūjayeccakraṃ mahābhūtadine budhaḥ |
yathāvidhividhānena sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 144 ||

khaḍgasiddhiñca vetālasiddhiñca guṭikāñjanam |
pādukāñjanasiddhiñca manaḥsiddhiñca dhātujam || 145 ||

mahāvīraṇasiddhiñca yakṣiṇīṃ caṭakodbhavām |
sarvaṃ tallabhate mantrī nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 146 ||

[sarvapāpanāśaprakāraḥ]

śivāṃ saṃpūjayeddevīṃ sagandhikusumaiḥ sudhīḥ |
mahāpātakayuktātmā tatkṣaṇāt pāpahā bhavet || 147 ||

śamīdūrvāṅkurāśvatthapallavairathavā'mbujaiḥ |
māsena hanti kaluṣaṃ saptajanmakṛtaṃ naraḥ || 148 ||

iti śrīpūrṇānandaparamahaṃsaviracite śrī-tattvacintāmaṇau trayoviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ || 23 ||



caturviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ

[devatāstavapāṭhanirṇayaḥ]

athocyate devatāyāḥ stavapāṭhavinirṇayaḥ |

[pāṭhasyāvaśyakatā]

na mānasaṃ bhavet stotraṃ vācikantu praśasyate || 1 ||

[pāṭhaniyamaḥ]

ante ca praṇavaṃ dadyādityuvācādipūrvakam |
sarvatra pāṭhe vijñeyamanyathā viphalaṃ bhavet |
śuddhenānanyacittena paṭhitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 2 ||

[pustakasyādhārasthāpananiyamaḥ]

ādhāre sthāpayitvā tu pustakaṃ prajapet sudhīḥ |

[haste pustakasthāpane doṣaḥ]

hastasaṃsthāpanādeva yasmādalpaphalaṃ bhavet || 3 ||

[pustakalekhakanirṇayaḥ]

na svayaṃ likhitaṃ tacca kṛtinā likhitaṃ paṭhet |
abrāhmaṇena likhitaṃ taccāpi viphalaṃ bhavet || 4 ||

[kalau sādhāraṇamanuṣyādikṛtastavapāṭhaniṣedhaḥ]

na ca svayaṃkṛtaṃ stotramanyenāpi kṛtaṃ na ca |
yasmāt kalau na praśastamṛṣibhirbhāṣitaṃ paṭhet || 5 ||

[ṛṣicchanda-ādīnāṃ pāṭhaḥ karttavyaḥ]

ṛṣicchandādikaṃ nyasya paṭhet stotraṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || 6 ||

p. 844) [prathamaṃ sahasranāmapāṭhaḥ]

yasya yasya ca devasya tasya tasya sahasrakam |
paṭhitvā prathamaṃ mantrī tasya stotraṃ tataḥ paṭhet || 7 ||

saṃhitājapane stotraṃ śubhaṃ prāpya samāpayet |

[adhyāyamadhye pāṭhavirāmo na karttavyaḥ]

adhyāyaṃ prāpya viramenna tu madhye kadācana |
kṛte virāme madhye tu adhyāyādiṃ punarjapet || 8 ||

[balidānavidhikathanam]

athāto balidānasya vidhānamatra vakṣyate |
camaraḥ kṛṣṇasāraśca matsyo meṣaśca śūkaraḥ || 9 ||

mahiṣo vānaraḥ khaḍgo balirmeṣādito bhavet |
kṛṣṇacchāgadvayaṃ dadyāt paścāddadyānmahābalim || 10 ||

[chāgalasya ṣaḍvidhatvakathanam]

śvetaḥ kṛṣṇo lohitaśca kapilaścitrapāṇḍarau |
ṣaḍvidhaśchāgalaḥ prokto gṛhasthānāṃ paro baliḥ || 11 ||

[balīnāmuttamamadhyamādhamatvakathanam]

uttamau śvetakṛṣṇau ca madhyamau citrapāṇḍarau |
lohitaḥ kapilaścaiva kaniṣṭhaḥ parikīrttitaḥ || 12 ||

trimāsāt parataścaiva yāvadekantu vatsaram |
kṛṣṇo vā śvetataruṇaḥ praśasto balirucyate || 13 ||

ṣaṇmāsāt parataścaiva yāvad varṣadvayaṃ bhavet |
uttamo'sau baliḥ proktastasmāddhīno'dhamaḥ smṛtaḥ || 14 ||

p. 845) [tyājyabaliḥ]

kṛṣṇavarṇo baliryastu śvetapuccho yadā bhavet |
chāgalaṃ tādṛśaṃ vidvān dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 15 ||

evaṃ site kṛṣṇapucche varjayenmatimān naraḥ || 16 ||

ākṛṣṇavarṇo yaśchāgaḥ pṛṣṭhavaṃśe sito yadi |
praśastaṃ taṃ vijānīyād viparītantu varjayet || 17 ||

[khañjādīnāṃ balau tyājyatā]

khañjaṃ kāṇaṃ tathā kubjaṃ sacchidraṃ vyādhisaṃyutam |
viṣāṇadantarahitaṃ varjayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 18 ||

[uttamamahiṣabaliḥ]

pañcābdaṃ māhiṣaṃ śvetaṃ praśastaṃ parikīrttitam |

[adhamamahiṣabaliḥ]

sa eva mahiṣaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ pāṇḍaraścādhamo baliḥ || 19 ||

[baliṣu pakṣiṇāṃ praśastatā]

pakṣiṇastu praśastāḥ syuḥ sarve varṇāḥ suśobhanāḥ |
tripakṣāt parataścaiva hīne cāpi vivarjayet || 20 ||

[baliviśeṣeṇa devatātṛptikālakathanam]

manuṣyaiḥ koṭivarṇāṇi tṛptiṃ gacchanti devatāḥ |
gaṇḍairvarṣasahasrāṇi mahiṣaiḥ śaradāṃ śatam || 21 ||

chagalairvatsarādūrddhvaṃ dvādaśābdaṃ daśābdakam |
pūrṇasāṃvatsareraiva trivarṣaṃ kacchapena tu || 22 ||

varāheṇa tu pañcāśattadarddhaṃ cāmareṇa tu |
abdaikaṃ godhikābhiśca ṣaṇmāsaṃ gātraśoṇitam || 23 ||

p. 846) pāyasenāpi ṣaṇmāsaṃ tṛptiṃ gacchanti devatāḥ || 24 ||

[strīpaśubalidānaniṣedhaḥ]

na dadyāt strīpaśormāṃsaṃ havyakavye viśeṣataḥ |

[home māṃsanirūpaṇam]

home bukkāgrajaṃ māṃsaṃ saromañcārddharātrake || 25 ||

siṃhavilvākṛtiñcaiva vastu ca pūgameva ca |
bukkāgramāṃsamānaṃ syāllulāpasya narasya ca || 26 ||

tathā pramāṇamātraṃ syādajasya vadanasya ca |
dhātakīphalamānantu godhikāyā itīritam || 27 ||

etadāmamayasyaiva bharjite mānavarjitam |
hīnendriyapaśormāṃsaṃ dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 28 ||

[māṃsahome kālaniyamaḥ]

divā na juhuyānmāṃsaṃ sandhyāyāṃ sāvasānake |
brāhmye muhūrte juhuyādathavā sandhiparvasu || 29 ||

ekena ca sahasraṃ syād dviguṇaṃ cārddharātrake |
brāhmye muhūrtte dviguṇaṃ sandhyāyāñca caturguṇam |
parvasandhau śataguṇaṃ tithisandhau śatottaram || 30 ||

kāmye ca juhuyānmāṃsaṃ puraścaraṇaśeṣakaiḥ |
yonyāvaṭe śataguṇaṃ sthaṇḍile'pi na cācaret || 31 ||

[balyutsargaprakāraḥ]

atha saṃprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā baliṃ samutsṛjed budhaḥ |
svayaṃ pūrvamukho bhūtvā baliṃ kuryādudaṅmukham || 32 ||

p. 847) athavaitadvyatyayaḥ syāt sindūrādyairalaṅkṛtam |
arghyapātrodakaiḥ śuddhairmūlena prokṣayettriśaḥ || 33 ||

[paśusnāpanamantraḥ]

oṃ vārāhī yamunā gaṅgā kāratoyā sarasvatī |
kāverī candrabhāgā ca sindhubhairavasāgarāḥ |
ajasnāne maheśāni sānnidhyamiha kalpaya || 34 ||

oṃ paśupāśavināśāya hemakūṭasthitāya ca |
parāparāya parameṣṭhine hū/ kārāya ca mūrttaye || 35 ||

oṃ sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt |
sa bhūmiṃ sarvato vṛtvā atyatiṣṭhaddaśāṅgulam || 36 ||

vilvapatrodakairebhiḥ prokṣaṇaṃ kārayedpi |
triśaḥ saṃpūjayettañca tatreṣṭaṃ gandhacandanaiḥ || 37 ||

tato nirīkṣya taṃ mantrī paṭhet karapuṭaṃ svayam || 38 ||

[balipraṇāmamantraḥ]

chāga tvaṃ balirūpeṇa mama bhāgyādupasthitaḥ |
praṇamāmi tataḥ sarvarūpiṇaṃ balirūpiṇam || 39 ||

caṇḍikāprītidānena dāturāpadvināśanam |
cāmuṇḍābalirūpāya bale tubhyaṃ namo'stu te || 40 ||

yajñārthe paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayameva svayambhuvā |
atastvāṃ ghātayiṣyāmi tasmād yajñe vadho'vadhaḥ || 41 ||

[balimūrddhni mantrajapaḥ]

vāṅmāyākamalābījaistanmūrddhni trirmanūn japet || 42 ||

p. 848) tato baliṃ samutsṛjya kāmyaṃ kṛtvā viśāladhīḥ |
ānīya khaḍgaṃ purato gandhādibhiḥ samarccayet || 43 ||

[khaḍgadhyānam]

kṛṣṇaṃ piṇākapāṇiñca kālarātrisvarūpiṇam |
ugraṃ raktāsyanayanaṃ gandhānulepanam || 44 ||

raktāmbaradharañcaiva pāśahastaṃ kuṭumbinam |
pibamānañca rudhiraṃ bhuñjānaṃ kravyasaṃhatim || 45 ||

[khaḍgapūjāmantraḥ]

iti dhyātvā yajet tañca mantreṇānena yājakaḥ |
rasanā tvaṃ caṇḍikāyāḥ suralokaprasādaka || 46 ||

māyādvayaṃ khaḍgapadaṃ kāli kāli padantataḥ |
vajreśvarī tato lauhadaṇḍāyai ṅe'ntāṃ namaḥ padam || 47 ||

kūrccabījaṃ likhet khaḍge sindūracandanādinā |
devyā bījaṃ likhettatra pūjayitvā namaścaret || 48 ||

iti dhyātvā sādhakastu namaskāraṃ samācaret || 49 ||

[khaḍganamaskāramantraḥ]

asirviśasanaḥ khaḍgastīkṣṇadhāro durāsadaḥ |
śrīgarbho vijayaścaiva dharmapāla namo'stute || 50 ||

tataḥ khaḍgaṃ gṛhītvā tu devatārasanādhiyā |
abhimantrya ca mūlena vakṣyamāṇena ca svayam || 51 ||

p. 849) viṣaṃ māyādvayaṃ kāliyugaṃ vikaṭarūpataḥ |
daṃṣṭre sphe/ sphe/ tato brūyāt phetkāriṇīti khādaya || 52 ||

dvitayaṃ chedayayugaṃ sarvān duṣṭāṃstataḥ param |
mārayadvitayaṃ paścādajaṃ khaḍgena chindhiyuk || 53 ||

kāli kāli kicidvandvaṃ pibayugrudhiraṃ padam |
sphrau/ sphrau/ kiliyugaṃ kāliyugmamityabhimantraṇe |

[baliskandhe khaḍganiyojanamantraḥ]

hū/ hū/ phaḍiti mantreṇa skandhe khaḍgaṃ niyojayet || 54 ||

[khaḍgalakṣaṇakathanam]

atha vakṣyāmi khaḍgānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tantravartmanā |
yena saṃchedanenāpi sādhakaḥ siddhimālabhet || 55 ||

[candrahāsalakṣaṇakathanam]

sarveṣāmuttamaḥ khaḍgaścandrahāsaḥ sukhāvahaḥ |
pañcāśadaṅgulaiḥ kāryaḥ kiṃvā tālatrayeṇa tu || 56 ||

[khaḍgam karabīraphalākārādikathanam]

karabīraphalākāraḥ kiṃvā padmadalākṛtiḥ |
athavā kubjakaḍgena bhedayet khalu sādhakaḥ || 57 ||

      
      52-54 | viṣamiti | tathāca - oṃ hrī/ hrī/ kāli kāli vikaṭarūpadaṃṣṭre spheṃ spheṃ 
phetkāriṇi khādaya khādaya chedaya chedaya sarvān duṣṭān māraya māraya ajaṃ khaḍgena 
chindhi chindhi kāli kāli kici kici piva piba rudhiraṃ sphrau/ sphrau/ kili kili kāli kāli hū/ hū/ 
phaṭ iti mantreṇa baleḥ skandhe khaḍgaṃ sthāpayet ityarthaḥ | 

p. 850) [balicchedane dātrādīnāṃ vidhiḥ]

mahābalīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ dātramatra prakīrttitam |

[churikādinā chedananiṣedhaḥ]

karttṛkayā kṣureṇāpi chindyācchurikayā na ca || 58 ||

kuṭhāraparaśubhyāṃ vā bhedayenna kadācana |

[tīkṣṇakhaḍgena chedanakathanam]

chedayettīkṣṇakhaḍgena acchidreṇa mukhena ca || 59 ||

nākāśe cchedayettantu same deśe śucau tathā |
chedayedaniśaṃ śastaṃ baliṃ pūrvamukhaṃ śubham |
udaṅmukhaḥ svayaṃ netrabījenaiva tu sādhakaḥ || 60 ||

[chāgādipaśūnāṃ pūrvābhimukhena cchedanam]

chāgalādipaśūnāñca viparītamukhena tu |

[balicchedakakathanam]

prahārāṇāñcādhikārī teṣāmevaṃ vidhīyate || 61 ||

svayaṃ vā bhrātṛputro vā bhrātā vā suhṛdeva vā |
sapiṇḍaśchedayed vāpi śuddhāntaḥkaraṇo yadi || 62 ||

[ripuprabhṛtyanupayuktānāṃ kathanam]

na ripuṃ nāṅgahīnaṃ vā na kāṇaṃ nāpi kubjakam |
na yojayecchatrupakṣaṃ balicchedanakarmaṇi || 63 ||
 

	60 | nākāśe iti | śūnyasthāne baliṃ na cchedayet | netrabījena vauṣaṭ itimantreṇa |

p. 851) [digviśeṣe balermastakapatane phalaviśeṣaḥ]

yadi vāyavyadigbhāge nair-ṛte dakṣiṇe'pi vā |
mastakaṃ patate caiva hānimatra vinirddiśet || 64 ||

śeṣeṣu dikṣu sarvāsu vidikṣu ca phalaṃ śṛṇu |
pūrvottaravidigbhāge patate yadi mastakam |
sarvasampatkaraṃ vidyād rājñāṃ rājyaṃ vinirdiśet || 65 ||

bhramitvā dakṣiṇenāpi yadā patati vai śiraḥ |
tadā siddhiñca māsānte vāmakarṇe vipad bhavet || 66 ||

rājñāmagnau tathā hānirgṛhasthānāṃ śubhaṃ bhavet || 67 ||

aiśānyamadhyayoścaiva patate yadi mastakam |
tadā svalpena kālena sarvasiddhirbhaved dhruvā || 68 ||

chinnaṃ śiro nipatitaṃ sahāsyaṃ yadi dṛśyate |
vibhūtiṃ tasya jānīyādrājñāṃ rājyaṃ vinirddiśet || 69 ||

ekavāre bhavet siddhirdvivāre hānirucyate |
gṛhasthānāṃ vijānīyādrājñāntu viparītakam || 70 ||

[balicakṣuṣi aśrudarśanaphalam]

yadāśru dṛśyate netre tadā hāniṃ vinirddiśet |

[dantānāṃ kaṭakaṭāśabdaśravaṇaphalam]

yadā kaṭakaṭāśabdo dantānāṃ śrūyate kvacit |
tadā tu maraṇaṃ vidyāddhāniṃ vā tatra nirddiśet || 71 ||


	67 | rājñāmiti | agnau pūrvadakṣiṇakoṇe |

p. 852) [baliśiraḥsthāpanam]

dadyādrākṣasadigbhāge mantrī baliśiraḥ svayam |

[raktapātrasthāpanam]

taddakṣiṇe raktapātraṃ viparītaṃ na saṃdadet || 72 ||

mahābalīnāṃ vāyavye pradadyāt sādhakottamaḥ |
balīnāmarisaṃjñānāṃ śirāṃsi purato dadet || 73 ||

devasya sammukhaṃ kṛtvā cchinnabhāgān prakalpayet |
tadeva śastaṃ jānīyānmadhyamaṃ viparītakam || 74 ||

kīlakānte nyasecchirṣaṃ yathā kampo na jāyate |

[balimastakopari dīpadānam]

śirṣopari jvaladdīpaṃ pradadyāccandanokṣitam || 75 ||

ghṛtena sārvabhaumaḥ syāttaile siddhiñca vindati |
sūtravarttīn pradadyād vā vastravarttyā pradṛśyate || 76 ||

[varttikāpramāṇam]

aṅguṣṭhasya pramāṇantu dīrghe caiva ṣaḍaṅgulam |
kārpāsasūtrairvā dadyādekaviṃśatisūtrakaiḥ || 77 ||

sugandhagarbhaistat kuryāt karpūrāgurucandanaiḥ || 78 ||

[grāhādīnāṃ śirasi dīpadānaniṣedhaḥ]

grāhāṇāṃ kacchapānāñca godhāyāśca viśeṣataḥ |
matsyānāmapi pakṣiṇāṃ na dīpaṃ dāpayecchire || 79 ||

[dīpasya dīrghakālajvalanaphalam]

śīrṣopari jvaladdīpo yāvatkālaṃ pravarttate |
tāvatkālaṃ vaset svarge tasmād yatnena dāpayet || 80 ||

p. 853) lomadāhodbhavaṃ gandhaṃ dhyātvā devī prasīdati |
tasmin saṃvarddhayeddevīṃ tasmāt pātraṃ vivarjayet || 81 ||

nirvāṇe mṛtyumāpnoti nīyamāne pradīpake |
na kṣīraśoṇitaṃ dadyānna cājyañca kadācana || 82 ||

śarkarākṣatadūrvādi pradadyāttrirmahāśatam |
saindhavaṃ śṛṅgaverañca kadalyāḥ phalameva ca || 83 ||

pātayenmadhurāṇāñca śṛtamuddiśya sādhakaḥ |
baliḥ pradāne tūddiṣṭo vārāhīsaṃhitākramāt || 84 ||

iti śrīpūrṇānandaparamahaṃsaviracite śrī-tattvacintāmaṇau caturviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ || 24 ||



pañcaviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ

[śrīmahātripurasundarī-sahasranāmastotram]

atha śrīmanmahādevyāḥ sahasraṃ nāma kathyate |
kailāsaśikhare ramye nānāratnopaśobhite || 1 ||

kalpapādapamadhyasthe siddhagandharvasevite |
maṇimaṇḍapamadhyasthe nānāpuṣpopaśobhite || 2 ||

taṃ kadācit samāsīnaṃ bhagavantaṃ jagadgurum |
kapālakhaṭvāṅgadharaṃ candrārddhakṛtaśekharam || 3 ||

triśūlaḍamarukaraṃ mahāvṛṣabhavāhanam |
jaṭājūṭadharaṃ devaṃ vāsukikaṇṭhabhūṣaṇam || 4 ||

vibhūtibhūṣaṇaṃ devaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ trilocanam |
dvīpicarmaparīdhānaṃ śuddhasphaṭikasannibham || 5 ||

sahasrādityasaṅkāśaṃ girijārddhāṅgabhūṣaṇam |
praṇamya śirasā nāthaṃ kāraṇaṃ viśvarūpiṇam |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā prāha taṃ śikhivāhanaḥ || 6 ||

kārttikeya uvāca |

deva deva mahādeva sṛṣṭisthityantakāraka |
tvaṃ gatiḥ sarvadevānāṃ tvaṃ gatiḥ sarvadehinām || 7 ||

tvaṃ gatiḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ dīnānāñca tvameva ca |
tvameva jagadādhārastvameva viśvakārakaḥ || 8 ||

p. 855) tvameva pūjyaḥ sarveṣāṃ tvadanyā nāsti me gatiḥ |
kiṃ guhyaṃ paramaṃ loke kimekaṃ sarvasiddhidam || 9 ||

kimekaṃ paramaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kiṃ bhogaṃ svargamokṣadam |
vinā tīrthena tapasā vinā vedyairvinā makhaiḥ || 10 ||

vinā layena dhyānena kena siddhimavāpnuyāt |
kasmādutpadyate sṛṣṭiḥ kasmiṃśca pralayo bhavet || 11 ||

kasmāduttīryate deva saṃsārārṇavasaṅkaṭāt |
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi kathayasva maheśvara || 12 ||

śrīmahādeva uvāca |

sādhu sādhu tvayā pṛṣṭaṃ tattvaṃ pārvatīnandana |
iti guhyatamaṃ putra kathayiṣyāmyasaṃśayaḥ || 13 ||

sattvaṃ rajastamaścaiva brahmaviṣṇuśivādayaḥ |
ye cānye bahavo bhūtāḥ sarve prakṛtisambhavāḥ || 14 ||

saiva devī parā śaktirmahātripurasundarī |
saiva prasūyate sarvaṃ saiva viśvaṃ prapālyate || 15 ||

saiva saṃharate viśvaṃ jagadetaccarācaram |
ādhāraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ sā vai rogārttihāriṇī || 16 ||

icchā kriyā tathā jñānā brahmaviṣṇuśivātmikā |
tridhā śaktisvarūpeṇa sṛṣṭisthitivināśinī || 17 ||

sṛjyate brahmarūpeṇa viṣṇurūpeṇa pālyate |
saṃharedrudrarūpeṇa jagatīṃ sacarācarām || 18 ||

p. 856) yasyā yonau jagat sarvamadyāpi varttate'khilam |
yasyāṃ pralīyate cāntye yasyāñca jāyate punaḥ || 19 ||

yāṃ samārādhya trailokye saṃprāptaṃ padamuttamam |
tasyā nāmasahasraṃ te kathayāmi śṛṇuṣva tat || 20 ||

      oṃ asyā śrīmahātripurasundaryāḥ sahasranāmastotrasya dakṣiṇā-mūrttirbhagavān 
vāmadeva ṛṣirgāyattrīcchandaḥ prakaṭaguhyatarasaṃpra-
dāyakulakaulikagarbharahasyātirahasyaparāpararahasyācintyaprabhāvavatī śrīmahātripurasundarī 
devatā ākāśabījaṃ māyā śaktiḥ kāmabījaṃ kīlakaṃ buddhiśaktirudānabījaṃ suṣumṇā 
nāḍī sarasvatīśaktisiddhyarthakāmamokṣārthe viniyogaḥ || 21 ||

hrī/ kalyāṇī kamalā kālī karālī kāmarūpiṇī |
kāmākhyā kāmadā kāmyā kamalā kāmacāriṇī || 22 ||

kālarātrirmahārātriḥ kaulinī kaulyarūpiṇī |
kumārī karuṇā kīrttiḥ kalmāṣanāśinīti ca || 23 ||

kātyāyanī kalādhārā kaumudī kamalāpriyā |
kīrttidā muktidā medhā nītijñā nītivatsalā || 24 ||

māheśvarī mahāmāyā mahātejā maheśvarī |
mahālolā mahājihvā mahādaṃṣṭrā mahābhujā || 25 ||

mahāmohāndhakāraghnī mahāmokṣaprakāśinī |
mahādāridryaśamanī mahāśatruvināśinī || 26 ||

mahāśaktirmahāśūramahiṣāsuramarddinī |
mahāmāyā mahāvīryā mahāpātakanāśinī || 27 ||

p. 857) mahākāmā mahākālyā mahāpātakahāriṇī |
mahāmantrā mantramayī maṇipūrakavāsinī || 28 ||

mānasī mānadā mānyā manaścakṣuragocarā |
gaṇanītā ca gāyattrī gaṇagandharvasevitā || 29 ||

girijā giriśā sādhvī giristhā girisambhavā |
caṇḍeśvarī caṇḍarūpā pracaṇḍā caṇḍamālinī || 30 ||

carccitā carccitakalā caṇḍikā caṇḍarūpiṇī |
yajñeśvarī yajñarūpā japayajñaparāyaṇā || 31 ||

yajñamātā yajñabhokttrī yajñeśī yajñasambhavā |
siddhā yajñapriyā siddhiryajñāṅgī yajñabandhikā || 32 ||

yajñapriyā yajñarūpā yājñī yajñakṛtālayā |
jālandharā jaganmātā jātavedā jagatpriyā || 33 ||

jitendriyā jitakrodhā jananī janmadāyinī |
gaṅgā godāvarī gaurī gomatī ca śatadrukā || 34 ||

ghargharā vedagarbhā ca devikākarasambhavā |
sindhurmandākinī viprā yamunā ca sarasvatī || 35 ||

candrabhāgā vipāśā ca gaṇḍakī vindhyavāsinī |
narmadā kadrukāverī vetravatī ca kauṣikī || 36 ||

mahendratanayā caiva ahalyā campakāvatī |
ayodhyā mathurā māyā kāśī kāñcī avantikā || 37 ||

dvārāvatī ca śikhinī mahākilviṣanāśinī |
padminī padmahastā ca padmakiñjalkavāsinī || 38 ||

p. 858) padmavaktrā ca padmākṣī padmasthā padmasambhavā |
drī/kārī kuṇḍalī rātrirhṛtpadmasthā sulocanā || 39 ||

ai/kārī bhūṣaṇā lakṣmīḥ klī/kārī kleśanāśinī |
haripriyā harermātā harinetrakṛtāśrayā || 40 ||

harivaktrodbhavā śāntā harervakṣaḥsthalasthitā |
vaiṣṇavī viṣṇurūpā ca viṣṇumāyāsvarūpiṇī || 41 ||

viṣṇumāyā viśālākṣī viśālanayanojjvalā |
viśveśvarī ca viśvātmā viśveśī viśvarūpiṇī || 42 ||

viśvātmikā viśvavidyā viśvapāśavidāriṇī |
viśvāsakāriṇī viśvā vidhiśaktirvicakṣaṇā || 43 ||

śiveśvarī śivārādhyā śivanāthā śivapriyā |
śivamātā śivākhyā ca śivadā śivarūpiṇī || 44 || 

bhaveśvarī bhavārādhyā bhavānī bhavanāyikā |
bhavamātā bhāvagamyā bhavakaṇṭakanāśinī || 45 ||

bhavapriyā bhavānandā bhavakaṇṭakahāriṇī |
gatirvareṇyā sāvitrī brahmāṇī brahmarūpiṇī || 46 ||

brahmeśī brahmadā brāhmī brahmāṇī brahmavādinī |
durgasthā durgarūpā ca durgā durgārttināśinī || 47 ||

sugamā durgamā dātā dayā gaurī durāpahā |
duritaghnī duradhyakṣā dūraduṣkṛtanāśinī || 48 ||

pañcamyā pañcamī pūrṇā pūrṇapīṭhanivāsinī |
sattvasthā sattvarūpā ca kalpasthā kalparūpiṇī || 49 ||

p. 859) nānāratnavicitrāṅgī nānābharaṇabhūṣitā |
viśvāmbikā viśvamātā viśvapāśavidāriṇī || 50 ||

viśālakāriṇī viśvā viśvaśaktirvilakṣaṇā |
javākusumasaṅkāśā dāḍimīkusumāruṇā || 51 ||

caturaṅgī caturvāhā caturdvāravyahāsinī |
sarveśī sarvadā sarvā sarvajñā sarvadāyinī || 52 ||

viśveśvarī ca sarvādyā sarvāṇī sarvamaṅgalā |
nalinī nandinī nandā ānandānandavarttinī || 53 ||

vyāpinī sarvabhūteṣu bhavabhāravināśinī |
kulīnā kulamadhyasthā kuladharmopadeśinī || 54 ||

sarvaśṛṅgāraveśāḍhyā pāśāṅkuśakarodyatā |
sūryā sūryasahasrābhā candrakoṭinibhānanā || 55 ||

gaṇeśakoṭilāvaṇyā siṃhakoṭivimarddinī |
dāvāgnikoṭijvalinī kadrukoṭisvarūpiṇī || 56 ||

samudrakoṭigambhīrā vāyukoṭimahābalā |
ākāśakoṭivistārā yamakoṭibhayaṅkarā || 57 ||

merukoṭisamucchrāyā garutkoṭisamṛddhidā |
niṣkalaṅkā nirādhārā nirguṇā guṇavarjitā || 58 ||

viśiṣṭā viśvajananī viśvākhyā viśvavarddhinī |
citrā vicitrā citrāṅgī hetugarbhā raṇeśvarī || 59 ||

icchāśaktirjñānaśaktiḥ kriyāśaktiḥ śucismitā |
śrutiḥ śrutimayī satyā śrutirūpā śrutipriyā || 60 ||

p. 860) śrutiprājñā mahātattvā pañcatattvoparisthitā |
pārvatī himavatputrī pārasthā pārarūpiṇī || 61 ||

jayantī bhadrakālī ca ahalyā kulanāyikā |
bhūtadhātrī ca bhūteśī bhūtasthā bhūtabhāvinī || 62 ||

mahākulamayī śaktirmahāvibhavavarddhinī |
haṃsākhyā haṃsarūpā ca haṃsasthā haṃsarūpiṇī || 63 ||

somasūryāgnimadhyasthā mūlādhāranivāsinī |
dvādaśārasarojasthā sūryamaṇḍalavāsinī || 64 ||

dvipatradalamadhyasthā lalāṭatalavāsinī |
ḍākinī rākiṇī caiva lākinī kākinī tathā || 65 ||

śākinī hākinī caiva ṣaṭcakravinivāsinī |
sṛṣṭisthitivināśā ca sṛṣṭisthityantakāriṇī || 66 ||

śrīkaṇṭhapriyā śrīkaṇṭhā nādākhyā bindumālinī |
catuḥṣaṣṭikalādhārā dehadaṇḍakalāśrayā || 67 ||

māyā kālī dyutirmedhā svadhā puṣṭirmahādhṛtiḥ |
hiṅgulā maṅgalā sītā suṣumṇāmadhyagāminī || 68 ||

parā ghorā karālākṣī vijayā jayaśālinī |
hṛtpadmanilayā caiva bhīmā bhairavanādinī || 69 ||

menakāgarbhasambhūtā taptakāñcanasannibhā |
antakūṭabījasthā ca trikūṭavanavāsinī || 70 ||

varṇākṣī varṇarahitā pañcāśadvarṇarūpiṇī |
vidyādharī lokadhātrī amarā apsaraḥpriyā || 71 ||

p. 861) dākṣā prajāvatī dhanyā dhanadhānyasamṛddhidā |
īśānī bhuvaneśānī bhuvanā bhuvaneśvarī || 72 ||

anantā'nantamahimā jagatsārā jagadbhavā |
acintyaśakticittātmā cintā cittasvarūpiṇī || 73 ||

jñānagamyā jñānamūrttirjñāninī jñānaśālinī |
asitā ghorarūpā ca sudhādhārā sudhāvahā || 74 ||

bhāskarā bhāsvatī bhītirbhāsvaddhṛṣṭānusāpinī |
anudyamā kṣamānandā durlabhā bhavanātmikā || 75 ||

viśvavandyā viśvavīrā viśvaghnī viśvasaṃsthitā |
śīlasthā śīlarūpā ca śīlā śīlapradāyinī || 76 ||

bodhinī bodhakuśalā bodhanā bodhinī sadā |
vidyotinī vicitrātmā vidyutpāṭalasannibhā || 77 ||

viśvayonirmahāyoniḥ karmayoniḥ priyaṃvadā |
rohiṇī rogaśamanī mahārogāpahāriṇī || 77 ||

vasudā rudrarūpā ca raudrī raudrārttināśinī |
prajā dākṣāyaṇī dakṣā dakṣayajñavināśinī || 78 ||

yaśaśvinī yaśaḥpūrṇā yaśodāgarbhasambhavā |
devakī devamātā ca rādhikā kṛṣṇavallabhā || 79 ||

arundhatī śacīndrāṇī gāndhārī gandhamālinī |
dhyānātītā dhyānagamyā dhyānākhyā dhyānadhāriṇī || 80 ||

lambodarī ca lamboṣṭhī jāmbavatyā jalodarī |
mahodarī muktakeśī muktikāmārthasiddhidā || 81 ||

p. 862) tapasvinī rāgahanā guhyaratnanirañjanā |
aśarīrā śarīrasthā saṃsārārṇavatāriṇī || 82 ||

amṛtā niṣkalā bhadrā sakalā kṛṣṇapiṅgalā |
cakreśvarī cakrahastā paracakravidāriṇī || 83 ||

padmarāgapratīkāśā nirmalākāśasannibhā |
rasajñā rasamadhyasthā gandhasthā gandharūpiṇī || 84 ||

paraṃbrahmasvarūpā ca paraṃbrahmasvarūpiṇī |
śabdabrahmasvarūpā ca śabdākhyā śabdavarjitā || 85 ||

ṛddhivṛddhiḥ parā siddhiḥ satkīrttirdīptasaṃsthitā |
āpūjyā śāmbhavī śaktiḥ sundarāṅgī tattvarūpiṇī || 86 ||

śāśvatī bhūtamātā ca mahābhūtādhipapriyā |
śrutiprājñā dharmasiddhirdakṣakanyā'parājitā || 87 ||

kāmasandīpanī kāmā sadā kāmakutūhalā |
bhogāpacārakuśalā amarā kamalānanā || 88 ||

bhaktānukampinī maitrī śaraṇāgatavatsalā |
kṣobhaṇī mohanī caiva stambhanī jṛmbhaṇī tathā || 89 ||

rathinī dhvajinī senā sarvamantramayī trayī |
jñānamudrā mahāmudrā jaganmudrā mahotsavā || 90 ||

sahasrabhujā cicchaktiḥ sahasrākṣī sanātanī |
siddhalakṣmīrmahālakṣmī rājalakṣmīḥ sulakṣaṇā || 91 ||

yajñasārā tapaḥsārā dharmasārā janeśvarī |
viśvodarī viśvapūjyā viśvākhyā viśvatomukhī || 92 ||

p. 863) viśvāsyā śravaṇā ghrāṇā viśvaprāṇā padātmikā |
taruṇādityasaṅkāśā kiraṇānekasaṅkulā || 93 ||

jaṭājūṭadharā mukhyā śuṣkamāṃsātibhīṣaṇā |
dvīpicarmaparīdhānā cīravalkaladhāriṇī || 94 ||

triśūlaḍamarukarā naramālāvibhūṣaṇā |
atyugrarūpiṇī cogrā kalpāntadahanopamā || 95 ||

trailokyadhāriṇī sādhyā siddhiḥ sādhakavatsalā |
savavidyāmayī sārā vairivargavināśinī || 96 ||

subhagā sumukhī ramyā mukharā somabhūṣaṇā |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśā mahāvṛṣabhavāhinī || 97 ||

mahiṣī mahiṣārūḍhā mahiṣāsuramarddinī |
daminī dāminī dāntā dayā dogdhrī durāpagā || 98 ||

agnijihvā mahāghorā ghoraghoratarānanā |
nārāyaṇī nārasiṃhī nṛsiṃhahṛdayasthitā || 99 ||

yogeśvarī yogarūpā yogamātā ca yoginī |
khecarī bhūcarī khelā nirvāṇapadasaṃśrayā || 100 ||

nāginī nāgakanyā ca suvanā nāganāyikā |
viṣajvālāvatī tīkṣṇā phaṇāśatavibhūṣaṇā || 101 ||

bhīmavaktrā mahāvaktrā vaktrāṇāṃ koṭidhāriṇī |
mahāvaktrā ca dharmākhyā dharmādisukhadāyinī || 102 ||

kṛṣṇamūrddhā mahāmūrddhā ghoramūrddhā varānanā |
sarvendriyā sarvasaṃsthā sarvendriyamanomayī || 103 ||

p. 864) sarvasaṃgrāmajayadā sarvapraharaṇodyatā |
sarvapīḍopaśamanī sarvābhīṣṭapradāyinī || 104 ||

sarvaiśvaryasamutpattiḥ sarvagrahavināśinī |
bhītighnī bhaktigamyā ca bhaktāpamṛtyunāśinī || 105 ||

mātaṅgī mattamātaṅgī mataṅgakulamaṇḍitā |
gaṇḍamaṇḍitasaṅkāśā guñjāhāravibhūṣaṇā || 103 ||

saṅgītaraṅgaracanā vīṇāvādyakutūhalā |
amṛtodadhimadhyasthā prabālarasanojjvalā || 107 ||

maṇimaṇḍapamadhyasthā ratnasiṃhāsanasthitā |
paramānandamuditā īṣatprahasitānanā || 108 ||

kumudā lalitā lolā lākṣālalitalocanā |
digvastrā devadūtī ca devadevādhidevatā || 109 ||

siṃhoparisamārūḍhā mahācalanivāsinī |
aṭṭaṭṭahāsinī ghorā ghoradantavināśinī || 110 ||

atyugrā raktavarṇā ca nāgakeyūramaṇḍitā |
muktāhāralatopetatuṅgapīnapayodharā || 111 ||

raktotpaladalākāramadamūrcchitalocanā |
samastadevatāmūrttirasurakṣayakāriṇī || 112 ||

khaḍginī śūlahastā ca cakriṇī cākṣamālinī |
śaṅkhinī cāpinī vāṇī vajriṇī raktadantikā || 113 ||

ānandodadhimadhyasthā kaṭisūtrairalaṅkṛtā |
nānābharaṇadīptāṅgī nānāmaṇivibhūṣitā || 114 ||

p. 865) jagadānandasaṃbhūticintāmaṇiguṇākarā |
trailokyanamitā pūjyā cinmayānandarūpiṇī || 115 ||

trailokyavarddhinī devī duḥkhaduḥsvapnanāśinī |
ghorāgnidāhaśamanī bījadaivādimālinī || 116 ||

mahāparādharāśighnī mahācaurabhayāpahā |
rāgādirogarahitā jarāmaraṇavarjitā || 117 ||

candramaṇḍalasaṅkāśā pīyuṣārṇavasambhavā |
sarvadevaiḥ stutā devī sarvasiddhairnamaskṛtā || 118 ||

acintyaśaktirūpā ca maṇimantramahauṣadhī |
svasti svastimatī bālā malayācalavāsinī || 119 ||

dhātrī vidhātrī sambhāvā ratijñā ratidāyinī |
rudrāṇī rudrarūpā ca raudrī raudrārttināśinī || 120 ||

sarvajñā caiva dharmajñā rasajñā dīnavatsalā |
anāhatā trinayanā nirbhavā nirvṛtiḥ parā || 121 ||

parā ghorā karālākṣī sumatiḥ priyabhāṣiṇī |
mahāmbikā mantramasyā mantramātā suputriṇī || 122 ||

śuddhānandā mahābhadrā nirgatā nirguṇātmikā |
dhariṇī dhāriṇī pṛthvī dharā dhātrī vasundharā || 123 ||

merumandaramadhyasthā sthirā śaṅkaravallabhā |
śrīmatī śrīmatāṃ śreṣṭhā śrīkarī śrīvibhāvinī || 124 ||

śrīdātrī śrīvidhātrī ca śrīmatī śrīmatāṃ gatiḥ |
umāśā śārṅgiṇī kṛṣṇā kuṭilā kuṭilālakā || 125 ||

p. 866) trilocanā trilokasthā puṇyasthā puṇyakīrttidā |
amṛtā satyasaṅkalpā pretyā sā prasthitodanī || 126 ||

pareśī paramārādhā parā vidyā parāparā |
sundarāṅgī suvarṇābhā surāsuranamaskṛtā || 127 ||

rudrāṇī puṣṭidā devī mānadā mānasapriyā |
kṛṣṇāṅgavāhinī caiva kṛṣṇā kṛṣṇamahodarā || 128 ||

śāmbhavī śambhurūpā ca śambhusthā śambhusambhavā |
bimbo(śvo)darī yogamudrā yogarūpā ca yoginī || 129 ||

yogamudrā yoganidrā yoginīkoṭisevitā |
kuṭilā nandikanyā ca śṛṅgārapīṭhavāsinī || 130 ||

kṣemaṅkarī sarvarūpā divyarūpā divīśvarā |
dhūmravaktrā dhūmrakeśī dhūmravaktāvadhūsarā || 131 ||

pinākī rudravetālī mahāvetālarūpiṇī |
bhaginī bhāginī dakṣā viṣṇuvidyā anāśritā || 132 ||

mantharā jaṭharā tīvrā agnijihvā bhayāpahā |
paśughnī paśurūpā ca paśuhā paśuvāhinī || 133 ||

pitā santoṣitā dhātā paśupāsavināśinī |
candraprabhā candrarekhā candrakāntivibhūṣaṇā || 134 ||

kuṅkumāṅkitasarvāṅgī sudhābudbudalocanā |
niṣṭhurā krūrahṛdayā akrūrāmṛtabhāṣiṇī || 135 ||

ākāśaliṅgasambhūtā bhuvanadhyānavāhinī |
nirupākṣī sahasrākṣī śatākṣī bahulocanā || 136 ||

p. 867) daṇḍāvatāriṇī tārā taruṇī tārarūpiṇī |
sudhādhārā ca dharmajñā dharmaśāstropadeśinī || 137 ||

bhaveśvarī bhavārādhyā bhaginī bhaganāyikā |
bhagavidyā bhagaklinnā bhagayonirbhagapradā || 138 ||

bhageśī bhagarūpā ca bhagaguhyā bhagāvahā |
bhagodarā bhagānandā bhagādyā bhagamālinī || 139 ||

mālinī mādhavī sādhvī madhurūpā mahotkaṭā |
candrikā candrimā jyotsnā viśvacakṣustamo'pahā || 140 ||

suprasannā mahādūtī yamadūtī bhayaṅkarī |
unmādinī mahārūpā divyarūpā surārcitā || 141 ||

trailokyaphalinī nityā nityānandamadoddhatā |
madirānandakaivalyā madirākṣī madālasā || 142 ||

siddheśvarī siddhavidyā siddhādyā siddhavanditā |
siddhārcitā siddhamātā siddhiḥ sarvārthasiddhidā || 143 ||

māyāmayī guṇātītā paraṃ jyotiḥsvarūpiṇī |
pareśī paragā pārā parā siddhiḥ parā gatiḥ || 144 ||

vimalā mohinī ādyā madhupānaparāyaṇā |
vedavedāṅgajananī sarvaśāstraviśāradā || 145 ||

sarvadevamayī nityā sarvaśāstramayī tathā |
sarvayajñamayī devī sarvadharmamayīśvarī || 146 ||

sarvayajñā mahāyajñā sarvamantrādhikāriṇī |
sarvasampattyadhiṣṭhātrī sarvavidyāvalī tathā || 147 ||

p. 868) sarvasaṃkṣobhaṇī devī sarvamaṅgalakāriṇī |
trailokyākarṣiṇī devī sarvāhlādanakāriṇī || 148 ||

sarvasammohinī devī sarvastambhanakāriṇī |
trailokyajṛmbhiṇī devī tathā sarvavaśaṅkarī || 149 ||

trailokyarañjinī devī sarvānmādasvarūpiṇī |
sarvārthasādhanī devī sarvaduḥkhavimocanī || 150 ||

sarvamṛtyupraśamanī sarvaduḥkhavināśinī |
sarvāṅgasundarī mātā sarvasaubhāgyadāyinī || 151 ||

sarvajñā sarvaśaktiśca sarvaiśvaryaphalapradā |
sarvajñānamayī devī sarvavyādhivināśinī || 152 ||

sarvādhārasvarūpā ca sarvapāpavināśinī |
sarvānandamayī devī sarvarakṣāsvarūpiṇī || 153 ||

sarvalakṣmīmayī nityā sarvepsitaphalapradā |
sarvaduṣṭapraśamanī paramānandadāyinī || 154 ||

trailokyanilayā tristhā trimātā tritalasthitā |
trividyā ca trayī māyā trailokyā tripurākṣarā || 156 ||

triyādavasthā trividhā tripurā tripurāsthitā |
tridhāmnī tridaśādhyakṣā trighnī tripuravāsinī || 156 ||

trivargā trigudī stristhā trimūrttistripureśvarī |
trividhā tridiveśānī tribhistripurasundarī || 157 ||

tripurā śrīstrijananī tribhuk tripurabhairavī | oṃ || 158 ||

p. 869) iti śrīpurasundaryā mantraṃ nāmasahasrakam |
guhyād guhyataraṃ putra tava prītyā prakīrttitam || 159 ||

gopanīyaṃ prayatnena paṭhanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
nātaḥ parataraṃ stotraṃ nātaḥ parataraṃ japaḥ || 160 ||

nātaḥ parataraṃ puṇyaṃ nātaḥ paratarā gatiḥ |
stotraṃ nāmasahasrākhyaṃ mama vaktravinirgatam || 161 ||

yaḥ paṭhet parayā bhaktyā śṛṇuyād vā samāhitaḥ |
mokṣārthī labhate mokṣaṃ svargārthī svargamāpnuyāt || 162 ||

kāmānavāpnuyāt kāmī dhanārthī labhate dhanam |
vidyārthī labhate vidyāṃ yaśo'rthī labhate yaśaḥ || 163 ||

kanyārthī labhate kanyāṃ sutārthī labhate sutam |
gurviṇī janayet putraṃ kanyā vindati satpatim || 164 ||

mūrkho'pi labhate śāstraṃ cauro'bhilabhate gatim || 165 ||

saṃkrāntyāñca caturddaśyāmaṣṭamyāñca viśeṣataḥ |
paurṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāṃ navamyāṃ bhaumabhārgave || 166 ||

paṭhed vā pāṭhayed vāpi śṛṇuyād vā samāhitaḥ |
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena paṭhanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 167 ||

sa muktaḥ sarvapāpebhyaḥ kāmeśvarasamo bhavet |
lakṣmīvān putravāñ śrīmān vallabhaḥ sarvayoṣitām || 168 ||

tasya vaśyaṃ bhavedāśu trailokyaṃ sacarācaram |
rudraṃ dṛṣṭvā yathā devā viṣṇuṃ dṛṣṭvā yathāmarāḥ || 169 ||

pannagā garuḍaṃ dṛṣṭvā siṃhaṃ dṛṣṭvā yathā gajāḥ |
maṇḍūkā bhoginaṃ dṛṣṭā mārjjāraṃ mūṣikā yathā || 170 ||

p. 870) śatravastu pālayante tasya vaktrāvalokanāt |
agnicaurabhayaṃ tasya darśanānnaiva sambhavet || 171 ||

pātakā bahavaḥ santi merumandarasannibhāḥ |
bhasmasāt kurute śrīghraṃ tṛṇaṃ vahnihutaṃ yathā || 172 ||

ekadhā paṭhanādeva sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet |
naśyanti bahavo rogā daśadhāvarttanena ca || 173 ||

śatadhāvarttanādeva vācāṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt |
sahasraṃ paṭhate yastu khecaro jāyate naraḥ || 174 ||

sahasradaśakaṃ yastu paṭhed vai bhaktimān naraḥ |
sā tasya jagatāṃ dhātrī pratyakṣā bhavati dhruvam || 175 ||

lakṣavārantu saṃpūrṇaṃ stotrarājaṃ paṭhet sudhīḥ |
bhavapāśād vinirmukto mama tulyo na saṃśayaḥ || 176 ||

sarvatīrtheṣu yat puṇyaṃ sarvayajñeṣu yat phalam |
sarvavedeṣu yaddharmaḥ sarvadāneṣu yat phalam || 177 ||

sarvaśāstreṣu prokteṣu yat phalaṃ parikīrttitam |
tat phalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ sakṛt pāṭhāllabhennaraḥ || 178 ||

śrutvā mahātibalavān putravān dhanasampadaḥ || 179 ||

dehānte paramaṃ sthānaṃ yat surairapi durlabham |
sa yāsyati na sandehaḥ stavarājasya kīrttanāt || 180 ||

iti śrīpūrṇānandaparamahaṃsaviracite śrī-tattvacintāmaṇau pañcaviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ || 25 ||



ṣaḍviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ

[mahāyantrasaṃskārakathanam]

athocyate mahāyantrasaṃskāraḥ sarvabhūtidaḥ || 1 ||

śubhe'hni snānamācarya śubhe lagne śubhe kṣaṇe |
kṛtanityakriyo mantrī prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ || 2 ||

bhūtaśuddhyādikaṃ kṛtvā kalpoktanyāsamācaret |
tataḥ saṅkalpya śuddhātmā śivamantraviśodhitam || 3 ||

pañcagavyaṃ samānīya tatra yantraṃ samāhitaḥ |
suvarṇādimaṇau kḷptaṃ kṣipet praṇavamuccaran || 4 ||

taduddhṛtya suvarṇādau pātraṃ saṃsthāpya mantravit |
pañcāmṛtena tīrthena jalena śītalena ca || 5 ||

sahasraśīrṣamantreṇa mūlamantrapuraḥsaram |
susnāpya ca viśuddhena jalamuttolya taijase || 6 ||

siṃhāsanādāvāropya kuṅkumādyairvilepayet |
pīṭhapūjāṃ tataḥ kuryāt svakalpoktavidhānataḥ || 7 ||

kuśaiḥ spṛśan mantrarājaṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet || 8 ||

[yantra-gāyattrī]

praṇavaṃ mantrarājāya vidmahe padamuddharet |
mahāmantrāya dhīrūpaṃ likhettato mahīti ca || 9 ||

p. 872) tanno yantraḥ pracorūpaṃ dayādeṣā prakīrttitā |
imāṃ yantrasya gāyattrīṃ japtvā devatvamālabhet || 10 ||

[iṣṭadevatāpūjanopadeśaḥ]

yatheṣṭadevatāṃ dhyātvā mudrayāvāhayet kramāt |
jīvanyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pūjayediṣṭadevatām || 11 ||

upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirnānāvibhavavistaraiḥ |
nṛtyagītaiśca vādyaiśca pañcaghoṣapuraḥsaram || 12 ||

tato japediṣṭamantramaṣṭottarasahasrakam |
tataḥ kalpoktamāsādyahaviṣā saṃskṛte'nale || 13 ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā guruṃ vibhavapūrvakam |
toṣayet svarṇaratnādyaiḥ pradadyāddakṣiṇāmapi || 14 ||

[upacāramudropadeśaḥ]

ṣoḍaśopacāramudrāyā ucyante lakṣaṇānyatha |
prasārayedubhau hastau hṛddeśe viniyojayet || 15 ||

aṅgulyo vistṛtāḥ kāryā madhye'ṅguṣṭhau tu vinyaset |
devatāsanavinyāsapañcamudrāṃ pradarśayet || 16 ||

īṣannamrāṅgulirjñeyā saṃvṛtāṅguṣṭhakandaram |
namre kaniṣṭhatarjanyau karayoragrasaṅgate || 17 ||

madhyamānāmikā kuryādiyamāsanamudrikā |
iṣannamrāṅgulirdakṣā saṃveṣṭyāṅguṣṭhakaṃ param || 18 ||

svāgate svastikā mudrā madhyamūlagatāṅgulī |
upacāre svāgate tu prayoktavyā vipaścitā || 19 ||

p. 873) tau ca prasāritau hastau pādyamudrā prakīrttitā |
saṃyuktānāmikāṅguṣṭhau tisro'nyāḥ saṃprasāritāḥ || 20 ||

madhuparkasya sā mudrā gaditā tantravedibhiḥ |
muṣṭiyuktaṃ tathā netramaṅguṣṭhāgramadhomukham || 21 ||

madhyamāṅguṣṭhakau lagnau tisro'nyāḥ suprasāritāḥ |
vastramudrā samākhyātā vastramudrāpravarddhinī || 22 ||

kaniṣṭhāṅgulikau lagnau trisro madhyāḥ prasāritāḥ |
yajñopavītamudreyaṃ kathitā mantrapāragaiḥ || 23 ||

madhuparkaṃ samuttānā mudrā lagnāṅgulī matā |
yuktā'nāmikayā'ṅguṣṭhā gandhamudreti kīrttitā || 24 ||

utthitā'dhomukhī madhyā baddhāṅguṣṭhā yadītarā |
puṣpamudrā samākhyātā puṣpadānavivarddhinī || 25 ||

aṅguṣṭhaṃ tarjjanīlagnaṃ tisraḥ saṅkucitāḥ parāḥ |
mudrā dhūpapradāne syāddevānāṃ tuṣṭikāriṇī || 26 ||

uttānā pauṣpikī mudrā dīpamudreti kathyate |
pañcāṅgulyagrasaṃlagnaprotthitā'dhomukhī yadi || 27 ||

tridhā nibaddhā mudreyaṃ naivedyasya prakīrttitā |
dvau karau pṛṣṭhasaṃlagnau bhrāmayed grathitāṅgulī |
sphoṭiketi samākhyātā praṇāme tāṃ pradarśayet || 28 ||

p. 874) [gandhādimudrāṇāṃ prakārabhedaḥ]

athavā darśayenmudrā imā gandhādibhiḥ kramāt |
kaniṣṭhā'ṅguṣṭhasaṃsaktā gandhamudrā prakīrtitā || 29 ||

aṅguṣṭhasya ca pṛṣṭhasya yuktā dhūpasya tarjjanī |
dīpasya madhyamānāmā naivedyasya prakīrttitā || 30 ||

itarāṅgulisaṃyogāt pañca mudrāḥ prakīrttitāḥ || 31 ||

gandhādiṣvapi datteṣu mudrāścaitāḥ pradarśayet |

[mudrāmāhātmyam]

mudrayā yat kṛtaṃ karma tadanantaphalaṃ bhavet || 32 ||

[devatāyāḥ pratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ]

atha vakṣye devatāyāḥ pratiṣṭhānavidhikramam |
puṇyakṣetre puṇyakāle śalyādirahite sthale || 33 ||

mantrakalpāvasāne ca pūrvedyuradhivāsayet |
sāyaṃ saṅkalpya vidhivad ghaṭasthāpanapūrvakam || 34 ||

gaṇeśādīn samabhyarcya dikpālān paripūjayet |
yathāśaktyupacārādyaiḥ pūjayenmūladaivatam || 35 ||

sugandhitailagandhādyairdivyāntamadhivāsayet |
candreṇetyādimantreṇa jayaghoṣapuraḥsaram || 36 ||

ityadhivāsanaṃ kṛtvā saṃyato vijitendriyaḥ |
prātarnirvarttya nityantu kḷptayāgasthale viśet || 37 ||

patākādhvajasaṃkīrṇe divyamaṇḍapamaṇḍite |
vitānacāmaracchatrapuṣpamālāvibhūṣite || 38 ||

p. 775) maṅgalāṅkurapātrādyaiḥ sakalaiḥ paripūrite |
maṇḍalaṃ sarvatobhadraṃ vedyā upari saṃlikhet || 39 ||

prāṅmukho vāgyato bhūtvā saṅkalpyābhīṣṭasiddhaye ||
pañcaśuddhiṃ samācarya kumbhamakṣatasaṃyutam || 40 ||

saṃsthāpya maṇḍale tatra yajetādhāraśaktikām |
pīṭhamanvantimāstatra sthāpayet sakalaṃ budhaḥ || 41 ||

dīkṣā kramoktavidhinā'bhiṣekakalasaṃ tathā |
pañcagavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śivamantraviśodhitam || 42 ||

tena susnāpayenmantrī pratimāṃ praṇavaṃ japan |
pañcāmṛtena haviṣā dadhnā ca payasā kramāt || 43 ||

tata udvarttanaṃ kṛtvā jalena snāpayedapi |
vāsasā jalamuttolya dukūle paridhāpayet || 44 ||

nayet sthāpitakumbhasya samīpe devatāṃ budhaḥ |
tato dhyātvā yathoktāṃ tāṃ mudrayāvāhayet kramāt || 45 ||

lelihāmudrayā hastaṃ vinyasya devatāhṛdi |
prāṇapratiṣṭhāmantrantu daśadhā prajapettataḥ |
tattaddaivatagāyattrīṃ śatamaṣṭottarāmapi || 46 ||

tataḥ kalpoktavidhinā parivārasamanvitām |
pūjayeddevatāṃ paścāt svavāme sthānamaṇḍape || 47 ||

aṣṭapatrasamāyukte pīṭhopari nidhāya tām |
sugandhidivyatailena dadyādabhyaṅgameva hi || 48 ||

p. 876) mūlena pāvamānena sahasraśīrṣayā budhaḥ |
snāpayed bhaktibhāvena abhiṣekaghaṭāmṛtaiḥ || 49 ||

karpūrāgurukastūrī prasūnacandanodakaiḥ |
nānātīrthodakenāpi svarṇaratnānvitairjalaiḥ |
sarvauṣadhijalairgandhajalairmūlena secayet || 50 ||

tataḥ śuddhadukūlena jalamuttolya sādhakaḥ || 51 ||

paridhāpya dukūle dve svarṇanirmitadolayā |
maṇḍale tāṃ samānīya nṛtyairgītairjayasvanaiḥ || 52 ||

ṣoḍaśairupacāraistu pūjayet kalpamānataḥ |

[homopadeśaḥ]

kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍile mantrī haviṣā saṃskṛte'nale || 53 ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā śrapayitvā caruṃ tataḥ |
yathāvidhividhānena srucā homaṃ samācaret || 54 ||

grāsapramāṇaṃ carukaṃ mūle nāhutimantravit |
ghṛtasruvaṃ tatra dattvā juhuyādagnisaṃmukhe || 55 ||

pañcaviṃśativāraṃ hi devatoddiśya yatnataḥ |
caruṇā dikpatibhyaśca dadyāt pūrvādito balim || 56 ||

tata āvṛtidevebhyo ghṛtenāhutimācaret |
athavā caruṇā tadvattato devasamīpage || 57 ||

gatvā puṣpāñjalīn pañca praddyācchatracāmare |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ mantraṃ japtvā mūlaṃ samāhitaḥ || 58 ||

abhiṣiñcettato devaṃ pūjākumbhasthapallavaiḥ |
mātṛkā mūlamantrañca japan jayajayasvanaiḥ || 59 ||

p. 877) gatvā homasamīpantu dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ |
dakṣiṇāṃ gurave dadyāt svarṇaṃ karṣapramāṇataḥ || 60 ||

arddhārddhāṃśaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ yathāvibhavapūvakam |
tataḥ santoṣayed vidvān gṛhītvā āśiṣo dvijān || 61 ||

iti devapratiṣṭhānamuktaṃ sarvasamṛddhidam |

[pratiṣṭhāphalakīrttanam]

yaḥ karoti pratiṣṭhānaṃ daivatasya mahātmanaḥ || 62 ||

vaseddvitrisahasrāṇi svarge devapramāṇataḥ |
tato lakṣasahasrāṇi brahmaloke mahīyate || 63 ||

athavā koṭikalpāntaṃ mohayedacyute pade || 64 ||

[maṭhotsargavidhiḥ]

atha vakṣye gṛhotsargaṃ vāstuyāgapuraḥsaram |
śaratkāle mahāpuṇye puṇyakṣetre śubhe'hani || 65 ||

durgāyai mandiraṃ dattvā sva-sva-mantrānusārataḥ |
pūrvoktaṃ sarvamācarya vṛddhiśrāddhapuraḥsaram || 66 ||

tataḥ saṅkalpya vidhivanmandire supariṣkṛte |
patākādhvajasaṅkīrṇe tuṅgatoraṇaśobhite || 67 ||

chatracāmaramālādyairvitānairupaśobhite |
vilikhya sarvatobhadraṃ sthāpayed kalasantathā || 68 ||

pūjayediṣṭadevantu ṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ |
aṅgadevān samabhyarcya tattaddevagaṇān yajet || 69 ||

p. 878) vakṣyamāṇena manunā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hunet |
daśākṣareṇa durgāyai dadyānmandiramuttamam || 70 ||

bhuvaneśīmahābījaṃ durgeyugamudāhṛtam |
rakṣiṇitryakṣaraṃ procya vahnijāyāvadhirmanuḥ || 71 ||

[maṭhadānaphalam]

mantreṇānena yo dadyānmandiraṃ supratiṣṭhitam |
sarvaiśvaryamavāpnoti vaset kalpāyutaṃ divi || 72 ||

[sādhanopadeśaḥ]

iha pīṭhavare divye sādhayet siddhimuttamām |
saptamyāṃ saṃyato bhūtvā mahāṣṭamyāṃ jayecchivām || 73 ||

mahāsnānaṃ mahāpūjāṃ dadyāt sandhau baliṃ tataḥ |
pradīpaṃ mastake dattvā tadraktaiḥ pūjayecchivām || 74 ||

kṛtvāsanaṃ chāgadehaṃ prajapenmanasā dhiyā |
tāñca dhyāyan vibhītaścet sādhako vijitendriyaḥ |
iha loke bhavedrājā paraloke śivopamaḥ || 75 ||

[sādhane phalaviśeṣopadeśaḥ]

yaḥ sadā bhāvayeddevīṃ svātmānaṃ tanmayaṃ naraḥ |
tasya vaśyaṃ jagat sarvaṃ yakṣarākṣasamānuṣam || 76 ||

bhāvena tanmayo bhūtvā yaṃ yaṃ paśyati cakṣuṣā |
sa sa dāso bhavet sārddhaṃ sā sā yāti kulāṅganā || 77 ||


	71 | bhuvaneśīti | etena hrīṃ durge durge rakṣiṇi svāhā iti mantro bhavati |

p. 879) bhāvena tanmayo bhūtvā yaṃ paśyati ruṣānvitaḥ |
hataśrīrjāyate sadyaḥ sahasā duḥkhito bhavet || 78 ||

bhāvena tanmayo bhūtvā prasanno yaṃ prapaśyati |
sa muktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo dāsavajjāyate dhruvam || 79 ||

bhāvena tanmayo bhūtvā yaṃ yaṃ bhāvayate mṛtam |
āśu naśyati niśrīkaḥ sabhṛtyabalavāhanaḥ || 80 ||

bhāvena tanmayo bhūtvā yaṃ yaṃ vidveṣṭi sādhakaḥ |
sarvaloke sa vidviṣṭo jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 81 ||

[homapraśaṃsā]

ekataḥ sarvakarmāṇi ekato japabhāvanā |
sarvādhikaṃ homakarma homāt kiṃ vā na sidhyati || 82 ||

[homākaraṇanindā]

anayā dīkṣito mantrī yadi homavivarjjitaḥ |
duḥkhaṃ prāpnoti manasā kāyena ca na saṃśayaḥ || 83 ||

akurvatāmagnihotramagnirbhūtvā maheśvarī |
dhanāni śīghraṃ grasati gṛhakṣetrapaśūnapi || 84 ||

bahuduḥkhenārjjitāni sañcitāni cireṇa yat |
ātmaprāṇādhikānyāśu bhūtvāgnirgrasatīśvarī || 85 ||

[agnihotrapraśaṃsā]

agnihotraṃ vinā naiva sampadāṃ bhājanaṃ bhavet |
agnihotraparasyeha cāgnirbhavati śītalaḥ || 86 ||

gṛhakṣetrādivītteṣu prayukto'pi dahenna tat |
agnau vasati deveśī tasmādagnau hunet sadā || 87 ||

p. 880) gurumabhyarccayennityaṃ dhanaistribhiḥ suśobhanaiḥ || 88 ||

militvā sādhakaiḥ sarvairnityaṃ samayamāviśet |
adīkṣitān dvijāṃścāpi tyaktvā karma samācaret || 89 ||

[cakrapraśaṃsā]

cakre pravṛtte tatrasthāḥ sarve varṇā dvijātayaḥ |
nivṛtte bhairavīcakre sarve varṇāḥ pṛthak pṛthak || 90 ||

devīputreṣu tiṣṭhatsu naikaḥ samayamāviśet |
aśubhaṃ jāyate tasya siddhihāniśca jāyate || 91 ||

[vittaśāṭhyaniṣedhaḥ]

vittaśāṭhyaṃ parityajya sarvakarmāṇi sādhayet |
vittaśāṭhyaṃ nihantyāśu putramāyuryaśo dhanam || 92 ||

[gurudevavañcananiṣedhaḥ]

guruṃ devaṃ vañcayitvā yaḥ kuryāddhanasañcayam |
rājabhirnīyate caiva taskarairnīyate'thavā || 93 ||

adhitiṣṭhanti yakṣāśca vahnirdahati vā cirāt |
tyaktvā nikhātaṃ mriyate satyaṃ tena na bhujyate || 94 ||

[vahnau homasya gurave dānasya ca praśaṃsā]

hutamekaguṇaṃ vahnau dattamekaguṇaṃ gurau |
labhyate koṭiguṇitaṃ viśvasenna parāmṛśet || 95 ||

yasya nāsti kva viśvāsaḥ kurute dhanasañcayam |
rājabhirnīyate caiva taskarairnīyate'thavā || 96 ||

iti śrīpūrṇānandaparamahaṃsaviracite śrī-tattvacintāmaṇau ṣaḍviṃśatitamaḥ prakāśaḥ || 26 ||

śrītattvacintāmaṇiḥ

samāptaḥ ||
